# Changelog

What changed in each version of Vanilla Accounting. The format follows
[Keep a Changelog](https://keepachangelog.com/en/1.1.0/), and the version numbers follow
[Semantic Versioning](https://semver.org/) as [`docs/releasing.md`](docs/releasing.md) applies
it to this project. `npm run update` prints the sections between your old version and the new one.

Two markers flag an entry you must read before you update:

- **⚠ figures**: the change alters a computed figure (a VAT Ziffer, a balance, a return) of an
  open period. A filed quarter's figures never change. After the update, `npm run test:books`
  shows the difference; check it, then accept it with `npm run test:books:update`.
- **⚠ action**: you must do something by hand, as the entry says.

## [Unreleased]

### Added

- A receipt whose VAT read keeps failing (#450), books schema 9: the VAT step counts on the
  receipt each reply of the AI that came and carried no VAT facts (not JSON, or without whether
  Swiss VAT was charged and its rates). A call that brought no reply counts nothing: the AI off or
  signed out, its plan's limit, no program, a timeout, a network error, a rate limit; the first
  refusal ends the run's VAT reads, and its note says the AI was not reachable. After the third
  the receipt is «Beleg nicht lesbar»: the step stops trying it, its payment reaches the task with
  its ways out (neu fotografieren, ohne Vorsteuer buchen, die MWST unter Angaben eintragen), the
  run note says «nach 3 Versuchen nicht lesbar» with the files, and the receipt shows «MWST nach 3
  Versuchen nicht lesbar». A read that succeeds, a new text or her typed VAT starts the count
  afresh; a receipt of a filed period is never counted. The read in «Buchen» now writes as the
  inbox, not as the person. Books of schema 8 load unchanged (no receipt has a count);
  `npm run update` writes 9, and an app of schema 8 refuses books of 9.
- Card statement lines (#409), **⚠ figures**, books schema 8: the app reads a credit card
  statement's lines by code (day, merchant, amount, the merchant's own currency and amount when the
  line names one) and keeps them on its receipt, only when they add up to the total the statement
  prints. The bill behind one line (a hotel, a foreign service) is linked to the card's settlement
  as the bill of that line, by code when exactly one bill fits it (amount to the cent in the line's
  or the merchant's currency, within 5 days, a word of the merchant's name), else offered: «Passt zu
  Zeile 3 der Kartenabrechnung (…)» in the drop row and on the receipt, «Mit dieser Zeile
  verknüpfen» one click; «Lösen» takes it apart and code never links it back. A settlement with such
  a bill is booked by its lines: a line with its bill takes that bill's VAT (a rate only when the
  bill shows Swiss VAT, Bezugsteuer for a foreign service as the guide says), the other lines the
  zero code as before; the hand form proposes no rate for the whole settlement from a bill of one
  line; a bill smaller than its line (a tip) takes its rate on the bill's gross only, the rest of the
  line the zero code. A credit line keeps its minus; a balance carried and the card's own payment
  («Saldo Vormonat», «Ihre Zahlung») are no lines. Days as «07/08/2026» and amounts as «1.234,50» are
  read; a statement whose lines cannot be read says so («Zeilen nicht gelesen: …») and its
  settlement is booked whole. When the statement leaves the payment («Lösen», deleted, set aside),
  its lines' bills go with it («Rückgängig» puts them back), and a bill of a line is never evidence
  of the whole settlement. Without an AI, linking a bill by code leaves the settlement's booking as
  it is. Why the figures move: a hotel's input tax at 3.8 % that was lost while its bill stayed
  «Beleg ohne Zahlung» is now claimed in the open quarter (Ziffer 405); books without card
  statements do not change. Never in a filed period: a filed card statement gains no lines and no
  bill is linked to a filed settlement. The books' schema goes from 7 to 8 (`Receipt.cardLines`,
  `Mapping.line`); the step adds nothing to old books, so no seal changes, and `npm run update`
  writes the new number. A card statement of an open period gains its lines on the next run of the
  inbox (every drop starts one) or when a person links it to its settlement. An app of schema 7
  refuses books of 8.
- Pairing without an AI (#397): a supplier's invoice whose payment names its number is paired by
  code when the amounts differ by rounding to 5 Rappen or by exactly the cash discount the payment
  states or the invoice offers («abzgl. 2% Skonto»); an invoice of the same business with the exact
  amount, paid within the terms it states (45 days when it states none, 90 at most), is paired too,
  unless the payment names another month, the payee was paid that amount more than once, or the
  invoice is older than the books' first payment. The business is told by the invoice's issuer
  (its letterhead), never by the firm's or the owner's name. Only when nothing else could be either
  of them. What may fit but is not sure (a rounded amount without a number, an
  undated till slip, two candidates) is offered with what differs («gleiche Firma · Betrag … statt
  … · 5 Tage früher») on the receipt, in «Beleg suchen» and in the drop row, one click to link.
- Hand form (#397): a payment less a cash discount says so and is booked as paid, its VAT on what was
  paid (a receipt of several rates leaves the codes to her and says why; a firm without input tax
  hears no VAT sentence); no account is guessed from a receipt's words, which only order the account
  list by whole words, and the form says so; a payment without a proposal says why in one line; the zero code of a payment
  without a receipt says «ohne Vorsteuer: kein Beleg verknüpft» instead of «wie beim letzten Mal», and
  a receipt that may fit is named with the rate linking it would bring.
- «Was muss ich noch machen?» (#398): the open period's card names every item one by one, each a
  link: first the receipts that wait for her own word (no date, no amount, not read, a possible
  copy: «Baumarkt · its amount · Datum fehlt»), then each payment without a booking with its day,
  name and amount, each receipt without a payment; five per line and «und n weitere» with the way
  to all. The AI adds at most one sentence and no counts; a period is said «Q3 2026», never
  «2026-Q3».
- Setup: the AI is told the buttons of the question on screen word for word, from the list the
  buttons are drawn from, and never names an answer that is not there («weiss nöd»); on the two VAT
  questions it explains how to tell and recommends none. «Wir rechnen keine MWST ab» has its line
  under both questions, whole under the method and short under «Abrechnungsart» (#398).
- Dock: one unit per word (#398). The bar shows «n von m Zahlungen gebucht» and the tasks in every
  state; its card says «Belege: n gelesen · m verbucht» and «Zahlungen: n von m gebucht».
- Setup shorter (#398): the assumed answers stand once, on the firm's card with «stimmt»; the
  summary says «9 Antworten angenommen · ansehen». Account numbers left the form's help texts
  («Konto 4000», «1093», «1100», «2850») and stand behind «mehr». Form 735 → 724 words, the
  conversation in the books 502 → 389 (tests/setup-help-browser.test.ts).
- Small (#398): an order confirmation shows no amount and offers «Als eigenen Beleg führen» once
  on its page; the bank's fee row reads «die Gebühr der Bank laut Kontoauszug, ohne MWST» in its
  «Erfasst» line; the drop's closing names both places with counts («2 Belege warten auf dich unter
  Belege · 14 Aufgaben unter Automatisierung › Aufgaben»); an unregistered firm's invoice has no
  «MWST 0 %» in its head and its firm card says what follows; an amount in a receipt's text reads
  with the thousands mark in the open list.

- AI step: before the browser opens for the sign-in, the app says what a person without an account
  will see: the page in English, where she creates the account; the subscription the card named is
  taken out with the vendor (its plans page linked beside the line); then she comes back and the app
  notices the sign-in by itself. After «… installieren» the vendor's
  command stays folded under «Befehl zeigen» (#385).
- Layout: a list or detail scrolled up ends below the floating top bar; nothing of it stands under
  the bar any more (#385). Quick Look of a receipt or a statement covers the whole window, above the
  dock and the bar.

- AI sidebar: «was muss ich noch machen?» is answered from the books. Before the first message the
  sidebar shows where the oldest period not filed stands (payments without a booking and how many
  wait for their receipt, open questions, receipts without a payment, tasks, the day the return is
  due), each line a link; the card needs no AI. The model gets the same state with every message
  and shows the card under its answer (#342). Its counts and words are those MWST shows beside a
  figure that is not final, and the due day and the questions lead to the return's list «Noch
  offen». When nothing of the period is open but payments of later days are not booked or tasks
  wait under Aufgaben (the turnover threshold of a firm not registered among them), the card and
  the answer say that instead of «nichts offen». A period that ended before the books begin reads
  «vor Beginn dieser Bücher», never «fällig seit».
- Keeping a rule books the open payments it decides at once (#383): the keep starts the booking
  run for each, as «Braucht keinen Beleg» does, closes their tasks, and says «6 Zahlungen
  gebucht» with a link to the bookings and «Rückgängig». Runs still going after half a minute
  are named («3 werden noch gebucht»), the line updates when they finish and their tasks close
  then; past 200 payments the rest are named and follow on the next run. «Rückgängig» puts the
  rules file back (none, after the first rule) and takes out the keep's bookings that are still
  the rule's; one changed since, or of a period closed since, stays and is named; the payments are
  as before the keep, their tasks back.
  Before, they stayed tasks and unbooked until some later inbox run. A payment that waits for its
  receipt is named so on the card («wartet auf den Beleg: die Regel bucht sie, sobald er da ist»),
  never as a booking the keep would make, and the card says how many the keep books and how many
  wait. A person's bookings, payments booked in part and payments of a filed period are untouched.
- Aufgaben offers the rule for the open wages of a firm with staff in one click («6
  Lohnzahlungen von Hand buchen · Regel für alle 6», #383): code drafts it (the words the wages
  share, money out, the wage account of the chart, no VAT code) and shows its card; nothing is
  kept before «Trotzdem behalten». The Swiss module names the wage account (`wages` in
  modules/ch/setup.yaml).

### Changed

- The setup asks only what the books need from the start (#452). No UID: the form, the setup
  conversation and `npm run init` (only `--uid`) create the books without it; Firma › Firma shows
  it as not set, with what needs it, and «Hinzufügen». An invoice with VAT asks for it, and the VAT
  return's XML waits for it: the review task withholds «Freigegeben» with a link to Firma › Firma,
  the XML step stops with words, `npm run vat-xml` refuses and says where it goes. An invoice of a
  firm not registered for VAT needs none. Not asked any more: fiscal year (calendar), starting point
  (new), customers abroad (no), the owner paying privately (yes), private use (none) and payouts
  (no; the drop row asks for the clearing account with the provider's first report); each takes the
  kind of firm's answer, else that one, books nothing differently and is shown as assumed in the
  books. The form drops «Was die Firma tut» and «Wohin die Bücher Ende Jahr gehen» (the first
  stays under Firma › Firma, the second is `--export-to` of `npm run init`; nothing reads it yet) and says how many of its questions are answered.
  The name field is just «Firmenname» (also in `npm run init`), no longer «wie im Handelsregister».
  Without a UID in Firma › Firma, the firm's own invoice is still its own when its name is the name
  of the first line (not the start of a longer one) and the one UID it shows stands in the
  letterhead below it (a VAT invoice prints it): its customer's payment is proposed with it, never
  linked by code, and a supplier's invoice addressed to the firm stays a supplier's. Once such an
  invoice is linked to money in, the receipt asks «Ist das eure eigene UID (nicht die eines
  Lieferanten)?» with «Übernehmen», which keeps it under Firma › Firma with the usual check; the app
  never keeps it on its own. An invoice of a firm without a UID prints no empty UID in its footer, and
  the printed 0535 leaves the UID row out. The fiscal year and the customers abroad, which nothing
  in the app changes, are not marked as assumed.
  Manifest schema 4: `company.uid` may be absent. A manifest of schema 3 loads unchanged (every one
  has its UID); `npm run update` writes 4, and an app of schema 3 refuses a manifest of 4 with
  «Update the app» instead of failing on the missing UID.
- «Buchen» waiting for «Einschalten» holds every booking by the firm's rules or the AI (#413): a
  rule or business file changed outside the app's own controls (by hand, by git, an AI's draft not
  kept) no longer acts on the next drop or mail. The inbox still reads, matches and links, but books
  nothing; a provider's report waits in its row, and is kept for «Einschalten» when the window
  closes; «Automatisch buchen» for all, «Neu buchen», `npm run classify` and a rule kept meanwhile
  book nothing either. A customer's or an authority's file and evidence.guide.yaml count as the
  firm's rules too (files there at the update make nothing wait; one written after it waits).
  `npm run classify` books nothing on a workspace the app has not opened on this computer yet; one
  `npm run init` or the desktop's setup created counts as switched on as it was made. Wherever she tries, one
  sentence says what changed, why the app waits and what ends it, with «Einschalten» right there;
  switched on, the app books what only waited for it (the payments by an inbox run, the report taken
  again). Her own answers and links, and a
  merchant name the app learns from confirmed links, make nothing wait: a new firm starts switched
  on and its first drop books as before.
- **⚠ figures** A supplier's refund without a credit note takes the code of the purchase it refunds
  with a minus (#357, owner's decision 2026-11-13; MWSTG Art. 41 Abs. 2): -I81 for an I81 purchase,
  -I0 for one without input tax, a part at the purchase's rate. Before, a company rule booked and
  the hand form proposed -I0, so the input tax claimed on the purchase stayed claimed; the AI's
  booking already did so for the full amount and now for a part too. The purchase must be found
  unambiguously: the same supplier and currency, no later, at least the refund's amount less what
  came back of it already (the refund's text naming its invoice picks it among several); not when
  other purchases add up to the refund or the purchase is split over two codes or a line without a
  code. The booking names the purchase. A
  purchase in a filed quarter stays as filed: the refund counts in the open quarter. When no
  purchase is found, the refund keeps -I0 and its reason, and the hand form, say so. A refund
  booked from now on (or booked again) lowers Ziffer 405 (or 400) of its open quarter; a booking
  that stands is not changed. `npm run test:books` shows the difference. The
  demos have no such refund: their figures do not move. Help: «Rückerstattung eines Einkaufs».
- **⚠ figures** A webshop order of two VAT rates (tea at 2.6 %, a teapot at 8.1 %) is booked from
  the order export by its line items, a line per rate at its sale code (V26, V81), under the order's
  usual ref and linked to its report (#416). Code books it only when the items add up to what was
  charged, shipping and discount are zero, nothing is refunded and exactly one way of putting the
  items at the rates matches the tax the export shows per rate («Tax 1 Name», «Tax 1 Value»), within the rounding
  a provider may do (half a Rappen per piece at that rate, the line's quantity counted, a Rappen at least). Every other such order waits for «Aufteilen» as before. Why the figures change: until now
  each such order waited, and its turnover was missing from the open period's return. An order the
  report holds for its rates in an open period is booked when that order export is dropped again or
  a payments export of the provider is dropped; one of a filed period is never booked, and an order split by hand stays as she booked it.
  The drop row names the orders split so («nach ihren Positionen auf die MWST-Sätze aufgeteilt»), and
  each entry's reason says it. «Rückgängig» of the report takes these lines back with its other
  sales. A later refund of such an order waits for her to book it by hand. An order with more
  line items than code tries (twelve at two rates, seven at three) waits with its own reason that names
  its rates and that limit. A held reason the app has no words for reads as «wartet auf dich».
  A split booked on the order's day moves onto the day a payments export later says it was charged.
- **⚠ figures** Agreed basis (#360): a payment booked by hand dates each line with a VAT code by its
  receipt and books it over Kreditoren or Debitoren, the payment settling that account on its own
  day (an exchange difference to 6960), as the app's own booking does; a receipt of a filed quarter
  leaves the line on the payment's day, declared in that quarter's Korrekturabrechnung, and one
  from before the year the basis began leaves it as it is. Before, a hand line always took the
  payment's day. The same holds for a sale answered on the hand form, a booking typed without its
  payment and then tied to it, and a batch booking (whose preview lists the lines it books). The
  hand form now proposes the account across periods too and says where the line goes, by the
  proposed code (a credit note or refund by its latest document); a payment naming the firm's own
  invoice of another period is booked from it, not only linked. The cash basis is unchanged;
  payments booked already stay as they are.
- Demos (#374): the bundled demos are written again by today's generator, so their payments show
  the other party's name and their bank fee rows are marked as fees, as in books read from a
  statement now; no booking and no figure changed. A test now fails when a committed demo is not
  what `npm run demo:generate` writes.
- Setup (#423): «MWST-Methode» and «Abrechnungsart» say where the owner finds her answer herself:
  in the ESTV portal, under «Neue Geschäfte» her firm, then «Anpassen» on the card «Abrechnungsmodalitäten», changing nothing; the Treuhand
  only when she has no access. «Abrechnungsart» no longer says that vereinbart applies unless the
  firm asked for vereinnahmt, and the setup's AI is given the same line, in her language, so it no
  longer names a basis as the one that applies. In Italian the basis question is «Sistema di rendiconto», the
  ESTV's word, no longer the name of the portal's card.
- Setup: the form marks what its kind of firm answered and she did not touch «angenommen», with one
  line why at the top of the questions («Diese Antworten sind angenommen, weil es bei Handwerk, Bau
  (GmbH) üblich ist: ändere, was nicht stimmt»); a touched answer loses the mark. Form and setup
  conversation keep what is still assumed in the new books, and the conversation in the books shows
  it on the firm's card and in the summary with «angenommen», «stimmt» to confirm it, and a link
  where the app changes it (the VAT setup, or what the answer set up, named: «Konto des Inhabers
  unter Firma › Geldkonten»), never as «deine Antwort». The opening's words follow «Ausgangslage»: a switch takes the old books'
  balance sheet, not «Eine neue Firma …». After «Buchhaltung anlegen» with a question open the focus
  goes to that question, which says «bitte wählen», not to its first answer. Kontoauszüge without a
  payment or a statement names no quarter and says what to drop: «Noch kein Kontoauszug.» (#385)
- One count per word (#385): the dock, Automatisierung and Belege put a receipt in one period, its
  payment's once it has one, else its date's; one without a date, or from before the books and not
  paid, is the working period's. So an undated till slip paid in Q3 counts in Q3 everywhere, and
  «gesammelt», «gelesen» and «zugeordnet» match Belege's «alle» and «zugeordnet» and the drop's
  sentence. A receipt paid in a later period now lists only there. «gelesen» means its text was read:
  one without a date or a possible copy is read, and said apart as before. Links and tasks about a
  receipt lead to the period Belege lists it in. The export keeps its receipts as before (their own
  date, and those of the period's payments). While no receipt is booked but payments are (bookings by
  hand, wages, the bank's fee), the dock says «11 von 23 Zahlungen gebucht» in place of «0 gelesen ·
  0 gebucht». Its third figure says «Aufgaben», counted as the corner's «n Aufgaben für dich» (open
  tasks, stopped runs and questions for you). Belege's «Braucht dich» says under its head what it
  counts.
- The way from a payment to a rule stays where the person is (#383): «Eine Regel für alle 3
  anlegen» under the hand-booking form opens the rule form right there, with the account, the VAT
  and a private share the form held; keeping or discarding it leaves her in her tasks. The rule
  form's account is the hand form's account field: a word («Lohn», «Miete») finds the account.
- A rule asks no VAT where none applies (#383): a rule on the wage account, a debt, the owner's
  or a money account, or for payments the evidence rules recognise as wages, books without a code,
  unless a receipt of its payments shows Swiss VAT: then it asks. The wage account counts only
  while the firm's account of that number is an expense account. A rule started from the hand
  form takes its VAT only when a receipt gave the rate or she typed it, never the zero code the
  form proposes for a payment without a receipt yet. Where it asks, «Keine Schweizer MWST» stands first, the rates as equals after it, with one line
  on how to tell from the receipt.
- The wider text rule under a payment («oder eine für alle 6 mit «Lohn» im Text») is offered only
  on words that say what the payments are (#383): never on a document's word («Rechnung»), a
  statement's («Belastung», «Gutschrift») or the bank's wording of a payment («Zahlung», «Karte»,
  `payment` in modules/ch/documents.yaml), and only where the businesses it reaches agree in kind.
- Drop without an AI (#384): a text PDF reads the total it labels as its total («Total CHF»,
  «Gesamtbetrag», «Rechnungsbetrag», «Amount due», «Montant total» …, with its currency; also in a
  column of amounts apart from its labels) when it shows no Swiss VAT: her own invoice of a sale
  abroad, a foreign supplier's invoice in USD or EUR, an insurance premium. A gross the Swiss-rate
  arithmetic proves still wins over a label line; a line of VAT, a base («auf …»), a payroll,
  a sum insured, hours or a 0.00 due is never the total. The VAT is now also proven from a line that
  names the rate on its base («MWST 8.1% auf …», «darin MWST 3.8% auf …»), the part without VAT at
  0 %. A text that says it charges no Swiss VAT (modules/ch documents.yaml `noVat`: «von der MWST
  ausgenommen», «not registered for VAT in Switzerland», «reverse charge» …) and shows no Swiss rate
  and no Swiss VAT number reads «keine CH-MWST», provisionally: an AI reads it again when there is
  one. A statement or two invoices in one PDF stay unread; the only amount of a document is its
  total only on an invoice and never a unit price.
- The drop's closing lines count every file once, in its row's words: statements, receipts, files
  that are no receipt, were there already, wait for an answer in their row, were set aside or not
  saved; also for a drop with nothing taken.
- An order confirmation, a delivery note, a reminder or a customs assessment is no «kein Beleg» any
  more: its row says «Auftragsbestätigung, gehört zu …» (or that its invoice is not in the books
  yet) without an amount, Belege lists it beside «alle» («und 1 Begleitdokument») instead of hiding
  it, and the invoice's detail names it. A total of 0.00 the AI read off it no longer keeps it from
  its invoice.
- A bank CSV whose payments the books hold already (the camt.053 of the same days, three payments
  or more, or on the account its IBAN names) says «schon da» instead of asking for its columns,
  with «Trotzdem übernehmen» as the way out; with known columns and one bank account it goes
  straight to the check. «Ist ein Kontoauszug» shows only where a file can be one.
- Belege shows a booked receipt without a date in its own group instead of hiding it; Kontoauszüge
  counts files in its file view.

- The bank's own fee (#359): a row of a bank's camt.053 export that the bank itself coded as its
  charge for the account or for payments («Kontoführung», a card's annual fee) is booked by the
  app, with or without an AI, on Bankspesen (6940) without a VAT code; a fee paid back the other
  way. A rule of the firm that matches the row still decides it. A row under the bank's name
  without that code (a loan, interest) and a custody fee go to the AI or to you as before. A chart
  without 6940 as an expense account leaves the row unbooked, and its form «Von Hand buchen» says
  which account is missing. On upgrade: the next inbox run books every unbooked fee row of an open
  period that the bank coded so; nothing already booked changes. Statements read from a PDF, a
  photo or a CSV never mark a fee row, so those go on as before.
- AI answers (#362): every message to the AI sidebar starts with what became of each rule the
  earlier answers drafted, rule by rule (kept and on, switched off or removed since, discarded,
  still a draft, or unknown), and of held changes, so it no longer says a kept rule is not kept.
  An answer and the «Stand» card under it wrap inside the sidebar instead of running past its
  edge. The booking model is told never to take an amount, share or ratio from a casebook example.
  The compensation fund is found as the payee only (a new `payee` match in a business file: the start of the counterparty's name), also as «SVA St. Gallen», «SVA Zürich», «SVA Aargau», «Caisse de
  compensation» and «Cassa di compensazione», so its payment needs no receipt; a company's own file
  of a business wins over the app's file that claims the same payment. Automatisierung offers no
  period before the books begin, names the receipts without a payment beside «Keine Aufgabe wartet
  auf dich», and its headline reads «… Belege gesammelt, … davon gebucht».
- Without an AI (#365): the firm's own invoice is paired with the money in that names its number.
  Only an invoice the firm issued (its UID; a firm without one, its name heading the invoice) is
  paid by money in, and only by the number an invoice word labels («Rechnung 2026-029»), never a
  customer, account or phone number. Code links it when that payment alone names the number, with
  the invoice's amount and on or after its day, and no payment of that amount goes the way an
  invoice is paid; two payments naming it, a number other payments name too, a payment that names
  another invoice of the series too, an offer, order confirmation, delivery note or reminder are
  proposed at most, and a part payment is never linked. A supplier's invoice is never paired with
  a refund that names it. The payment then opens «Von Hand buchen» with the sale at the invoice's
  rate; nothing is booked without her.
- A task about a payment names it everywhere, the corner included: «Zahlung von Hand buchen ·
  Familie Keller», with the amount and the day. Three or more such tasks are one line in the corner,
  «11 Zahlungen von Hand buchen», listed under Aufgaben (#365).
- The account field finds an account by the whole words a person types for it besides its name
  («Miete», «Benzin», «Versicherung», «Werkzeug», «Autoversicherung»): the Swiss chart's accounts
  carry them as `hints`, used while the firm's chart still names the account as the standard does.
  ↵ after typing takes no row a hint alone found on the other side of the payment (#365).
- The dock's card says how many of the period's payments are booked («24 von 30 Zahlungen
  gebucht»), a booking by hand without a receipt among them; the bar's «gebucht» stays the receipts
  booked, and its tip names each figure's unit (#365).

- The first screen of a fresh install is about the books, not the AI (#343): what the app is in
  two sentences, then «Neue Buchhaltung erstellen», open existing books, «Von anderer Software
  übernehmen» or the demo, with the language switch in sight. The look at this Mac's AI runs in
  the background: with an AI ready the setup conversation runs as before; without one, new books
  open the form at once, and one line offers «KI einrichten». Before the Terminal opens, each
  program's card says what it needs and what happens: an account with the vendor, a paid plan
  (about 20 USD a month), the sign-in in the browser in English, that the app sees neither
  password nor login, and that it can wait. The install command is folded under «Befehl zeigen»,
  also in Firma › KI and the KI column. «Von anderer Software übernehmen» no longer promises to
  take the old journal as examples: it says that nothing is imported by itself yet.
- Belege says as a fact how many receipts wait for the AI's reading on a Mac without one («3
  Belege warten aufs Lesen: das macht die KI.»), with «KI einrichten» and «Von Hand erfassen».
- The demo says that it is one: a line along the bottom names the made-up firm while the demo is
  open and leads back to your own books; opened from the start screen it shows Automatisierung,
  and the first time a card says what to look at.
- The start screen names no files any more («Den Ordner wählen, in dem deine Buchhaltung liegt.»).
- After a drop, the window says what came of it and leads there (#341). Its last lines count the
  payments read and from when to when, how many are booked and how many wait for what («23 Zahlungen
  eingelesen, 02.07.2026 – 30.09.2026: 12 gebucht, 8 warten auf ihren Beleg, 3 warten auf deine
  Antwort»), and the receipts read and linked, each with a link to them in their period. The page
  behind the window now goes to the period most of the files landed in. Before, the newest date
  among them decided: one till photo without a date, dated today, left the person in an empty
  quarter beside 23 payments. A receipt dated only by the day the phone took it decides nothing.
- MWST opens on the oldest return not filed, when its link names no period: the period after the
  filed ones, or for books that never filed the period of their first payment or booking. Before, it opened where the newest file was dated, which could be the empty current
  quarter while the quarter before held all the payments. The other pages still open on the newest
  data, and a later return still names the earlier one that is not filed.
- A return's figure that is not final says so. While payments of the period are not booked, wait
  for their receipt, or questions are open, MWST shows «noch nicht endgültig» beside the amount and
  one line with the counts («11 Zahlungen noch nicht gebucht (8 davon warten auf ihren Beleg) · 2
  Fragen offen»); a click opens the list «Noch offen in Q3» beside the return (`/vat/open`), the same
  list the review of the return shows. The result no longer tells to transfer the amount to the
  ESTV while something is open, also when it is 0.00 because nothing is booked yet. A final result
  of 0.00 says «Keine Steuer geschuldet: nichts zu überweisen. Die Abrechnung ist trotzdem
  einzureichen.» An approved return keeps its figure and its instruction.
- Automatisierung no longer says «Nichts wartet auf dich» while payments of a period not filed are
  not booked: it says «Keine Aufgabe wartet auf dich. 11 Zahlungen noch nicht gebucht (alle warten
  auf ihren Beleg)», with a link to them, and only the count when some of them are yours to book.
  A drop of files the books hold already says that nothing new came in. The dock's card says the same; its number still counts
  tasks, stopped runs and questions only.
- A workflow's drawing names what leads from step to step in words («Freigegeben», «Geändert»),
  and a task links to its run with «Lauf ansehen», not with the run's id.
- Without an AI, booking by hand starts from what the app knows (#344). The form «Von Hand buchen»
  of a payment opens filled, once the app knows what the payment is: a rule of the firm that
  decides it (also one that asks first, with its question), else the account of the same
  business's last booking. A customer's payment of an invoice the app issued settles Debitoren
  without a code where the invoice's revenue is booked already (agreed basis), else takes the
  invoice's own accounts and codes, and the form points to confirming the payment on the invoice.
  An invoice paid already is not proposed as revenue again: only what is open is, and the form
  names the rest (a double payment to send back); a payment before its invoice on the agreed basis
  is a prepayment to confirm on the invoice. Money from the owner (her name as the payer) goes on
  her account without a code; money between your own accounts, from an authority or with another
  business's document is no sale and gets no revenue account and no code; money back from a
  supplier, up to what the firm paid it, goes on the account of its last purchase. Each assumption
  is said under the form («Die App nimmt eine Rückzahlung an. Ein Verkauf? Dann das Konto
  ändern.»). When the app cannot load what it knows of the payment, the form opens without a
  proposal and says so. Other money in is
  taken for a sale on the firm's default revenue account, with the code the app would book (on the
  net tax rate method the firm's rate), and the form says so: «Eine Rückzahlung oder eine Einlage?
  Dann das Konto ändern.» A sale whose document is not read gets no code and says why. Money out
  with a receipt gets the receipt's rate and an account only where a rule, an earlier booking or
  the business's file names one. A receipt gives the rate, and the code follows the account you
  choose: a proposed V81 leaves when you book onto the owner's account, a proposed M81 becomes I81
  on an operating cost. On the agreed basis a receipt dated in another period than its payment
  proposes no account and says why. The line under the fields names each proposal and where it
  comes from; nothing you typed or picked is replaced, and it is booked only by your click.
- The account field of that form finds what a layperson types: «Ertrag», «Einnahme» or «Umsatz»
  lists the revenue accounts, «Aufwand» or «Kosten» the expenses, though no account is named so.
  For money in the revenue accounts come first, for money out the expenses, the firm's default
  accounts on top; ↵ takes the first row, which is the one marked. «Dienst» + ↵ on a customer
  payment takes 3400, not 1170 Vorsteuer. The VAT field, once the account is chosen, lists for a
  typed rate («8.1») only the codes of that account's kind (three for a sale instead of fifteen)
  and says so; a code or its name still finds any code.
- A task about a payment names it (who, how much, when), so three «Zahlung ohne Buchung» read
  apart. Under the form: how many payments of the kind are not booked, and «Eine Regel für alle n
  anlegen», which opens Firma › Regeln › «Neue Regel» started from this payment; where the
  payment's lasting words («Lohn») hold for more open payments than its payer does, a second link
  starts the rule with them (`/company/rules?payment=<ref>&by=text`), only when they tell the
  payments apart (never the bank's «Belastung E-Banking Auftrag»). A rule started by text takes
  those words as whole words («Lohn», not «Lohnausweis»), not the whole text of one payment with
  its month and year, and is kept only after «Trotzdem behalten», as any rule on one word.
- A photo or scan no AI read is «noch nicht gelesen», no longer «gelesen»: in the drop dialog (with why,
  «KI einrichten» and «Selbst eintippen»), in the list of Belege instead of «ohne Datum», on the
  receipt (a notice with both ways, and under Datei instead of «(kein Text erkannt)») and in its
  Verlauf («hat den Beleg abgelegt, nicht gelesen»).
- The drop dialog says «beiseitegelegt, wird nicht verarbeitet» instead of «Quarantäne, kein
  Ablauf», and no longer how a file was recognised («Struktur», «Hinweis»).
- Setup (#364): «MWST-Methode», «Abrechnungsart» and «Ausgangslage» say, in the form, the setup
  conversation and `npm run init`, the facts that tell (the Saldosteuersatz is a rate the ESTV approved
  on the firm's application, filed on form 0553; vereinbart is what the law provides, vereinnahmt
  applies when the firm asked for it and the ESTV approved it; the Treuhand knows) and what a wrong pick costs (Firma › MWST changes
  it while the year has no entries); «Abrechnungsart» no longer cites «MWSTG Art. 39» or «auf
  Gesuch», «Ausgangslage» says what each answer changes. Neither VAT question is
  answered for her any more: no kind of firm preselects them, no answer is highlighted as the
  proposal, and the form asks for both before it creates the books. Before the books the count is of four
  parts, the same for every firm («2 von 4 erledigt», with Firma · Art der Firma · MWST · Bank), no
  longer of questions («0 von 14», «1 von 4», «4 von 8»). The review marks the answers the kind of
  firm gave and she never said «angenommen», naming that kind of firm, until she answers them. In the books, after an answer
  the newest question comes into sight, and after the last one the summary starts with the first
  step, one sentence with «Kontoauszug einlesen», the rest behind «Mehr dazu»; it stays in sight
  while an AI answer streams in and when the setup opens again on it, until she scrolls. «Partner und
  Wissen», never asked on books without bookings, is no longer counted as discussed («4 von 4»,
  listed «kommt mit den ersten Buchungen»). Without an AI the greeting no longer offers «in deinen
  Worten», and the receipts step offers «KI einrichten» instead of «Mit mir einrichten». Until the
  look at this Mac's AI has answered, the receipts step offers neither, so «Mit mir einrichten» is
  never shown and then taken back when a slow look says no AI answers. A look that fails ends as
  «no AI» with its reason and is taken again when you come back to the window, instead of leaving
  the step without either. With books open, the Mac app's window now always loads its own build of
  the page; a checkout with an older build in its `dist/` could serve that one after the setup.

- Setup (#347): «MWST-Methode» and «Abrechnungsart» have a third answer, «Wir rechnen keine MWST ab»,
  in the form, the setup conversation and `npm run init` (`--vat-method no-vat`). It answers «nein» to
  «Im MWST-Register eingetragen?», so the method, the basis and the period are no longer asked; a line
  under it says it is only for a firm not in the VAT register (registration is required from CHF
  100’000 turnover a year) and that a registered firm that does not know its method asks its
  Treuhand, that a firm whose Treuhand files its returns is registered, and what a wrong pick costs.
  The review and the summary say plainly what follows, the app's invoices without VAT included; the
  summary and Firma › MWST say that it changes there while the year has no entries, and an invoice
  draft of a firm not registered says «Rechnung ohne MWST». The VAT question in the books offers the
  same answer and no longer «Weiss nicht»; its card confirms with the summary's sentence. Typed words
  never make the firm unregistered: the answer is always her click.

### Fixed

- The compensation fund's Akonto payment is booked net of the employees' share also when the
  payslips come in the same drop as the bank statement (#489). The drop hands each file to its own
  run: the statement's run booked the fund's payment (it waits for no receipt) while the wages were
  still net, and the payslips' run booked the wages again gross but never asked about the payment
  again, so the employees' AHV/IV/EO and ALV stayed in the employer's cost. Now every run books a
  contribution payment the app booked to the share the payslips withheld for that fund, whenever
  that share changes, one line per account, marked as decided by code with its own reason; a payment
  she booked or changed herself, or whose proposal she took back («Rückgängig» makes the lines it
  puts back hers, as a change by hand), stays as it is. Books format: the check `withheldShare` of
  `JournalEntry.by` is part of books schema 9 (#450, not released before), no step of its own. Where a proposal is
  still offered, it says what it does in her words: «Vorschlag: Den Arbeitnehmeranteil (CHF …) aus
  den Lohnabrechnungen von den Sozialversicherungskosten abziehen», the button «Arbeitnehmeranteil
  abziehen» (before: «von 5700 auf 2270 umbuchen», «2270 ausgleichen»), in all four languages.
- A run no longer offers «Als Eröffnungssaldo übernehmen» for a bank balance from inside the year
  (#487): the books open on 1 January, so a first statement of Q3 with the balance of 30.06. is no
  opening, and taking it would have left out the first half year on every balance sheet. The
  offer stands only for a statement's opening balance of a day before 1.1. (31.12., or the last
  day the bank booked before a first statement of 5.1.) or of 1.1., when the account moved on no
  day before 1.1. A camt opening balance dated on a day the bank booked is read as the close of the
  day before, so a 31.12. balance with fees of 31.12. is not offered. When an account's statements
  begin later, the run says so («Die Auszüge dieses Kontos beginnen am 01.07.2026. Den Saldo per
  1.1.2026 zeigen frühere Auszüge oder die Schlussbilanz des Vorjahres in der Eröffnung:») with
  both ways out, «Frühere Auszüge ablegen» and «Eröffnung erfassen».
- Mac app (#373): no question of macOS comes before the app said why. At the first start of
  another build (an unsigned build is a new app to macOS each time, rc to rc too), books in
  Documents, Desktop, Downloads, iCloud Drive, a cloud provider's folder or on a volume are no
  longer read before the window: the start screen says «Deine Buchhaltung … liegt im Ordner «Dokumente».
  Gleich fragt macOS …» and opens them on «Weiter»; their entry in «Zuletzt geöffnet» (start
  screen and menu) shows the same words first. The chat's microphone says, before macOS first
  asks about the microphone and the speech recognition, what both are for; «Prüfen» of a source
  that reads the Mail app says that macOS will ask whether the app may control Mail. The phone's
  listener (Firma › Handy, which the firewall may ask about) starts again only once books are open.
- A drop in which the AI's booking of one payment was refused (#482) no longer leaves stuck runs
  «Eingang verarbeiten: Lauf steckt» behind the task badge. The run finishes, and the payment says
  why it waits, as #470 meant. «Automatisch buchen» on such a payment goes on to «Von Hand
  buchen». A run that sticks on a fault of the app itself now says so in her words: «In der App
  ist hier ein Fehler passiert: der Lauf ist angehalten und hat nichts Falsches geschrieben.» The
  run's page adds «Weiter» versucht es noch einmal, beside that button. Before, it showed an
  internal step name and file. A payment's and a statement's «Verlauf» show the same words. A
  company's own step that lists what it hands on keeps every key its action hands on, so such a
  workspace keeps booking.

- A courier's customs invoice whose own VAT on its fee is read as a tax line at no rate («MWST»
  beside a Vorlageprovision at 8.1 %, the line as much as 8.1 % of the fee) books that VAT on the
  fee's line (4040 M81), no longer as import VAT without input tax (4030 M0), and
  «Veranlagungsverfügung fehlt» no longer asks an assessment for it (#483). One tax line per rate
  is the courier's: the one that states the rate, else the one bare «MWST» line as much as that
  rate's tax on its fees (also read with the tax in), never one as much as an assessment linked
  beside the bill or one that names more than the tax; every other is import VAT. «MWST 8.1% auf …» is
  now split in code the same way instead of going to the AI. Payments booked already stay as they are.
- The drop window (#483): a file whose run stopped says what the books made of it, as Belege
  does: a balance sheet «Bilanz» with «Eröffnung erfassen», a product photo «kein Beleg»; only a
  receipt still waiting for its payment, its reading or its date and amount says «stehen
  geblieben», and only those count as waiting for her.
- The compensation fund's payment with its bill linked (#476): it was booked whole as the
  employer's expense while 2270 still held the employees' share the payslips had withheld, so that
  share was an expense twice. Where every payslip behind 2270 is linked and its deductions read per
  kind (AHV/IV/EO and ALV the fund's, BVG the pension fund's) and add up, the app books exactly the
  fund's part on 2270, never more than 2270 holds, and the rest as the employer's contribution;
  the booking says so. Where it cannot tell, the open item «Beitragszahlung ohne den Anteil der
  Arbeitnehmenden» offers «2270 ausgleichen» on the return's list and on the payment: a proposal in
  words, booked by her click in one write and undone in the toast, the lines back with who booked
  them and why. Where the payslips tell the fund's share, the item and the click ask for what the
  payment lacks of it, never for another fund's deductions on 2270. The payslips are read in the
  layouts payroll programs and pdftotext give (a label with its amount, columns whose order the
  rates prove, a minus after the amount, a correction row, a payment of several wages); a row
  that is no deduction (an AHV number, a basis, a sentence) names no fund. No VAT figure changes.
- «Was muss ich jetzt noch machen?» (#476): the card under the answer and the facts the AI is
  handed list what the return's list «Noch offen» lists, in its order (a contribution payment
  without the employees' share among them), and then what only the card adds (a proposal of the
  setup, a missing opening). The firm's own invoice that only waits for its customer is a fact on
  all three («eigene Rechnung, wartet auf die Zahlung der Kundschaft»), never a point to count.
- The open list under a return (#477) no longer opens with «Nichts davon hält die Abrechnung auf»
  while payments of the period are unbooked. Its first sentence names them, the money in and the
  money out with their count and sum: «4 Zahlungseingänge über CHF … und ein Zahlungsausgang über
  CHF … sind noch nicht gebucht: die Zahl ist noch nicht endgültig.» These
  are the same payments the return's page counts beside «noch nicht endgültig». With every payment
  booked and nothing withheld the sentence is as before. What withholds «Freigegeben» is unchanged.
- The drop window with many files (#453): the line «Gesamt 51 % · 1 von 43 Dateien fertig» no
  longer lies over the tabs (it shrank to nothing once the list was long), and «Schliessen» no
  longer falls below a small window: between the head and the foot, what came of the drop, the
  list and the lines below it scroll as one. From 10 files the window shows an overview: one bar
  for the whole drop with what is done now («3 werden hochgeladen · 6 warten auf den Start · 1
  sucht die Zahlung»), and where the files stand, in counts («45 Dateien · 9 brauchen dich · 2
  Kontoauszüge eingelesen · 12 einer Zahlung zugeordnet · …»), each a filter of the list in place
  of the tabs, kept for the whole drop. What needs her is listed first; the files still worked on
  or done are a line each with their count, opened by a click. The counts come from the states the
  rows show and follow them live; a filter whose last file left it shows the whole drop again. A
  file is judged by its own run only: a later file's run, which takes up the receipts still waiting
  for their payment again, no longer sets the files at rest back to «in Arbeit» and the bar back to
  0 %; and the window asks for the runs' steps at once when a file is taken, every 2 s while files
  are worked on, in one loop of asks that returns to every 10 s; the bar never goes back.
- A payment of social insurance contributions while the wage deductions account (2270) holds
  nothing is booked, and a payment a run leaves unbooked says why (#470). With wages booked net,
  the AI is told that 2270 holds nothing and the whole contribution is the firm's expense (only
  when the books carry a wage in the year: a firm without wages paying its owner's own AHV is told
  nothing); an answer that still takes more off 2270 than it holds is booked in the same run with
  what 2270 holds on it and the rest on the employer's contribution account the answer names
  (else the payee's earlier one, else 5700),
  and the booking's reason says so (before: nothing was booked and the reason stood only in the
  run's detail). A payment a run tried and left unbooked keeps the run's reason in the run log:
  its page says «nicht gebucht: …», the open list, the MWST page's list, the drop's summary and
  the AI's card name it beside the payment, and its «Verlauf» names the run (before: «Bisher hat
  kein Lauf hieran gearbeitet»). The hand form of a contribution payment proposes the payee's
  earlier contribution account (else 5700) and, for a compensation fund, what 2270 holds on 2270;
  for another payee it takes nothing off 2270 and says what 2270 holds. No VAT figure
  changes: contributions carry no code.
- «Noch offen»: a month of a payment provider's fees without its bill names the bill as the
  provider's file names it and where the provider keeps it («Shopify Payments Invoice» …
  «Shopify Admin › Settings › Billing › Bills»), and shows the VAT it would show, estimated from
  the fees at the bill's rate («geschätzt CHF … (8.1 % in CHF … Gebühren)»), no longer the
  fees as if they were the VAT. Its «Rechnung hinzufügen» opens the file dialog that drops it; the
  line no longer opens Belege on another receipt, and the sidebar's card leads to the list (#459).
- What the sidebar's AI is told about an account without its opening and a provider's clearing
  account carries days and states (#459). The bank's balance the statements imply is the one
  before their first payment, on its day: books whose statements begin on 01.07. no longer hand
  it over as the balance of 1.1., and the card says «laut Auszügen … vor der ersten Zahlung am
  01.07.2026». A clearing account below zero names its provider's reports in the books with their
  days and the bookings each made, and whether the account has its opening, instead of guessing
  «a sales report not dropped yet».
- Mac app, first start: macOS no longer asks about the Documents folder over the first screen
  before you chose anything (#373). The app reads nothing in Documents, Desktop or Downloads until
  a step needs it. «Neue Buchhaltung erstellen» first says that your books go into «Dokumente»,
  that macOS will now ask and that «Erlauben» is needed, with «Anderen Ordner wählen» beside
  «Weiter»; after «Nicht erlauben» it says where to allow it in the System Settings and still
  works with another folder. Once allowed, the step goes on at once. Books opened before in such a
  folder are read only after macOS answered: when it asks at a later start, the window comes first
  with the start screen and the books open after «Erlauben». The menu's «Zuletzt geöffnet» names
  them without reading their folders, and the start screen says that macOS asks about the folder
  of those books while it does. Books in a folder macOS refuses keep their name and say how to
  allow it; a folder whose own rights refuse the app says so apart. New books never open books
  already in the folder named for them: the form proposes a free name («Buchhaltung 2026 2»), also
  after a restart. macOS's answer counts for this version of the app only: after an update the
  words come again before macOS may ask again.
- A provider's monthly Payments Invoice no longer waits for the AI (#458): no payment comes for it,
  so the inbox takes its date and total from its text by code and the booking step books its input
  tax, as for the other months. Before, one failed AI answer left the bill in the inbox with
  «fehlt: Datum, Betrag» and its input tax unbooked. Where Belege says the booking without a
  payment «fehlt noch», the new button «Vorsteuer buchen» books it at once, also on a date and
  total she typed; it says why when it cannot («kein Datum», «eingereichte Periode», no fee
  account). Such a bill no longer offers «Mit Zahlung verknüpfen» as its main action. A copy of
  such a bill with other bytes (the same invoice number in the same month, or a copy as Belege finds
  one), and one she marked as a copy, no longer books its input tax a second time: Belege names the
  bill it copies. The button is not offered on a firm without input tax.
- «Buchung passt nicht zu ihrem Beleg» no longer names a line the receipt leaves untaxed (#455). A
  hotel bill of a room at 3.8 % and a Kurtaxe without VAT, on a card settlement, booked room I38
  and Kurtaxe I0, got the task «Der Beleg zeigt 3.8 % MWST, die Buchung steht auf … I0» for the
  Kurtaxe, and «Nach Beleg buchen» claimed CHF 0.22 input tax on it. The check now takes the bill of
  a card statement's line as evidence of that line's bookings only, sets a VAT group the receipt
  names by its tax alone against the lines at the gross that tax proves (allowing for the tax's
  rounding to the cent), leaves a receipt in another currency out of the sum, and tells a line at 0 %
  beside a line at the receipt's rate nothing when no receipt in the line's currency can be compared.
- «Buchung passt nicht zu ihrem Beleg» now also names an over-claim of input tax (#469): a line at a
  rate above the receipt's (I81 on a bill at 2.6 %), and a rate on more than the receipt taxes
  (I81 on a whole bill whose tax covers only part of it, a tip on top of a bill, a Kurtaxe booked
  at 3.8 % beside the room, also on the whole card settlement). The task says «Der Beleg zeigt
  8.1 % MWST nur auf …, gebucht sind … auf I81», and «Nach Beleg buchen» books the receipt's rate on what it taxes and the rest at the zero code. A line at 0 %
  alone on a receipt that names its tax only is offered the rate only on what that tax covers. A
  hotel bill read as the room's group and «0 %» without an amount, booked room I38 and Kurtaxe I0,
  is no longer told «Der Beleg zeigt 3.8 % MWST» on the Kurtaxe (whose click booked it at 3.8 %); a
  group's gross read above what its tax proves counts as what the tax proves. The
  app's own runs book no such line where the receipt's VAT is known: a rule's or the model's rate
  goes on what the receipt taxes at it, the rest to another rate the receipt shows, then to the
  zero code, with the reason on the line. An under-claim and a line without a receipt are told as
  before; a person's booking is never changed.
- The fields of Firma's forms stand in line at every width (#454): a hint under one input («kein
  Konto», the account's name) or a label on two lines no longer pushes the field beside it down
  (Eröffnung, Regeln and the others). Firma › Eröffnung names its amount fields «Saldo per 1.1.2026
  (CHF)» and, for a foreign-currency account, «Saldo per 1.1.2026 (EUR)» beside it, and its table's
  column (and Abschluss's) «Saldo per 1.1.2026», no longer «Eröffnung 1.1.2026», which read like a
  date; an amount typed there is right-aligned and shown in the app's number format once left.
- A repayment to the owner before any of her outlays is booked (#457): «Spesen Juli 2026» said
  «wartet auf ihren Beleg; ist er verknüpft, bucht die App sie», though no receipt is ever linked to
  it. While receipts that could be hers are filed (read, dated before it, paid by no payment of the
  firm, not its own invoice) it now says it waits for her privately paid receipts to be booked as
  outlays, in the period's list, the bank detail, the drop, «Automatisch buchen» and the hand form,
  as it did once one was booked. Without such a receipt, and for a payment to her a rule decides, the
  receipt wait stays.
- A receipt that names no VAT is «MWST noch nicht gelesen», no longer «keine CH-MWST» (#391).
  Only a model, or the receipt's own words («nicht MWST-pflichtig», «not subject to Swiss VAT», «von
  der MWST ausgenommen», never beside «inkl. MWST» or a Swiss rate, 7.7 % too), say it carries no
  Swiss VAT; the next run with an AI reads it. Nothing claims input tax on such a receipt: with an
  AI its payment waits until the receipt is read (the run says «… warten, bis die MWST ihrer Belege
  gelesen ist»; «Buchen» on the payment reads it first, in the same click), a rate code the AI or a rule writes on it becomes the zero code, and the
  company's «unlesbarer Beleg zählt als 8.1 %» counts only a receipt that could not be read. A rule
  drafted on such receipts asks for the VAT instead of filling «keine», and Bezugsteuer is no longer
  booked because a receipt said nothing. A VAT word counts only as a word: «August», «Muster» and
  «Industrie» are no longer read as «USt». TVA, IVA and a UID written with spaces («CHE 111.222.333
  TVA») count now.
- Pairing (#426): a bill whose own number the payment names, with the same amount to the cent, is
  paired by code past its terms too, up to half a year after its date (a compensation fund's Akonto
  bill of early July paid at the end of September), so the payment is booked with it in the same
  drop, not «ohne Beleg». Past the terms only a number of six characters or more that is neither the
  amount nor a day or a month, that one payment names and one receipt carries, and a bill dated on
  or after the books' first payment: last year's bill of a licence or a policy that keeps its number
  is never paired with this year's payment. A title written as a compound with a number of its own
  («Akontorechnung AK-26-Q3-30871», «Prämienrechnung PR-2026-2210») is read as the bill's title and
  number; a payslip («Lohnabrechnung»), annual accounts («Erfolgsrechnung 2025») or a calculation are
  not, a reminder or an order confirmation below such a title stays what it is, and a compound
  credit note («Warengutschrift WG-…») pays in.
- Pairing (#456): the compensation fund's Akonto bill is now paired with its payment in the drop.
  The fund's payments are booked without waiting for a receipt, and that had also kept them from
  ever being paired, so the bill stayed «gelesen, noch keine Zahlung» beside a payment booked «ohne
  Beleg». Only the compensation fund's bills now pair (its file says `pairs: true`); the ESTV's and
  the tax office's notices stay filed apart, never paired with their payment. A payment of the VAT
  itself claims no input tax: the ESTV's and the tax office's files say so, and a line on the VAT
  settlement account (2201, 2200) carries no VAT code whatever the AI answers, also where its answer
  «the amount is the tax» had made an I81-2. In «Zahlung verknüpfen» a payment of the same amount
  that names the receipt's own number comes first, above a «Skonto?» or a rounding guess.
- Social insurance (#426): with wages booked gross, the AI is told what the deductions account
  (2270) holds on the payment's day, deducted on the payslips and not paid on yet, for a payment to
  the compensation fund or to a business booked on the contribution accounts before, so a bill that
  states its contributions by kind and not by party books the employees' share on 2270 and only the
  employer's share on the staff expense (it booked the whole payment as expense, and 2270 kept the
  employees' share). Such payments are asked after the wages of the same drop are booked, and the
  year's opening of 2270 counts. An answer that takes more off 2270 than it holds is not booked. A
  payment to the compensation fund (or to a payee that took its share off 2270 before) booked on
  AHV/ALV or BVG with nothing on 2270, while 2270 still holds a credit at the period's end, is named
  under «Noch offen» («gebucht ohne den Anteil der Arbeitnehmenden», with what 2270 holds then);
  never one the employer pays alone (family allowances), and gone once 2270 is cleared. No VAT
  figure changes.
- «Noch offen» (#428): the sentence above the list named an open VAT question and an order a sales
  report could not split for every firm, one without a sales report too. It now names only what of
  the list holds up «Freigegeben», counted as the review counts it (a net tax rate without its
  activity code among them), or says that nothing of it does.
- The owner's repayments (#428): a payment to the owner that waited while her account held outlays
  not enough for it said «wartet auf ihren Beleg». It now says that it waits until the receipts
  paid privately are booked as outlays (on the payment, in the counts beside the return and the
  AI's facts; the drop's summary counts it as not booked), and its hand form says what ends the
  wait. A payment to her that a company rule decides, or a wage, keeps its own wait. «Automatisch buchen» that books nothing says
  why (its receipt, the outlays, a receipt that cannot be read), and «Automatisch buchen» for all
  counts these payments as a group of their own. The inbox run that books an outlay of the owner's
  now books the repayment it covers in the same run: one of three repayments needed a second click.
- «Was muss ich jetzt noch machen?» (#428): the AI started with the firm's own invoice that its
  customer has not paid yet, where there is nothing to do, and left out a bank account without its
  opening balance and the setup's proposal waiting on a payment. The facts it is handed and the
  card under its answer now leave the firm's own invoice out of the work only when that is sure
  (its UID on it, no money in that may be its payment, on the agreed basis its issue booked), name a missing
  opening (Firma › Eröffnung), a payment provider's clearing account below zero and a proposal
  that waits, and list everything most important first, as code ranks it. A run attached to a
  message is named by its workflow and day, not by its id.
- A receipt's VAT typed by hand on a photo the app cannot read is kept (#438): the run that books
  its payment again tried the photo once more and dropped what she had entered, so the receipt read
  «MWST noch nicht gelesen», was called «nicht lesbar» and its payment lost the input tax. What a
  person enters stays over every later run, the model is told her VAT when it books the payment, and
  the receipt's Verlauf says «nicht gelesen · Angaben von Hand erfasst» instead of «hat den Beleg gelesen».
- «Gerüst», «Gerüstbau» and «Gerüstmiete» find an account in the account field (#438): Aufwand für
  bezogene Dienstleistungen (4400); before, «Gerüst» found none.
- Firma › Eröffnung (#427): a foreign-currency bank account is no longer refused with a terminal
  command. Typed, it shows a second field: its value in CHF and its balance in its own currency,
  as the balance sheet shows both; the statements are checked against the latter. Beside them the
  form names the balance's value at the ESTV rate of 31.12. and how far the typed value is off it,
  with a click to take it; values of opposite signs, or one of them 0, are refused.
- Kontoauszüge (#427): a difference to the bank that the opening explains is no longer blamed on «eine
  Zahlung … nicht oder falsch gebucht». A foreign-currency account without its opening is named
  «Anfangsbestand fehlt» in its currency, as a CHF account was; and books whose opening is entered
  but that start later in the year say that the difference lies before the statements' first
  payment, with the bank's and the books' balance on that day.
- Drop and Belege (#427): a balance sheet («Bilanz», «Zwischenbilanz», «Balance sheet», «Bilan»,
  «Bilancio») is no longer a receipt «gelesen, noch keine Zahlung» with its total as the amount. It is
  kept beside the receipts, never paid, and says so in its row and in Belege; the one of the last day
  of the year before the open year is offered for the opening («Eröffnung erfassen» to Firma ›
  Eröffnung). The opening stays the person's: nothing is written to it.
- After the drop she sees her data (#424): what «Dateien hinzufügen» reports as booked (a provider's
  reports, the payouts her answer in a row booked, a statement read or kept, a file taken back) is on
  every page at once, the MWST figure and the dock too; before, the page kept the figures of before
  the drop until the window was reloaded. Closing the window after a bank's PDF statement opens that
  statement in its own quarter; before, it opened a «Datei» view in today's quarter that said the
  file was not read.
- A payment provider's payouts list dropped before the firm has that provider is named as the
  provider's other export, with the reports to bring instead, at the first drop too (#425); before,
  it became a bank statement's column question; the dialog's head says it was set aside, not
  «schon in den Büchern». For a provider the firm does not have yet, its file
  must name four columns or more and the CSV have all of them, so a bank's CSV is never taken for
  one. A CSV whose table does not add up after its columns were answered ends with what to do and
  «Andere Spalten wählen» and «Weglegen»; one a camt.053 covers says to set it aside, not to keep a
  PDF, and offers «Weglegen».
- Keeping rules (#396, #392): «Rückgängig» on a second kept rule took back the first rule's bookings
  and the second rule's file. Each keep now has a line of its own above Aufgaben and Firma › Regeln
  («4 Zahlungen gebucht · Rückgängig»), true to its own numbers and still there after a reload; the
  server records what each keep did, and its undo takes back that keep alone. A rule kept before a
  later one of the same file says «Rückgängig erst, wenn die später behaltene Regel zurückgenommen
  ist» and offers «Alle 2 zurücknehmen, die neuste zuerst». «Rückgängig (bis …)» stands for a week,
  and only while it means what it says: once the rule booked more since, a period with one of its
  bookings is closed, or its file was changed otherwise, the line says so instead. A rule kept,
  taken back and kept again books the same payments again. The undo's removed bookings show under
  Änderungen, and it brings back exactly the tasks the keep closed. A rule kept from the AI sidebar
  shows the same line, «nichts gebucht · 2 warten auf ihren Beleg · Rückgängig». A payment a kept
  rule decides is no task «keine Regel der Firma passte» any more: the rule books it, or, when it
  waits for its receipt, the rule books it once the receipt is there. «Lohnzahlungen von Hand
  buchen» leaves Aufgaben once the wages are booked. «Automatisch buchen» in Automatisierung's list
  books what the firm's rules decide, asks no AI, and names what it left and why («10 braucht die KI
  oder dich …»), instead of opening the diagram; on a payment whose booking she deleted it asks first.
  A payment an account's reconciliation holds is booked by neither: the rule's card says it waits for
  the reconciliation.
- A payment provider's sales and fee reports stay receipts after a later drop (#405). The next run
  read only PDFs and rebuilt the receipts from them, so the reports (CSV files) and every link to the
  sales and fees they booked went from the books, while those bookings still named them and the
  files still lay in `receipts/`. A run now keeps every receipt whose file lies there. A file whose
  bytes changed under its name is read again: the new read is its receipt, the app's links to the old
  one go and its bookings are made again from the new read; a person's link goes along only when the
  new read says the same, else it stays with the old receipt, kept beside, as does a person's booking
  and what lies in a filed period. The run names each such file and how many links to check. Books damaged so get their reports back on the next run: each CSV in
  `receipts/` whose bytes the bookings name is its receipt again, with its links to every open
  booking and what it holds; nothing of a filed period changes.
- The write door refuses a module or a run that removes a receipt whose file still lies in the
  workspace, or a link whose receipt and payment both stand, unless it lets go of it on purpose (a
  person's «Rückgängig» of a report, a link of its own it replaces) (#405). A run can therefore stop
  on it: nothing is saved, and the message says that only a person removes such a receipt (Belege ›
  «Löschen») or link, or that it is the app's error to report.
- An order a sales report holds is an open item of the period of its day (#405): in «Noch offen»,
  on the MWST page's «noch nicht endgültig» line, in the sidebar's card and in the review. An order
  whose VAT rates the report cannot tell apart (a cart of tea and a teapot) withholds «Freigegeben»
  until it is booked: its report's receipt has «Aufteilen», one or two amounts at the firm's rates,
  booked by the person once they add up to the order. An order held for another reason (a possible
  test order, one booked elsewhere) is shown with that reason and withholds nothing, and one of the
  days before the provider's cut-over day (less its payout days) is counted, never held; one of a filed
  period stays named on its report and is no open item. A refund of an order split so is held for
  the person to book by hand. Each held order keeps its day, gross, currency and delivery country on
  the report's receipt: optional fields added to `report.held`, as `heldMore` and `filedRefunds` were.
- A month whose fees the provider's payments export booked while its monthly bill (Shopify's
  Payments Invoice) is not in the books is named as a fact in «Noch offen»: the VAT on those fees is
  not deducted. It does not make the period unfinished, and a month of a filed period is not named.
  A payout in the list is named by the provider the app took it for («Shopify»), not by the processor
  that paid it. The review's clearing account check names the total of the orders its sales report holds.
- The owner's outlays paid back (#402): a payment to the owner by name (the payee) that is no wage,
  in francs, that no company rule decides, and that the receipts the owner paid privately and the
  firm has not paid back yet cover (each such payment in date order against what is left after the
  earlier ones, across the year boundary; the opening balance is not counted) waits for no receipt.
  The hand form proposes bank / the owner's account without a code, never the salary's account «wie
  bei derselben Firma» (between payments to the owner a precedent counts only of the same kind, wage
  or no wage); with an AI the model is told so, and its answer is booked only onto the owner's account:
  any other answer books nothing and leaves the payment to the person, asked once (the form then says the AI did not book it). Every payment to the owner booked onto her account uses up what is open, covered or not (a draw too). A repayment in another currency
  gets no proposal, and the form says why. Guide rule `cls-owner-outlays-repaid`.
- A receipt the owner paid privately (#402) is read so by its payment line («Bezahlt mit Mastercard
  ****1150 (privat)») or by the owner's card, known from a receipt marked so or from two receipts paid
  privately, never a card that paid one of the firm's own statement payments. Its first button is
  «Als Auslage von <Inhaber> buchen», one click; the line under it says why («Karte ****1150 wie auf dem
  Beleg vom 08.07.2026») and what it books (Aufwand / Kontokorrent); a selection offers «Als Auslagen
  … buchen» for several. While the app books it, the receipt says so in place of a hand form with an
  empty account.
- Setup (#402): a private share of the phone or the car named in words («öppe d Hälfti») is kept with
  the words that named it in onboarding.json. The phone's: the provider's first booked payment offers
  the rule «50 % privat» on its card (once per business; «Nicht jetzt», «Nie vorschlagen»; gone once a
  rule takes it; not where the firm's chart uses the phone's account for something else). The car's
  stays for the year close, no rule offered. One changed answer on the review keeps naming her kind
  of firm («Beratung, Dienstleistungen (GmbH), mit einer Änderung») instead of «Etwas anderes».
- The model's zero input code (I0) is left off an account that takes no code (#402): a wage, the
  employer's contributions (AHV, FAK, BVG), the bank charges, a debt, the owner's account. No figure
  changes: I0 has no box. A rule's code stays what its card showed.
- A bill of several VAT groups (8.1 % and «0 % (Export)») is compared with all lines of its
  payment (#406): two lines booked each at its group's rate raise no task any more; a rate booked
  beyond what the bill shows at it does, naming the amount and rate the bill shows. What a bill's
  total holds beyond the groups read only excuses a line at the zero code, never accuses a rate
  line. «Nach Beleg buchen» then books the payment's lines by the bill's groups, never one rate on
  a line the bill shows at another; it is offered only for lines on one account pair, of one code
  family, with the same tags, and never rewrites a line a person booked. Its «Rückgängig» puts the
  lines back in one step, unless one was changed since.
- The drop names a payment provider's payouts list as what it is, with the reports the app books
  from and, where the provider's file states it, where the admin exports them (#406); it is not
  read as a bank statement of empty lines, and «trotzdem als Kontoauszug lesen» asks its columns.
  Only for a firm whose own knowledge has that provider. A CSV whose day and amount columns yield
  no movement asks its columns with nothing chosen. A provider's file names such exports under
  `otherExports` and the report menus under `exportedAt` (processes/README.md).
- A provider's monthly fee bills dropped read «3 Monatsrechnungen von Shopify: MWST der Gebühren
  gebucht», not «einer Zahlung zugeordnet»: they have no payment (#406).
- The sale question for a transfer that names an order of a provider's order export by its name
  («#1099»), one the provider did not collect, proposes from that order's tax and says so (#406).
- MWST: a rate row's turnover (Ziffer 303 and the others) is the figure the return files, as the
  review and the XML show it; it was the nets rounded per entry plus the tax on the total, Rappen
  off (#406). No filed figure changes.
- Payment providers (#404): her answer in the drop row of the provider's report (the clearing
  account, the cut-over day, the fee account) no longer makes «Buchen» wait for «Einschalten»: an
  answer to the app's own question is her switching on, and the payouts it decides are booked at
  once, bank / clearing account without VAT; the row says «40 Auszahlungen von Shopify gebucht».
  The same file changed by hand still waits; when «Buchen» waited already, the row and Aufgaben say
  so, with the way to «Einschalten». «Einschalten» of «Buchen» books the payouts code decides and
  says how many.
- A provider's payout never gets a revenue proposal in the hand form: from its clearing account
  without a code once the company's file names it, otherwise no account (before the cut-over day:
  as the business's last booking), with a sentence on what a payout is and what it waits for. Its
  line on the payment names no file path any more. A revenue account that is none is refused.
- Aufgaben names a provider's payouts as its payouts («40 Auszahlungen von Shopify ohne Buchung»),
  grouped, with the one thing to do (the order export, or «Jetzt buchen»).
- The provider's questions in the drop row come one at a time and once (the second report row
  waits for the first), each labelled with its proposal and why; the cut-over day reads
  `dd.mm.yyyy`; the revenue account is shown with its source and can be changed; the fee code is
  said in words (the input tax comes from the provider's monthly invoice).
- Bank statements: every payment has a reference of its own (#401). A Wise camt.053 whose
  references stand only in the transaction details is read by them (the entry's own reference still
  wins). A Wise statement that names no bank reference for a payment is refused in its row, with the
  advice to download the camt.053 from the bank; nothing is written. Before, such payments came in on
  an empty reference: none could be opened, linked or booked, and a receipt linked to one was linked
  to all. Books that hold them keep them as they are (a filed one as filed) and import again; the
  same file dropped again gives the open ones their bank's reference and says so («7 Zahlungen haben
  jetzt ihre Referenz der Bank»), unless an entry or link of hers stands on several open ones at
  once, or an entry of hers beside filed ones (#419); she can delete such an entry, drop the file
  again and book it anew on its payment.
  No write can put a payment, a link or an entry on an empty reference any more. Such payments in a
  filed period no longer block every later statement; «Auszug entfernen» takes them along with their
  file; a second Wise account added in the drop row reads as a Wise account.
- Drop: «Ja, Bankkonto hinzufügen» on a statement of an account the books lack adds the account in
  its row (bank and ledger account proposed, currency and IBAN from the file) and reads the statement
  at once. The dialog stays open with every row and its closing lines; before, it closed and went to
  Firma › Geldkonten, and the statement had to be dropped again (#401).
- A credit card's monthly statement taken as a receipt says so in its drop row and its detail: the
  receipt of the card's debit on the bank account, its lines booked one by one where they were, and
  that the app does not read card statements as bank statements (#401, #94).
- The setup conversation keeps its order after a typed message (#385): the message stands under the
  question it was typed at, above that question's answer, and the open question with its buttons
  stays under her newest message, also when the words took a step back. In the books the rail shows
  the topics «n von 4 besprochen» counts («Partner und Wissen» comes with the first bookings), and
  the chip of the object open stands on the first message about it, not again on each next one.
- Hand booking without an AI (#382): a payment whose text names the number of her own invoice in
  the app («Rechnung 2026-031») opens with «Deine Rechnung 2026-031 gefunden» and proposes from
  that invoice, even when its amount is not read. The form proposes a VAT code only for what the
  invoice proves: a Swiss rate it shows gives that rate (a mixed invoice a line per rate), a place
  of supply outside Switzerland and Liechtenstein after «Leistungsort», the country written out in
  full as the place itself («Leistungsort Deutschland», «Konstanz, Deutschland»), gives V0-E; on
  the net tax rate method F1 only where it proves a domestic sale. A bare label, a place at home,
