  a street or other words around a country, «inkl. MwSt.», a foreign VAT or a statement without a
  place propose no code, and
  the form asks «War das ein Verkauf mit MWST?»; her answer there books the lines and sets the
  code exactly as the question card's answer does, with «Immer so?» after it. «Verknüpfen und
  buchen» links the invoice and books in one click (also when the run linked it already and its VAT
  is unclear: the answer is taken, the link kept; with several invoices only a line without a code
  is asked about); across periods on the agreed basis it is only
  linked. Revenue goes up to what is open of the invoice, the rest is a line of its own; an
  instalment reads the VAT off the invoice the first payment holds. Before, such a payment opened
  with V81 «keine Rechnung verknüpft» and a sale abroad was booked with Swiss VAT; it never does
  now when the text names an invoice of hers. Money from a foreign IBAN or in a foreign currency
  without a document is asked too. The amount field stays readable beside a proposal line (it had
  shrunk to two characters). A wage follows her last booking of it, else the wage account without
  a VAT code (setup.yaml `wages`; none where the books carry wages with deductions); «Spesen»
  lists Bankspesen first and «Farbe» finds the material account; the account list puts first what
  the linked receipt's lines name. A payment out with its receipt linked proposes the account of
  her own last booking of that business (a rule's too) with the receipt's rate, and says so («wie
  deine letzte Buchung bei … am …»); it is booked only by her click. A hand task says «keine Regel
  der Firma passte auf diese Zahlung».
- Files dropped on Kontoauszüge go through «Dateien hinzufügen» as on any page (#361): every file
  gets its row, the statement under «Kontoauszüge» with «Auszug öffnen» and the closing sentence on
  its payments, and closing the window opens that statement. Before, the statement was read in
  around the dialog («Kontoauszüge 0», no sentence) and photos vanished without a word.
  «Auszug hinzufügen» and a missing month's field hand any other file to the dialog too. A drop
  while the app is still loading waits until it can be taken, offline or in audit mode it says
  that nothing was taken; before, it did nothing, or the browser opened the file.
- The rows of a drop say what Belege says (#361): a photo the AI finds no receipt at all (a holiday
  photo) reads «kein Beleg» with what it shows, with «Ist doch ein Beleg», never a receipt of
  CHF 0.00 (an order page, a delivery note, a customs assessment or a card slip stays a receipt; in
  doubt the AI reads it as one); a photo of an invoice already in the books asks «schon da?» with
  «ist dasselbe» and «verschieden»; a receipt whose date cannot be read waits «fehlt: Datum» and
  gets no date, in its name neither (the AI no longer dates it by the day it was read, and an
  empty answer no longer erases a date code read). The closing sentence counts as the rows do:
  read text PDFs are read also without an AI, a receipt without date or amount waits for you, a
  photo no AI read waits for one («Fotos und Scans liest nur eine KI», with «KI einrichten»).
  «Ist ein Kontoauszug» is offered on files that could be one, no longer on a receipt linked to its
  payment.
- Files set aside as no receipt (a brochure, a holiday photo, one a person marked so) are now
  archived under the name they came with; the AI no longer names them (#361).
- Mac app, first start after «Move» to Applications: the first screen comes by itself once you
  allow the Documents folder. Before, the moved app showed no window, a click on the Dock icon a
  blank one, until it was closed and opened again: the app read the Documents folder on its main
  thread while macOS asked, and the page's first load then failed with nothing loading it again.
  The folder is now read in the background, and a page load that fails is tried again; one that
  never loads says so (#363). On the first screen, the start page and the top bar, a control under
  the window's drag strip (the language switch) takes its click again. The top bar, folded to its
  pill, no longer covers the page heading. From the start page, «Neue Buchhaltung anlegen» goes on
  to the form (or the save panel, with an AI) without a second click, and the form's «Sprache der
  App» is the language the app is in.
- The Swiss chart's names with a comma read whole: «Telefon, Internet» was «Telefon», and so 13
  accounts (1093, 1520, 1700, 4040, 5700, 6100, 6360, 6500, 6510, 6520, 6540, 8500, 8510) since
  before 0.4.0. New books get the whole names; existing books keep the names their chart has (it is
  theirs: no migration; their account hints still work), rename them under Firma › Kontenplan if you like. The chart's loader
  refuses a key it does not know, so a name cut at a comma fails at once (#365).
- Mac app, first screen: «Neue Buchhaltung erstellen» opens the system's save panel again (the
  name prefilled in Documents, «Erstellen»), and the setup starts in the folder you name. In
  0.4.0 and 0.4.1 the button did nothing: the main process had no answer for the page's call
  (#339). A call the desktop app does not answer now says so in a sentence («Das Fenster zum
  Auswählen des Ordners liess sich nicht öffnen …», «Die Datei liess sich nicht speichern …»)
  under the button, in the form, or as a toast for an invoice's PDF, instead of nothing. A
  folder named there that already holds something is not continued in silently: the app says
  whether it holds books («Diese Buchhaltung öffnen»), a started setup («Diese Einrichtung
  fortsetzen») or other files (choose another name), and writes nothing into it.
- Mac app, start: after «Verschieben» into the Applications folder the copy that was moved
  starts nothing more (it went on to start its server and window while it quit). A click on
  the Dock icon shows the window also when it exists but did not appear within four seconds
  (or its page failed to load), not only when it was closed, and is heard from the first moment
  of the start. «Nicht erlauben» on macOS's question about the Documents folder no longer ends the
  start without a window; a start that fails says so and quits (#339).
- «Zur Startseite» on a fresh install stays on the start screen instead of falling back into
  «Einrichtung».
- The drop window's bar moves while the files are read. It counted only files at rest, and the
  files of one drop share a run, so it stood at «6 % · 1 von 17» and jumped to 100 %. It now adds
  the steps the run has behind it and the files its step has finished.

- After the first statement, wages no longer wait for a receipt (#340). A payment whose own text is a wage's («Lohn Juli 2026», «Lohnzahlung 09/2026», Salär, Gehalt, salaire, stipendio, salary: the word at the start of the text or followed by a month, a period or a year) is booked without waiting on a firm whose setup says it has staff or an employed owner. The counterparty's name is never read, and a text that also names an invoice, a subscription, a licence, bookkeeping, a firm's legal form, social insurance or tax still waits. A wage is freed from the wait only: a receipt that matches it is still paired, and the payment is then booked again from it. For existing books with a profile that names employees, open-period payments worded so that waited for a receipt are booked on the next run; filed periods, firms without employees and books without a profile are unchanged. The bank's own fee row is known by its code for charges or the bank's whole name, as before, and now never under a card payment's code; the persona test's statement had no bank transaction codes, which no bank's export lacks, and carries them now. «Braucht keinen Beleg» on a payment without a booking goes on to book it in the same click and says what came of it, with «Rückgängig» for the mark in the same message; where the app could not book it by itself, the payment says so («nicht gebucht: die App konnte sie nicht selbst buchen. Unten von Hand buchen.») instead of «kein Beleg nötig · nicht gebucht».
- After «Los geht's» the AI sidebar is the ordinary one, at its ordinary width, also after a
  restart. It no longer opens as «Einrichtung», and a conversation of a finished setup no longer
  answers with the setup's topics (#342).
- Beside the wide setup conversation the page is no longer cut on a 1440 or 1280 px window: the
  setup leaves the page 820 px before it grows, the three ways into Belege wrap in a narrow list,
  a statement's notes stand one under the other, the top bar no longer lies under the «für dich»
  button, and the line under «Einrichtung» wraps instead of ending in «…» (#342).

- The KI column and the setup conversation («Mit mir einrichten») no longer answer with Claude
  Code's own «Not logged in · Please run /login», once as the answer and once as the error. A CLI
  that is not signed in, not installed, at its plan's limit or switched off answers with the app's
  sentence for that state and «KI einrichten» (#344). A booking or reading run on a Claude Code
  that is not signed in says so in the same words, as on Codex.

### Changed

- The setup asks only what the app cannot know (#345). In the books it no longer asks the firm,
  the VAT setup and what you sell again (the form or the conversation asked them a minute ago):
  it shows them once, with «Etwas ändern». It no longer asks from when to book, which returns
  were filed, the old program, how its books were kept and who has its login, and the list «Was
  der Umzug braucht» is gone: none of it led to anything the app does. A bank account with its
  IBAN and books without bookings are not walked through. A firm set up by the form reaches the
  summary in two clicks (eleven before). The summary lists each fact once, calls a topic left
  with «Später» «später», and ends with the first step as a link: «Kontoauszug einlesen».
- The count in the setup moves with every answer («3 von 5 besprochen»), and the conversation
  before the books shows «n von m beantwortet». The greeting names no one. «Später» on the
  opening says what it means and where it comes back.
- Typed setup before the books: a firm name and owner given before the UID are kept, so the UID
  alone completes the firm; the AI is told what is answered and no longer asks the next question
  itself (the app shows it with its buttons); a trade is the preset «Handwerk, Bau», and material
  charged with the work is sold too (account 4000 in the chart); answers from your words survive
  the kind of firm picked after them. Half-yearly returns on the effective method (quarterly on
  the Saldosteuersatz) are not taken from loose words: the app says what is usual.
- Setup questions say the matter in plain words first, the term after it (Kontokorrent,
  Eigenverbrauch, Durchlaufkonto, Standardkonten, Eröffnungssaldi); three of them have «Weiss
  nicht» with the answer the app then takes.
- MWST: a period that ended before anything in the books is dated reads «Keine Zahlungen oder
  Buchungen in dieser Periode · die Bücher beginnen am …», no longer «offen · fällig seit …» in
  the warning colour; «Abrechnung erstellen» stays. Its result of 0.00 says «Keine Steuer
  geschuldet: nichts zu überweisen.» without «Die Abrechnung ist trotzdem einzureichen», and
  Automatisierung's last stage reads «vor Beginn dieser Bücher» for it instead of «überfällig seit
  …». One rule decides it for MWST, Automatisierung and the link to an earlier return: nothing is
  filed in these books and nothing in them is dated on or before the period's end.

## [0.4.1] - 2026-11-12

Two fixes for the first run of the Mac app and a dropped statement.

### Fixed

- «Einrichtung» and the setup conversation before the books follow the newest turn as a chat
  does: once the Claude Code check answers, the folder step's answers («Von anderer Software
  übernehmen», «Erst die Demo ansehen», «Ohne KI einrichten») scroll into sight above the input
  box instead of lying under it, where a click landed in the box (Mac app window 1440 × 900).
  Smooth only without reduced motion.
- A bank statement dropped on Kontoauszüge opens again when it lands in another quarter. Since a month without a statement shows as «fehlt», the page selected the first such row of the new quarter (in November: October's gap) before the books with the new statement had loaded, and the statement just read was not on screen. A list now moves its selection to the first row only when its rows change under a selection that stayed (a filter, a search), never when a link changes both at once.

## [0.4.0] - 2026-11-12

The app learns the company and takes more of the quarter off you. Receipts and payments match
in either order, several receipts per payment and a part per payee of a collective payment; a
payment that needs a receipt waits for it instead of being booked without. Booking rules come
from a wish in your own words or from a correction («Ins Wissen übernehmen?»), are previewed on
a card before you keep them, and are listed under Firma › Regeln. New areas Geschäftspartner and
Wissen; the AI setup on a fresh Mac, with Claude Code or Codex; statements as PDF, scan or photo
for any bank; payment providers (a shop, a card terminal) over a clearing account; VAT on the
agreed basis, the net tax rate method and firms without VAT registration. Entries marked
**⚠ action** need a step from you, chiefly the clearing account of a payment provider and the
phone's pairing link under `npm start`; entries marked **⚠ figures** change figures of an open
quarter. No filed figure moves. `npm run update` moves the books into the new workspace layout
and migrates them by itself; commit the result in the company's repository.

### Changed

- A page whose link names no period now opens on the quarter of the newest payment, receipt or booking, not on the oldest quarter not filed yet. Rechnungen also counts its newest issued invoice. Before, in November the demo opened on Q2 and October's files seemed missing. Files you drop move the page to where they landed (a statement's last booking day, a receipt's date once read), and an invoice you issue is shown in its own period, so it no longer vanishes from a list of another quarter. A receipt you open while it is still being read stays selected and opens in its own period once its date is read; before, the selection jumped to another receipt. MWST says beside the state when an earlier return is not filed yet («Q2 2026 nicht eingereicht · fällig seit 31.08.2026»), a link to it.
- The period picker applies a quarter, a year or another preset with one click; «Anwenden» is left for «Benutzerdefiniert», whose calendar opens only when you choose it.

### Security

- The API of `npm start` answers this computer only under a loopback name: `localhost`, `127.0.0.1` or `[::1]`, on any port. Before, a web page of any site open in your browser could point its own name at 127.0.0.1 (DNS rebinding) and then read and change the books through the browser, since the server only looked at where a connection came from, and that browser is on this computer. A request from this computer under any other name is now treated as from the network: the books, receipts and every write are refused (403, `foreignHost`, «Nur unter localhost, 127.0.0.1 oder [::1] erreichbar, nicht unter einem anderen Namen.»), and the phone's paths answer as they answer the network, the upload and status only with the pairing token once one is set. The phone app on the local network is unchanged: it reaches the upload and status under the Mac's network address, with its token. The Vite dev server (`npm run dev`) forwards your browser's `localhost` name and checks names itself. The Mac app had this check already; its refusal now carries the same code.

### Fixed

- A payment provider's first report proposes as the cut-over day the first day the report covers, within the open period, no longer the open period's first day. Accepting the old proposal for an October report moved the payouts of the months before onto the clearing account.
- A credit note for part of an invoice, an amount with the VAT included, now comes to exactly that amount. Before, the app turned the amount into a net price rounded to the Rappen and added the VAT again, so at 8.1 % it usually came to a Rappen less or more, and no net price gave the amount asked. The line now keeps the amount with the VAT included: the VAT is derived from it, the net is the rest, and the sheet, the QR-bill and the booking show the same figures. An invoice line can be priced the same way: pick «8.1 % inkl.» as its rate. Lines priced net are computed as before. invoices.json is now schema 2, so an older app refuses it instead of reading such a price as net.
- A wish that becomes a rule without a VAT rate («Parkhaus immer auf Reisespesen 6640») can now be kept. The app takes the rate from the receipts linked to the rule's payments: when they all show the same Swiss VAT rate, the rule books at that rate, and when none shows Swiss VAT, at the zero rate. When the receipts show different rates, or no receipt is linked yet, the rule's card asks once, «Mit welcher MWST bucht diese Regel?», with a button per rate and «Keine Schweizer MWST». The same holds for a rule made in Firma › Regeln › «Neue Regel» with the VAT field left empty (its placeholder now reads «aus den Belegen»); before, an empty field booked without VAT even when the receipts showed 8.1 %. A firm without input tax books such a rule without a code. Before, the card refused the rule with «MWST-Code «» fehlt oder gibt es nicht (none für ohne MWST)» and offered no «Behalten». The new rule card in the AI sidebar now also scrolls into view above the input field when it appears. Before, it could sit half under the input.
- **⚠ figures** Import VAT on a courier's customs bill comes back as input tax when the customs office's assessment (Veranlagungsverfügung MWST, eVV) is among the documents, also when the assessment prints its tax as a table without the label «Gesamtbetrag MWST». The app now reads such an assessment by its rows (each tax that is a base times a rate it states), links it to the courier's payment by the amount, and books the deduction -/4030 with the code of the assessment's rate (M26-2 at 2.6 %, M81-2 at 8.1 % or when it states several rates), Ziffer 400. Before, the assessment was filed as a document without a total, the import VAT stayed a cost, and linking it by hand was refused (`importVatUnproven`). The coffee demo's chart has the import accounts 4020, 4030 and 4040. An open quarter with such an assessment gains its input tax; filed quarters stay as filed.

- A quantity or price typed quickly on an invoice draft's sheet is saved as typed. Before, a number field selected its old value only one frame after you entered it, so digits typed in that moment landed beside the old value: quantity 12 became 121 or 2, a price became ten times what was typed. Now the first key typed into a number field replaces its value whenever it comes.
- «Nach Beleg buchen» no longer offers a rate for import VAT. On a courier's or customs payment, the line on the import VAT account (4030) without a code got the receipt's rate (M26) with one click, in the journal and in the return's review, which claimed 2.6 % of the import VAT as input tax instead of the whole tax the customs assessment proves. That line now keeps its zero code, as the customs logic books it: its input tax comes only from the one-line deduction (-/4030 M81-2) once the assessment is linked.
- Export: a preset keeps the period you picked. Choosing «1. Semester» and then «Prüfung ESTV / AHV» exported the whole year (55 receipts instead of 17); now the preset changes only the parts and options, and brings its own period only while you have picked none. On a half-yearly firm the period row offers «1. Semester» and «2. Semester» beside the quarters. Enter in a date field of the period picker applies the range, like «Anwenden»; before, it only moved the calendar.

- A collective payment (Sammelzahlung) whose one part a rule or code booked and whose other part the model booked is now saved whole. Before, the inbox run saved the code's part after its first round, and then dropped the model's part, since the payment already counted as booked: it stayed booked for that part's amount only, with no task and no question. Now the parts the model is asked about are booked together with the code's parts once every part has its lines; when the model leaves a part without lines, no part is booked, the payment stays open for the next run and in Aufgaben, and the run reports it as failed.
- **⚠ action** Security: under `npm start` without `VANILLA_PHONE_TOKEN`, anyone on the same network could upload files into the inbox (`POST /api/upload`) and query `/api/status`. Now the server makes a new random pairing token at every start, the terminal prints the pairing link with it for each network address, and a request from another device to those two paths without it is refused (401 `phoneToken`). The books, receipts and every other path were already refused from the network (403 `localOnly`) and still are; requests from this computer need no token, as before. Action: a phone paired with `npm start` scans the new link after each restart, unless `VANILLA_PHONE_TOKEN` in `.env` keeps one code. The Mac app is unchanged.

- A bank statement reads «Stimmt» only when its payments add up to the bank's closing balance and the books show that balance too. Before, «Stimmt» stood beside a books balance that differed from the bank's close. Now the statement says how far the books are off, and when the payments without a booking make up the difference, it says so.
- A debit that a camt.053 statement books to the bank that keeps the account (its own name as the other party, no account number) is the bank's fee row, under any transaction code, and books at once without a receipt. Before, only the codes for charges marked it, so an account fee booked under a payment's code waited for a receipt.
- Plainer words in Bank: «Braucht keinen Beleg» instead of «braucht keinen», «ohne Buchung» instead of «ungebucht», and «die App bucht sie gerade» instead of «der Lauf bucht sie».
- A late receipt that shows Swiss VAT, linked to a payment already booked without input tax, now says so on the receipt's page beside its payment («Der Beleg zeigt 8.1 % MWST, die Buchung steht auf … I0») with «Nach Beleg buchen». A booking a person made, or one booked before the app recorded the receipts it saw, is never booked again by the app, and before only the booking's own page showed the offer. A booking a person made «Ohne Vorsteuer» while no receipt was linked now also offers the click when the receipt arrives; before it read «So gewählt … die Buchung bleibt», as if she had decided against the receipt.
- Opening the books no longer leaves `vanilla-accounting/AGENTS.md` and `vanilla-accounting/CLAUDE.md` untracked in the company's repository. The app writes them for an AI opened in the terminal and renews its section on each update; it now commits them as its own save (shown in Änderungen), also the two files an earlier version left untracked. A file you are still editing yourself gets the app's lines and stays for you to save.
- An undone edit stays undone. After ⌘Z (or «Rückgängig» in the toast, or a change in another tab) an open form now shows what the books hold: a booking's form, a receipt's details and VAT, a payment's note. Before, a booking's account field kept the undone account, and focusing it and leaving it booked that account again as your change. A field you changed and have not saved yet keeps what you typed, and leaving a field saves only what you changed. Likewise the year close's receivables entry: «Konto 1100 anlegen» fills its empty Soll at once, no longer only after closing and opening the form again.
- PDFs (invoices, reports, the export with PDFs) keep working under `npm start` after the browser that prints them ends. An installed Google Chrome was seen ending 12 to 18 s after its start, and every later PDF failed with «Connection closed.» until the server restarted. The next PDF now starts the browser again, once; when that start fails too, the error names the browser and what to do. The app also picks a steadier browser first: CHROME_PATH, then Chrome for Testing from Playwright's cache, then an installed Chrome, Edge, Brave or Chromium (the same order the browser tests use). The Mac app prints with its own Chromium and is unchanged.
- «Auszug entfernen» and its «Rückgängig» no longer lose statement balances. Removing a statement read every statement and balance of the books again from the bank-statements folder, so balances read from the PDFs (a filed quarter's included) were replaced, and the undo, which read the file in again, could not bring them back: the month then said «Der Endsaldo ist aus dem PDF nicht gelesen.» and the next month «Der Anfangssaldo fehlt». Now the removal takes only the statement, its balances and the payments it alone brought, and «Rückgängig» puts the file and every record back exactly as it was. A statement of a filed month is not removed any more, and a camt.053 read in later no longer replaces, adds or drops a balance of a filed period.
- A credit note that a refund of its exact amount pays back near its date no longer blocks the receipts of a marketplace's other charges. Before, when the model doubted that refund pair, the free credit note let one more combination of the vendor's invoices add up to another charge, that charge had two fitting sets, and the app linked none of the sets that shared its invoices. Now such a credit note takes part in no set (it stays for its refund and the person), and each charge gets the invoices that add up to it.
- A payment with two or more receipts linked that add up to less than it paid (by more than a centime per receipt, in its currency) now waits for the missing receipts like a payment without any, and is listed with them. Before, it was neither booked nor waiting: the model's booking of the part the receipts prove did not balance and was refused.
- The receipt match no longer adds a receipt to a payment whose receipts already add up to it. Before, for a vendor that groups several receipts into one payment (a marketplace), the model could link a receipt of the vendor's other charge to a payment its two receipts paid exactly: that payment then had receipts worth more than it paid, and the other charge waited for a receipt it had lost. Now a link that would make a payment's receipts in its currency add up to more than it paid, by more than a centime per receipt, is not made, and the receipt stays free for its own payment. A single receipt above its payment (instalments, a part payment), receipts in another currency, a credit note in the set and a receipt linked to several payments (a card charge split across balances) are not compared. You can still link such a receipt by hand: the payment then shows «Belege CHF … über der Zahlung» beside its receipts and is booked as usual.
- A card payment shown as «Post Billingonline Zollikofen» (Swiss Post's online label) now belongs to the business Swiss Post.
- An invoice in CHF paid from a EUR or USD account books the input tax the invoice states. Before, the app converted the payment at the ESTV daily rate and took 8.1 % out of that, so the tax grew with the exchange difference and a transfer fee inside the payment. Now the payment's line keeps its account and the ESTV rate without a rate code, and a single-leg line «M81-2» credits that account by the invoice's tax, in CHF. This applies when every linked invoice is in CHF, read, with Swiss VAT, and its total lies within 5 % of the converted payment (farther off, the payment pays only a part and keeps the gross code). An invoice in the payment's currency is converted as before, and so is a part of a collective payment, whose invoices are the whole payment's. Payments booked already stay as they are; only new bookings, by the model or a company rule, change.
- Receipts that arrive after their bank statement now pair with a payment the app had already booked without a receipt. Before, when the model put such a payment onto a money account (the transfer account, for example), the payment counted as one that needs no receipt: the receipts that came later, even ones matching a collective payment's parts to the centime, were never linked, and the payment never got its booking with input tax. Now only a person's booking says a payment has no receipt of its own. The app's own booking stays a candidate, and once a receipt is linked the next run books the payment again from it. Dropping the statements first or the receipts first ends the same way.
- A collective payment (one bank booking with a part per payee, each with its own name, IBAN and amount) is now paired with a receipt for each part. Before, only one receipt could pair with it, or none, and the whole payment was booked without a receipt. Each part is now a candidate of its own: a receipt pairs the part of its amount, by the part's payee, IBAN and reference, and a part a receipt holds takes no second one. The payment stays one transaction with every part's receipt linked to it. It is booked with one line per part, each from its own receipt, by a rule, the customs invoice or the model, as a single payment would be. The parts without a receipt go to the model together, as before. Parts nothing tells apart (one payee, one amount) take receipts that nothing tells apart one to one, as alike payments do, and fewer such receipts than parts are linked all the same.
- A courier's invoice that prints the import VAT it advanced only as «MWST.» at no rate («Kosten …, Total MWST.-frei») and cites the customs declaration's number is read as a customs invoice: code books its fee with the courier's VAT on 4040 and the import VAT on 4030 without input tax, and the customs office's assessment of that number brings the deduction (– / 4030 M81-2), linked or not. The AI and a booking rule can no longer put a rate code on the import VAT account (4030 M81 claimed 8.1 % on the import VAT itself): the code is left off and the booking says why, as the AI's changes in the sidebar were refused already. An import VAT line the AI booked on a courier invoice code cannot read gets its deduction from an unlinked assessment of the same tax whose declaration number the invoice cites.
- Receipts and payments that nothing tells apart are paired. Two card charges of one amount, one day and one shop, with two receipts of that amount and day from that shop, used to wait for a receipt forever: each receipt had two equal candidates, so neither pair was clear, and the model declined to choose. When n receipts and n payments fit each other alike (the same amount, the same days, the same name, and no other candidate close), the matching chain now pairs them one to one, the receipts by id against the payments by ref, as clear pairs the model checks like any other. Which receipt goes with which payment changes no booking. Unequal counts, or candidates that differ in their day or name, still go to the model or to you.
- «Ausstellen» on an invoice or a credit note now shows its PDF right away, in a window with «PDF herunterladen», «Per E-Mail senden» (once e-mail is set up) and «Später». Before, the document sat behind «Senden», and an invoice issued but never downloaded or sent there looked ready while nothing had gone out. «Senden» shows the PDF too, and the plain download on an issued invoice is labelled «PDF herunterladen». When the browser that prints the PDF fails (it will not start, or ends mid-print), issuing now says so in plain words, and the invoice stays a draft with its number not taken. Before, the browser's own error text was shown.
### Added

- An account the chart lacks can be added from the account field itself (a booking's Soll and Haben, a new entry, a payment's booking, the batch and the drop row), no longer only under Firma › Kontenplan. Typing a number or a name lists, after the chart's own accounts, the accounts of the Swiss KMU chart the books lack, each with its number and name («6600 Werbeaufwand anlegen»); picking one adds it under that name, as Firma › Kontenplan adds an account (`PUT /api/accounts/<number>`), and puts it in the field. Only accounts of the standard chart are offered (`GET /api/accounts/standard`), one of some legal forms only (2850 Privat of an Einzelfirma) only when the firm's legal form is one of them; any other number still goes through Firma › Kontenplan. A list cut at 60 entries says how many it left out. A standard account typed and left without picking it is not booked; the form says which entry of the list adds it. The list stays open while the account is added, so a click on the entry no longer lets the form save the typed number first, and a new entry or correction booked right after adding its account no longer refuses it as unknown.
- A payment provider's report asks its first questions with an answer filled in. The clearing account is proposed and named after the provider («1093 Durchlaufkonto Shopify», the next free number of its ten when another provider's file names 1093) and is added to the chart when the answer is kept, not when the answer is refused; the revenue and fee accounts are proposed from the setup's defaults (the fee code I0 where the firm's form takes it). The questions no longer name the file the answer is kept in. Once one report's answer is kept, the other rows of the same provider ask again, so the fee report no longer asks for the clearing account just given.
- VAT adjustments (`config.vatAdjustments`) in the app: Firma › MWST › Nachdeklarationen lists them and lets a person enter, change and remove one, no longer only by editing ledger.json. A line names its open period (dated on the period's last day), an input-tax Ziffer, a signed amount, the filed entries it refers to and a reason; it is golden rule 1's case of a VAT event of the open period on a filed entry, such as import VAT refunded now on a filed import. `POST`, `PUT` and `DELETE /api/vat-adjustments` refuse a line of a closed period, before or after the change (403, `periodClosed`), and a line that names no filed entry before its period, an unknown entry, a Ziffer the form does not take for it, no amount or no reason (400, `vatAdjustment`). The MWST page shows the line under its Ziffer with links to Firma › MWST and to the entries. The stored shape is unchanged.
- **⚠ action** A payment provider's monthly fee bill books the input tax it shows. The provider keeps its fees from the payouts, so no payment carries them; its bill (Shopify's «Payments Invoice») shows the Swiss VAT on those fees. The inbox's run now books that VAT once per bill: one entry without a payment, empty debit, the company's fee account (`provider.fees.account`) in credit, the code of the tax itself, in the bill's currency at the ESTV daily rate of its day, and the bill linked to it. A correction you booked by hand already gets the bill linked instead. A bill of a filed period, one without the provider's Swiss VAT, or one whose fee account is missing stays with you, and the run log says why. Deleting the booking takes the bill apart from it, so the run never books it again. A business file says which of its documents is such a bill (`documents:` with `proves: vat-correction`); the app's Shopify file now names the Payments Invoice. Action: the code follows the fee account (I81-2 on an operating expense account, M81-2 on cost of goods); to book another, name it in the company's file of the provider, for example `fees: { account: "6580", vat: I0, billVat: M81-2 }`. Until now this entry was booked by hand each month, so Ziffer 400 (or 405) of the open quarter grows by the bills' VAT once the run has seen them.
- A booking rule books a private share as own use (Eigenverbrauch) when it says `private: { share: 50, as: own-use }`: the whole payment on its account with its input tax (6510 I81), and with it the share's input-tax correction owner's account / that account with the private-use code at the line's rate (2451 / 6510 K81-B, Ziffer 415; a refund takes it back with -K81-B). The tax owed is the same as with `private: 50`; the return shows the whole input tax in 405 and the correction in 415, as an owner books a subscription used half privately by hand. The correction is a line of the payment's own booking, so it is booked once and goes with it; only a line that claims input tax gets one (no receipt with Swiss VAT: the zero code, nothing to correct). A correction a person booked by hand on a ref of its own (the payment's day, owner's account / the rule's account, that code) stands: the rule books none beside it, and the rule's card counts such payments as booked as it would book them. A rule whose code claims no input tax (`none`, `0`, Bezugsteuer) is refused with `as: own-use`, and so on the net tax rate method and without VAT registration. «Neue Regel» in Firma › Regeln now takes a private share («Privat (%)») and, on a firm with input tax, «als Eigenverbrauch»; «Immer so?» offers the rule after a payment booked whole and corrected as own use, also from the correction's own ref. The card writes a correction line with both its accounts. No figure of an existing booking changes; the books' format is unchanged.
- «Kundenzahlung ohne Rechnung» has a fourth answer, «Ausland mit ausländischer MWST …», for a sale abroad that carried a foreign VAT (OSS, IOSS or a registration abroad): you type the foreign rate or the tax amount (each fills the other) and the account it goes onto, and the answer books the net as an export at V0 and the tax onto that account without a code, as a shop's sales report books such an order (#206), never the gross at V0. The account is proposed from your last such answer, else from the `provider.foreignTax` of your payment providers' files; with none or several you name it once. A rate or amount the sale cannot hold, or an account the chart lacks, is refused with its reason and the question stays open. «Zurücknehmen» deletes the tax line and puts the gross back. «Immer so?» keeps the split in the payer's rule (`then: { account, vat: 0, foreignTax: { rate: 19, account: "…" } }`), which books the payer's next payment so; a rule's `foreignTax` goes only beside a zero code and never with a private share.
- A statement PDF kept as its month's statement gives the books its closing balance and the references and foreign amounts its bank export lacks, for any bank (issue #243): from its table, which the AI reads once and keeps beside the PDF (`<name>.read.json`), or which a person fills in on the statement («Saldo und Referenzen von Hand erfassen», prefilled with the statement's payments and the balance the bank states for its last day; «Korrigieren» on a read). With no such balance the closing field stays empty: a table saved without one gives the books its references and no balance, never the books' own figure as the bank's. A question of the PDF that waits (#254) is its one table, and closing the question keeps that table. The inbox's run reads one month's worth by itself, at most one PDF of each account; a larger backlog waits for «N Kontoauszüge von der KI lesen lassen?» above Kontoauszüge (or `npm run read-statements`), which reads one after the other, shows N of M and can be cancelled. Without AI the list says how many stay unread and why. Filed months are not read. A reference or foreign amount goes only onto the payment a line is beyond doubt (same day, direction and amount, then the counterparty's words), never onto a filed payment; the statement says how many lines it left out. A call that fails keeps nothing, is named, and is asked again on the next run; so does an empty answer or one cut off before its end. Only a person's table ties a line to a payment, never the AI's answer. «Neu lesen» first names what goes (the table and the references it gave the books) and, for a table filled by hand, takes it away only after «Ja, neu lesen». A balance the books hold from a file stays until that file's table gives another; a camt.053's balance of the same day stands over a table's, read or typed (only a year-end confirmation stands against it); a filed month's balance never changes. `npm run extract-balances` and the readers of two banks' layouts are gone.
- A statement PDF of an account that has a camt.053 or a bank CSV is kept as its statement without a question (issue #254), whichever comes first and in any session: the decision comes from the statement files in the books, not from the drop. The PDF is read only when its own words do not name the account and its days. A PDF of an account without either is kept as its statement at once (named by the days it shows), and the question whether its movements are read in now waits in the books with the table as read: in the drop row, and under Kontoauszüge on that statement after the dialog closes or the app restarts («Die Bewegungen dieses PDFs sind noch nicht eingelesen …», «Einlesen» or «Als Monatsauszug behalten»). The account's first camt.053 or CSV closes it as kept. That first camt.053 or CSV after movements were read from PDFs counts no payment twice: a read movement of the same day and amount is its payment, under the read's ref with the bank's references and IBANs, its bookings and receipt links kept; between two equal amounts of one day the payee decides (its IBAN, its name, the text), never the order in the file. Every other read movement on the days the bank file covers goes into one reconciliation of the account, shown on its statements: «Abgleich: bisher aus PDFs gelesen ↔ jetzt vom Bank-Export», a row per read movement with «Gleich wie …» one of the bank's movements or «Eigene Zahlung». A read movement on another day within seven days of a bank movement (a value date read for the booking day, a PDF line of 29 September and a camt.053 from 1 October) takes part only as a candidate: «Gleich wie …» or «Nicht in diesem Export», never «Eigene Zahlung»; left apart, it stays as it is, and the export that covers its day decides it in a reconciliation of its own. Only the bank movements within seven days of those read movements take part, open or filed, so a PDF read months later holds no month between; a bank movement left unpaired stays free for a movement read later. A pair of opposite directions or two currencies is refused. Code proposes only pairs beyond doubt (the same day and amount, the same amount within seven days, a payee's movement a few Rappen off) and never two payees that differ; every other row starts at «— wählen —» and «Abgleich bestätigen» applies all at once. Until then no run books any of its movements («Automatisch buchen» answers that the reconciliation waits) and the filing, the seal and the year close refuse a period that holds its days, naming a movement whose statement shows it. A pair of an open read movement leaves the bank's movement with the read's bookings, receipt links and invoice payment (for another amount the app's booking goes, the invoice is matched again and the bank's amount is booked once; a person's booking of the other amount is hers to change first). A filed read movement never changes: its bank twin is marked as booked in the filed quarter (named on it, «Trotzdem buchen» takes the mark) and only a difference of a few Rappen is booked on its day, without VAT, to an account the person names, the year end's exchange-difference account proposed only when the chart has it. Not booked there, the bank's movement is simply booked, and the year close counts the filed read movement as booked once its twin is. A movement paired, or named a payment of its own, takes part in no other reconciliation: the next import asks nothing again, and the read statement file is never rewritten. A bank file holding a movement that a confirmed reconciliation set in a read movement's place is not taken back, neither by «Auszug entfernen» nor by «Rückgängig»: the refusal names the movements, since their bookings and receipt links would go and the read movement would not come back. The twin of a filed read movement that books nothing of its own may go: the read movement stays booked, and the bank file read again is reconciled afresh. A reconciliation with a movement of a filed day (a bank movement of 28 September and a PDF read for October) is confirmed like any other; the filed movement stays as filed. A filed bank movement without a booking is never proposed for an open read movement and such a pair is refused: the read movement would go with its booking and receipt links, and the filed one could never be booked. The read movement stays a payment of its own. Confirming writes the invoices only once the books are saved. A PDF read later with «Bewegungen aus dem PDF einlesen» next to the days a camt.053 covers goes the same way. Nothing of a filed period changes: a camt.053 that would add a payment there is still refused. Closing a period (the filing's close, `npm run seal -- <q> --close`, the year close) closes the statement questions of its days with the PDF kept as the statement (the year close saves that before the year closes, so its own save is not refused), and «Als Auszug behalten» works on a filed one: no question is left that nothing could answer. Every statement PDF has «Aktionen» (on its statement, and on its drop row) with «Bewegungen aus dem PDF einlesen»: the AI reads it, or the form by hand when the AI is off, with the same duplicate guard; on an account with a camt.053 it names the days the account has already, and imports only the other days. A PDF statement that brought no movement says so as a fact («Juli 2026: keine Bewegungen eingelesen.») with «Bewegungen aus dem PDF lesen». In the table, an empty form (no movement, both balances 0.00) no longer shows the green «Geht auf» and is not read in, and the hand form says why there is no AI read: the AI switched off, no AI set up, the AI's answer was no statement, or the call failed with the provider's words. A statement still asking in the drop dialog for its account or its columns goes to Belege › Quarantäne when the dialog closes, instead of being lost. **Format:** the books go to schema 7: they keep a statement PDF's open question (`asks.jsonl` of its year), each account's reconciliation (`reconcile.jsonl` of the year of its last day, with the days its bank files cover, `covered`, and the movements named payments of their own, `own`), and mark a bank movement paired with a read one booked in a filed quarter (`waitsForPerson` `filedTwin` with the `read` movement). An older app would drop the questions and reconciliations on its next save, so it refuses the new books. No record changes, and every seal stays as it was. `npm run update` writes the new number, with a backup in `data/backups/`.
- VAT codes in plain words (issue #224). Wherever the app shows a code, it names it first and puts the code small beside it: «Material 8.1 % (MWST inbegriffen) · M81», «Material 8.1 %, nur die Steuer · M81-2», «Rückerstattung Verkauf 8.1 % · -V81», «Bezugsteuer Dienstleistung Ausland 8.1 % (Betrag ohne MWST) · B81-1». This holds for Buchungen' rows, filter chip and detail, the bookings of the same payment, the booking form's hints, the rule cards and Firma › Regeln, the AI sidebar's change cards, the sale questions, Automatisierung's proposals, the year close, the MWST tab's entries and MWST-Codes. Every VAT field (a booking by hand, a payment's lines, a detail's «bearbeiten», a batch, «Neue Regel», a rule from a partner's notes, a year-end entry) is now a picker: it lists the codes the firm's form takes, grouped by what you are doing (Einkauf Material und Waren, Betriebsaufwand und Anschaffungen, Verkauf, Einfuhr nur die Steuer, Ausland, Korrektur und Nicht-Entgelte), current rates first, searchable by words of the name or by the code; «-» first lists the refunds. A typed rate («8.1») still lets the accounts choose the code. Hovering or focusing a code opens its card in every area, no longer only in Buchungen, MWST and MWST-Codes. The names come from the code catalogue (`vatCodeName` in src/accounting/vat-codes.ts with the texts `vatCodes.plain.*` in German, French, Italian and English), which also writes the new «Name» column of docs/vat-codes.md. Receipts and evals keep showing rates only.
- Help pages in German (issue #226), built from the app's own sources so the app and the help say the same: the VAT-code catalogue (src/accounting/vat-codes.ts with the UI texts), the quarter's steps (processes/quarter-close.flow.yaml), the VAT thresholds and periods (modules/ch), the limits the app names when one is hit, and the casebook's cases (examples/cases/) as booked. Pages: getting started, the Mac app, phone pairing, mail sources, the AI and working without it, eight common cases (courier customs bill with import VAT, refund of a sale, Bezugsteuer, card payment in a foreign currency, shop payouts over a clearing account, private share, a missing receipt, correcting a filed quarter), the VAT codes, the quarter, the kinds of firm and the limits. Every page says which app version it describes. Static HTML and one stylesheet, no script, no tracking. `npm run help:build` writes them to dist-help/, `npm run help:preview` serves them on this machine; a CI job builds them on every change. They are not published yet.
- A receipt the inbox's run still works on shows as in processing (issue #218), no longer as needing you. Belege lists it in its own group «Wird verarbeitet» with a turning ring instead of the orange dot and what the run is doing («Wird gelesen …», «Die MWST wird gelesen …», «Sucht die Zahlung …», «Bucht die Zahlung …», or «Wartet (3 vor diesem)» while its run waits behind others); «Braucht dich» and the dock's badge leave it out. Its detail says the same with «Lauf ansehen» in place of «Betrag fehlt» and «Noch keine Zahlung», and its empty fields read «wird gelesen …». You may still enter its date, amount or description: the run keeps what you entered and names the file by your date. Once the run comes to rest, a receipt that still lacks something moves to «Braucht dich»; one whose run stopped says so, with the reason and the run linked. The drop dialog's rows say the same, and Kontoauszüge says «N Zahlungen ohne Buchung: der Lauf bucht sie» (each payment «wird gebucht») while the run is open. The list loads the books again after each step of a run, so a file just read shows at once. **Format:** the books go to schema 6, because a receipt still being processed now records which of its date, amount and description you set by hand (`handSet`; the mark goes once it is filed). An older app would not know the mark and would let the run write over what you entered, so it refuses the new books. No record changes, and every seal stays as it was. `npm run update` writes the new number, with a backup in `data/backups/`.
- Statements without camt.053 (issue #217). A statement dropped as a PDF, a scan or a photo, of any bank, whose days no camt.053 of the bank covers, is read by the AI into its movements (day, value day, amount, counterparty, text, reference, and a card charge's amount in another currency where printed), its opening and closing balance and each page's carry-over; code then checks that they add up to the cent, page by page, and the drop row shows the movements as a table marked «aus PDF gelesen», each line correctable while the check reruns. «Einlesen» imports it only once it adds up, through the same path as a camt.053: the app writes the camt.053 the bank did not give beside the PDF in `bank-statements/<bank>/` (under a name no file there has; a card charge's other currency as its InstdAmt, so a receipt in that currency matches it), so account, refs, balances and matching are the camt path's. A movement the books hold already is marked «schon in den Büchern» and not added again. When the IBAN and days the AI reads show that a camt.053 of the bank covers days of the statement, none of its movements is imported (a value day read for a booking day would double a payment): the row says so and offers «Als Monatsauszug behalten». Taking back a read statement leaves every payment a camt.053 also holds in the books, with its bookings, under the camt.053's ref. Without AI (Firma › KI, named in the row) the same table is filled by hand, or the PDF kept as the month's statement as before, or the bank's CSV is dropped: the row asks which column holds what once, keeps the answer per account in `knowledge/bank-csv.yaml`, and reads the next CSV of that header by itself. Kontoauszüge › upload hands a PDF no camt.053 covers to the drop dialog, which reads it. A receipt, photo or scan that is a statement turns into one with «Ist ein Kontoauszug». The AI reads at most 20 pages of one statement and a CSV at most 5000 rows; both limits are named when hit. A statement PDF of days a camt.053 covers is kept as that month's statement exactly as before: its month-end balance and Wise's transfer references are still read by the per-bank readers (`npm run extract-balances`, src/providers/statement-pdf, src/providers/wise-pdf). Retiring those readers waits until the AI's read supplies references, original amounts and month-end balances with proven parity (issue #243).
- Belege, Buchungen and a statement's payments pick several rows at once (issue #216): ⌘-click toggles a row, ⇧-click a range, a checkbox shows on hover and stays while anything is picked, ⌘A picks the rows shown after the chips, Esc none; at most 200, and a pick past that says how many it left out. The detail column then shows the selection: how many, their sum in the books' currency, the days they span, how many a filed period holds, each row with its link. Its «Aktionen» run batches, each with a preview first and one «Rückgängig» (⌘Z) for all of it: receipts «Zahlung suchen», «Kein Beleg», «Neu lesen (KI)» (20 at a time), «Löschen»; payments «Buchen …» on an account and VAT rate (no input tax without a receipt in a batch), «Braucht keinen Beleg», «Regel daraus»; entries «Konto ändern …», «MWST-Code …» (a code with input tax only on an entry whose payment has a receipt or other proof), «Dimension …»; a change of account or code teaches as by hand. A filed object is left out and named; a batch of filed objects only is refused. The undo stays on the server and is refused, naming the objects, when one changed since the batch. While rows are picked the AI chat carries them as a chip («12 Belege ausgewählt ×») and works on exactly these; an answer with several changes is taken back in one step
- «Aktionen» on every receipt, payment and entry (issue #216), also with A and in ⌘K: only what fits it, the most likely action as the primary button beside it. A receipt whose text says it was paid in cash or privately («bar bezahlt», «Rückgeld», «privat ausgelegt», in four languages) offers «Privat bezahlt buchen» first, beside «Noch keine Zahlung» (only a statement that it was paid counts: «Bei Barzahlung 2 % Skonto», «Barzahlung nicht möglich» or a hotel's «Bar» line do not). «Von Mitarbeitenden bezahlt» books an employee's outlay on that person's account; a firm without one is asked once for the person and the account of its chart, and keeps it as a money account (a balance-sheet account in a currency the books know)
- «Löschen» for a file that is no receipt (issue #228). A quarantined file (terms, a brochure, a newsletter) gets «Löschen» beside «Ansehen» and «Ist ein Beleg», and the list «Alle in der Quarantäne löschen» with the count; a file listed as «keine Belege» gets it on its row. A confirmation names the file and says it cannot be taken back; for a file of the books, where their repository keeps a history, it says the history still holds it. A file that a payment, a booking or a bank statement refers to stays, and the refusal names what refers to it; a file of a closed period stays too. Deleted files leave data/quarantine/ or the receipts folder.

- «Verlauf» on a receipt's, a payment's and a journal entry's detail (issue #215): folded, the runs that worked on it, newest first, read from the books' run log (a run whose item is the object, or that wrote it: a payment's runs include the one that booked its entry or linked its receipt). Each row names the workflow, when, where it stands (done, waiting at a step, stuck with its reason, discarded) and what it wrote, each a link to its detail. The newest 20 are shown, with «Die neusten 20 von N Läufen. Alle zeigen» when there are more. A run opens its view in Automatisierung; a run whose details the run store no longer holds (retention, another computer) is marked «Details nicht mehr gespeichert», and its run view shows the run log's lines instead of an error. A person's own edits are not in it yet: the books record who changed an entry, not when.

- Firma › KI: «KI ganz ausschalten» runs the whole app without AI on this Mac (issue #212). No model is asked anywhere: not for reading a receipt or its VAT, booking, pairing, the KI column, the setup conversation, «Vorschlag der KI», a workflow step or Terminal's AI. Each place says the AI is off and offers its way by hand: a receipt the AI would read waits and says so, pairing finds by amount, date and name only, «Neu klassifizieren» books by your rules, the KI column's input names Firma › KI. Switched on again, one inbox run starts at once and does what waited. The switch is kept on this Mac and changes nothing in the books.
- **⚠ action** Foreign VAT on an export order no longer lands in revenue (issue #206). A sales report's export order whose tax column shows a foreign VAT (for example 19 % German VAT, collected under the EU's IOSS on a consignment of at most EUR 150) was booked as one line at V0 on the gross, so the foreign tax counted as export turnover. Now the company's file of the provider names an account for it, `provider: { foreignTax: "2201" }` (a pass-through account the IOSS return is paid from), and the report books two lines: the net at V0 onto revenue and the tax onto that account without a VAT code; a refund the same reversed, the tax in the sale's share to the cent. Without that account such an order waits in the report's row («Ausfuhr mit ausländischer MWST: das Konto dafür fehlt»), never booked gross. Action: a firm that charges foreign VAT on exports (IOSS) adds `foreignTax` to its file of the provider and drops the report again; an export the report booked before as one gross line at V0 stays as booked, filed or not (the app never books an entry again), and its refund is booked the same way, gross at -V0 without a tax share; an entry from elsewhere that booked such an export gross at V0 on its day holds it, so it is never booked a second time beside it. A sale booked as one line is known when the books carry any line of its amount under its ref (as beside a person's Storno and rebooking of it); a sale booked as two lines only when the books carry exactly those two. A refund paid in the open period of an export of a filed quarter that the books do not carry takes its tax share off the account for foreign tax, which never held the sale's tax: the account is off by it until the person corrects the sale. The foreign tax line carries no VAT code, so the foreign VAT stays out of the Swiss return (Ziffer 200 and 220); a strict reading of MWSTG Art. 24 Abs. 1 may count it in 200 and deduct it in 220, with no tax effect either way, a firm's choice to confirm with its fiduciary. Exports without tax, and the demo goldens, are unchanged.
- Kontoauszüge › «Datei» lists each camt.053 read in as the file it is, by account, not split into months (issue #208): its bank and BIC, IBAN, owner, the period the bank states and the first and last booking day, the number of movements, the opening and closing balance with their days, the file and the day it was read in, and every movement it holds, each a link to its payment. `/bank/files/<account>/<file name>` opens one.
- When a dropped camt.053 asks which account it is of, its row folds out «Details» (issue #208): the bank as the file names it and its BIC, the account's currency, IBAN and owner, the period the bank states and the first and last booking day, the number of movements, the opening and closing balance with their days, the full file name, and the first 5 movements (date, amount, counterparty, text) with «… und N weitere». When exactly one of the accounts offered has the bank the file names (its `bank:` in the servicer's name, the BIC's institution code, or the IBAN's bank code beside an IBAN already known under that bank), that account comes first, highlighted as «Vorschlag»; you still click it. No proposal when none or several match.
- «Verlauf» on a receipt's, a payment's and a journal entry's detail (issue #215): folded, the runs that worked on it, newest first, read from the books' run log (a run whose item is the object, or that wrote it: a payment's runs include the one that booked its entry or linked its receipt). Each row names the workflow, when, where it stands (done, waiting at a step, stuck with its reason, discarded) and what it wrote, each a link to its detail. The newest 20 are shown, with «Die neusten 20 von N Läufen. Alle zeigen» when there are more. A run opens its view in Automatisierung; a run whose details the run store no longer holds (retention, another computer) is marked «Details nicht mehr gespeichert», and its run view shows the run log's lines instead of an error. A person's own edits are not in it yet: the books record who changed an entry, not when.
- **⚠ action** Foreign VAT on an export order no longer lands in revenue (issue #206). A sales report's export order whose tax column shows a foreign VAT (for example 19 % German VAT, collected under the EU's IOSS on a consignment of at most EUR 150) was booked as one line at V0 on the gross, so the foreign tax counted as export turnover. Now the company's file of the provider names an account for it, `provider: { foreignTax: "2201" }` (a pass-through account the IOSS return is paid from), and the report books two lines: the net at V0 onto revenue and the tax onto that account without a VAT code; a refund the same reversed, the tax in the sale's share to the cent. Without that account such an order waits in the report's row («Ausfuhr mit ausländischer MWST: das Konto dafür fehlt»), never booked gross. Action: a firm that charges foreign VAT on exports (IOSS) adds `foreignTax` to its file of the provider and drops the report again; an export the report booked before as one gross line at V0 stays as booked, filed or not (the app never books an entry again), and its refund is booked the same way, gross at -V0 without a tax share; an entry from elsewhere that booked such an export gross at V0 on its day holds it, so it is never booked a second time beside it. A sale booked as one line is known when the books carry any line of its amount under its ref (as beside a person's Storno and rebooking of it); a sale booked as two lines only when the books carry exactly those two. A refund paid in the open period of an export of a filed quarter that the books do not carry takes its tax share off the account for foreign tax, which never held the sale's tax: the account is off by it until the person corrects the sale. The foreign tax line carries no VAT code, so the foreign VAT stays out of the Swiss return (Ziffer 200 and 220); a strict reading of MWSTG Art. 24 Abs. 1 may count it in 200 and deduct it in 220, with no tax effect either way, a firm's choice to confirm with its fiduciary. Exports without tax, and the demo goldens, are unchanged.

- Belege and Buchungen filter by chips (issue #211). «Filter» under the counts offers the fields, and each choice is a chip with × to remove it. Chips of different fields all hold (AND); two chips of the same field are alternatives, said between them («Geschäftspartner: FedEx oder Amazon»). Belege: Geschäftspartner (the business the app routes the receipt to), Zustand (zugeordnet, offen, ohne Datum, braucht dich, unlesbar, kein Beleg, Quarantäne), Monat, Ab, Bis, Betrag über, unter or genau, Währung, the MWST rate the receipt shows, Art, Zahlung verknüpft. Buchungen: Konto (Soll or Haben), the VAT code's rate, Geschäftspartner, the same dates and amounts, Währung, Gebucht von, Beleg (verknüpft, keiner, fehlt), Korrektur einer eingereichten Periode. Typing in the search offers the matching values as chips; the search itself stays «Text enthält». The counts are shortcuts to the state chips, Buchungen' «ohne Beleg», «Korrekturen» and origin buttons to theirs. The chips are in the link (`?f=state_open&f=over_100`, docs/urls.md), so a link reopens the filter; Belege's old `?filter=offen` links redirect. When nothing is left, the list says which chips exclude everything and offers to remove them, each with how many rows that brings back. Where a receipt came from (drop, mail, phone, collection) is not kept on a receipt, so it is not a field yet.

- «Zahlung verknüpfen» filters the payments by chips (issue #232). Under the search: «Filter» with Konto (each money account of the workspace by its label, currency and role), Betrag über, unter or genau, Ab, Bis, Richtung (Ausgang, Eingang) and Beleg (keiner, verknüpft); two chips of one field are alternatives, chips of different fields all hold. ⌥1…9 toggles the money accounts in the order shown beside the chips; ↑↓ and ↵ still pick and link. «Passt am besten» is ranked within the filter, and when the filter hides a best fit the dialog says so («1 passende Zahlung ausgeblendet durch den Filter») with «Alle Filter entfernen». The Konto chips stay chosen for the next receipt in the same browser tab (kept in the browser, never in the books). A booking without a payment moved on no money account, so a Konto or Richtung chip leaves it out.

- **⚠ action** Shop and card-terminal payouts go over the provider's clearing account (issue #203). A payment provider is a business whose file has a `provider:` section (Shopify's ships with the columns of its order and payments exports). Its payout is booked by code bank / clearing account without VAT, needs no receipt, and is never matched to one: no receipt set, no pair, no model match, no order link. Drop the provider's order export (CSV) into «Dateien hinzufügen»: the app recognises it by its columns, files it as a receipt and books each order onto the clearing account with its own code (delivered in Switzerland or Liechtenstein V81, abroad V0, a refund with -V81), dated by the day the provider collected it (the order's day on the agreed basis); the payments export books the fees against the same account. An order of a filed quarter is not booked and is counted in the row; a Swiss order without VAT waits for you; a report dropped twice books nothing twice; «Rückgängig» takes it back. The row asks once for a missing clearing account with the cut-over day (from which day on the provider's payouts go over it; the first day of the open quarter proposed), revenue or fee account and keeps them in the company's file of the provider (`provider: { clearing, since, … }`). Nothing dated before the cut-over day changes: payouts, sales and fees before it stay as they were booked, and the app never books an existing entry again. The report reaches back the provider's payout days before the cut-over day (Shopify: 7; the company's file may set its own `payoutDays`): an order collected then and paid out after the cut-over day is booked onto the clearing account on its own day, so no sale and none of its VAT goes missing at the cut-over and the clearing check comes out even; one the clearing account already carries (booked per order the old way) or that a payout before the cut-over day booked as sales may have paid waits for you instead of counting twice. Earlier orders are counted in the row, never booked. The clearing account must be a balance-sheet account. Order pages are linked to their sale as proof of the delivery, not to the payout. A card terminal's payout report books its sales and fees onto the clearing account the same way. The quarter's review shows each clearing account's balance, its month ends and what the provider paid out after the quarter's end. Action: a booking rule with `fees:` («bei SumUp immer so») is refused now and shows as broken under Firma › Regeln; put the provider's accounts into its file instead (`provider: { clearing, revenue, fees: { account, vat } }` in knowledge/suppliers/<id>.yaml), or drop its first report and answer the row. Until the company's file names the clearing account and the cut-over day, the provider's payouts wait for you («Durchlaufkonto fehlt …», «Umstellungstag fehlt …» under the run's note and on the payment): neither a rule nor the AI books them, since neither knows their sales, exports or fees. Only the orders the provider collected are booked: Shopify's export names how each order was paid (`Payment Method`), and orders paid by PayPal, bank transfer, a gift card or the test gateway are counted in the row («anders bezahlt, nicht gebucht»), never booked onto the clearing account. A Swiss order at the reduced rate (2.6 %) is booked with the reduced sale code; Swiss VAT shown on an export waits for you. A refund is booked on the day the money went back when the provider's export names it; Shopify's order export does not, so its refunds wait for you instead of being dated on the order's day. A refund paid in the open quarter of an order of a filed quarter is booked. What the books already carry after the cut-over day waits for you instead of being booked twice: an older sale on the clearing account holds the order it names (or, naming none, every order up to its day), and a payout booked as sales holds every order, refund and fee up to its day. Held orders are named in the row and kept on the report's receipt, shown on its detail. «Rückgängig» takes only the report's own bookings and the order pages' links, and leaves the sales another overlapping report also names. The order in which you drop the order and payments exports does not matter. One source of sales per provider: Shopify's payout PDFs no longer book sales. The quarter's clearing check says that the payouts after the quarter's end are the most the balance may be (they hold later sales too), names a balance below zero as a difference too, and says that a clearing account in euros carries exchange differences until the year close. A company's file may set its own `payoutDays`. A report that fails on an error is refused in its row; the rest of the drop goes on. Shopify's app file no longer routes every Stripe payout to Shopify; a payout whose reference begins with the provider's name («Received money from Stripe … with reference Shopify …») is still Shopify's when the bank names no payer or a payment processor of the provider's file (Shopify's: `processors: [ stripe ]`), so a customer's own transfer «Shopify Bestellung 1001» is no payout. When the bank's payments of a report's days are in the books (from its first order Shopify Payments collected, plus the payout days) and no payout of the provider is recognised among them, the report books nothing and its row says why and which bank accounts it looked at: «Trotzdem übernehmen» books it (the quarter's clearing check shows a difference if the payout was booked as sales), and the payout's name belongs into `match.tx` of knowledge/suppliers/shopify.yaml («Shopify öffnen» opens the partner). With `provider.account` the company's file names the bank account the provider pays into; only that account's payments count for the check. A payout dated in the payout days before the cut-over day that arrives after the report booked sales before it waits for you instead of being booked as sales. On the agreed basis the day the provider collected an order decides whether the report reaches it; the entry is dated by the order's day. An older sale or a hand entry on the clearing account that names that order alone with its amount (net of a Storno of it) counts as its booking and is no longer held on every taking; one naming several orders («Bestellungen #2001 und #2002») holds them all instead of booking the second again. The row names the orders it counts before its reach and says that a longer `payoutDays` reaches further. A clearing account another provider already uses is refused. A payout that waits says why on the payment itself. Filed quarters stay as they are. The demos' payouts are booked as before (their books are generated), so no demo figure changes.

- Several receipts that pay one payment are found across currencies, on card charges split across balances, and for suppliers that pay in batches (issue #190, part 2). Code links only what is exact and unique, never more than the payment; anything else is a proposal. Receipts in CHF paid from a Wise EUR balance are now added up against the amount the bank names on the payment («Card transaction of … CHF»), and linked when exactly one set of them fits; before, a set had to be in the payment's own currency. A set is linked only when it fits to the centime; a set within a centime per receipt (a shop that rounds each order) is never linked, even when it is the only one, and is proposed as fitting but for rounding. A card charge Wise took from two balances (one leg from EUR, the rest from CHF, each naming the whole charge) is one charge when the legs, at the ESTV rate of the day, add up to the charge within 3 % (one leg may be almost all of it, from a balance that held a few euros): its receipt, or its set of receipts, is linked to every leg, and each leg is booked with its own share in its own currency at the ESTV rate of its day, with the receipt's VAT; before, only one leg got the receipt and the other waited for one forever. Two whole charges of one amount at one shop on one day from two balances add up to twice the charge and stay two charges. A leg that waits while another leg of the same charge holds the receipts gets them too. The AI booking a leg is told that the receipt also pays the other legs and their amounts, and a booking whose input tax, with the other legs', exceeds the receipt's VAT is refused and left to you. A supplier whose business file says `grouped: true` (a marketplace, a courier) is treated as a batch payer: code tries its open receipts in date order, the oldest first, adding one at a time, also beyond the 12 receipts it combines freely and, when the bank names no original amount, at the ESTV rate up to 1.5 % below the payment, never above it. Such a run competes with the free combinations: when another set fits as well, every one is a proposal. Its receipts count as one supplier, whatever UID or title each shows. Which suppliers are batch payers now comes from the business files in both layers instead of three names in the app's code; a business is recognised by its file's `match`, a receipt without a UID also by the addresses and the name in its first and last lines, a payment also by the receipts linked to it (a business known only by its UID). A receipt that carries a UID of its own is never routed by its text: a shop's invoice that names FedEx as its carrier, links a fedex.com tracking page or says «Zahlungsart: Amazon Pay» stays the shop's. The app's AliExpress file still names no UID (alibaba.com receipts print the same one); its Tax Invoices are recognised by their filename, the archived one, or the one they came with when no business's filename pattern matches the archived one (`{orderId}_payment.pdf`, also when archived as `<date>-alibaba-<store>.pdf`; a stale original name never outvotes the archived one), or a title naming AliExpress. The buyer's own UID (company.uid), which many receipts print, says nothing of who wrote a receipt: it neither stops the app reading the receipt's text nor makes one supplier of receipts from several shops whose VAT number was misread as the buyer's. A business file may name a UID it prints that others print too (`sharedUid`): FedEx's, which the customs office's assessments of its shipments print as well, so a FedEx invoice is recognised by its text, its title or its filename (`<date>_fedex_…` as staged, `<date>-fedex.pdf` as archived; the app's filename patterns now take `-` or `_` and the end of the name), and an assessment joins no FedEx set. A title's vendor names a business as a whole word, and the titles prompt says the vendor is who issued the document, never a carrier or payment method it mentions. A set that is close to a payment but beyond the rounding reach is shown as close, no longer as fitting but for rounding. A company's own file of a business may say `grouped: true` when the app's file does not. When you link several receipts of one business to one payment by hand for the second time, «Immer so?» under the payment drafts the flag into that business's file and shows it on the usual card to keep with one click; it is not offered for a business whose file says `grouped` either way, and a copy you linked beside its original counts once. Two documents of the same charge (the receipt and the invoice PDF of one card payment) count once in a sum, and a receipt that repeats one a payment already holds joins no set; receipts you linked to a payment yourself are never taken for copies of each other. The matching chain no longer lets a copy compete with its original for a payment; the copy is left out of pairing until you answer the copy question in Belege. Kontoauszüge says when code stopped looking while it held back a set of the payment's own supplier (more receipts near the payment than it combines, or a batch payer with more open receipts than a run takes, 40), and how many sets fit when more are found than shown; the sets now come from the server, with the batch payers and the ESTV rates; when they cannot be loaded, Kontoauszüge says so, and another payment never shows the sets of the one before. `npm run eval:pairing` reports the multi-receipt payments found in full, in part, wrong and missed, counting documents (invoices, a copy once), and money in with order documents on its own line.
- Bills that prove a VAT-only correction are their own kind (issue #190, part 4). Some bills are linked on purpose to a small correction, not to a payment: a platform keeps its fees from its payouts and bills them monthly, and the bill's input tax is booked once as a single-leg entry without a payment (a `-2` code, docs/runbooks/booking-patterns.md). A business file now says which of its documents these are: a document type with `proves: vat-correction` and the `text` patterns that recognise the bill (processes/README.md). Such a bill is never paired with a payment, neither by code, nor in a set, nor by the AI's match, nor by `npm run evidence`; Belege no longer says «Noch keine Zahlung» but «Beleg einer reinen MWST-Korrektur», and names the booking without a payment whose VAT-amount line (one leg, a `-2` code) equals the bill's VAT to the centime within 10 days before to 90 days after the bill, with «Verknüpfen»; with no such booking it says the booking is still missing and how much input tax it books. No app file says it yet: a company writes it into its own file of the business.
- Card names a supplier goes by on the statement are learned (issue #190, part 1). A card statement often names a supplier otherwise than its receipt does («AMZN Mktp DE*…» for the marketplace's invoice, «PAYPAL *SHOP …»). When you or the AI link two payments with the same name to receipts of one business the app knows (by its file), the app writes that name into the company's file of the business (`knowledge/suppliers/<id>.yaml`, `match.tx`, and `learnedTx`), cut before the card's reference; Änderungen shows the file as changed.
