From then on a payment with that name is the business's: the pair search counts it as the name, so code links such a pair on its own, and the booking routes the payment to the business's rules. A name is never learned when a link puts it on another business's receipt, when a business's file claims its payments already, or when it is only the bank's own words. Geschäftspartner › Details marks a learned name «gelernt»; «Entfernen» takes it out of the file at once, and the app never learns it again. In general, a payment that names the receipt's business by its file (`match.tx`, `iban`) now counts as the name in the pair search, as two words of the vendor's name do.
- The pair search knows each supplier's usual delay between receipt and payment (issue #190, part 3). From the links already in the books it takes, per supplier (the business by its file, else the vendor on the receipt's title), the median number of days from receipt to payment, once three of its receipts are linked and the middle half of their delays lies within 7 days. For a supplier that is paid days or weeks later (a courier, a platform), a candidate's day now counts from the day that delay expects, so the payment on the usual day outranks one that is merely nearer the receipt, and a payment up to 14 days past the usual delay is a candidate even beyond the fixed 60 days. A supplier paid within three days (a subscription) is scored as before. «Zahlung suchen» says that the search reaches further for such a supplier.
- A courier's customs invoice is booked by its positions, in code (issue #197). Each line the app read off the invoice is named by its words (modules/ch documents.yaml `customs`, in German, French, Italian and English) and lands on its account: the import VAT the carrier advanced on 4030, customs duty on 4020, the carrier's fees (Vorlageprovision, Verzollung, Kontoabwicklung) on 4040, freight on 6280. Fees and freight carry the carrier's Swiss VAT where the line shows it; duty and import VAT carry none. The lines must add up to the invoice and the invoices to the payment; a payment in another currency is shared in proportion to the positions, within 5 % of the invoices' worth at the day's rate. Otherwise nothing is split: the model books the payment as before, and its reason says why («Zollrechnung nicht nach Positionen gebucht: …»). The import VAT is booked without input tax, and the booking says so: the carrier's invoice does not prove it, only the customs office's assessment (Veranlagungsverfügung MWST) that names the firm as importer does (MWSTG Art. 28 Abs. 1 lit. c). With the assessment linked, a one-legged line off 4030 with M81-2 deducts its tax (Ziffer 400). An assessment that arrives later is found by its amount and its number (the courier's invoice cites it) and brings the deduction on the next run: on the payment's day while its quarter is open, else as a correction of its own in the open period with `corrects`, its description naming the filed entry, and linked to the assessment, so the assessment's page shows the correction as made. Each import VAT line of an invoice of several shipments is deducted by its own assessment; one assessment proves one line, and copies of it (the same number) count once. A deduction a person removed or changed is never booked again, an assessment she took off a payment is not brought back to it, an import VAT line already booked at a rate gets no second deduction, and an assessment she typed as something else is not used (`importVatDeduction` on the assessment, an optional field older builds ignore). The assessment is kept as a document beside the courier's invoice («Veranlagungsverfügung des Zolls»). Import VAT paid to the customs office itself has the assessment as its receipt: the matching pairs it with a payment whose text names its number, for its amount, and the payment is booked on 4030 with the deduction. On the agreed basis a document beside the invoice (a customs assessment, a delivery note) no longer dates a line's tax point. The bookings say `by: code, purchaseVat`, a check every build knows, so a sealed quarter reads the same in older builds. The VAT step reads a customs invoice line by line. A firm without input tax books no deduction. A company rule for the courier still books before the split. The books' and the manifest's format versions are unchanged.
- Receipts and payments match in either order (issue #187). A receipt that came before its bank statement was matched only once, at its own intake; now every intake run (a statement, dropped or collected receipts, «Eingang verarbeiten») looks again at every receipt without a payment and every payment without a receipt. Code goes first, so fewer receipts cost an AI call: a dropped receipt whose payment code pairs clearly (by the date and total code reads off it) is archived, named on the fast tier and linked without its own match prompt, and without an AI it is archived and linked by code; only the rest goes to the AI's match, shown the code's short list where there is one, and the match prompt lists its payments in fewer words. Code scores the candidates by one table (amount to the cent, or the original amount the bank names for a card charge in another currency in the text of the charge; the day; the vendor's name; a document number) and links a clear pair, unique both ways by a margin. With an AI the clear pairs are checked first, about 20 per call on their short facts, and the AI chooses from short lists of up to five at 80 % or more; without one, code links only the clear pairs that also name the vendor, carry a document number or the bank's original amount, and the rest waits. Payments that need no receipt (marked so, own transfers, those booked onto the owner's account, those a company rule exempts) are never paired. A credit note (its sign, its VAT or its title) is matched to money in. Each link says who made it and what matched («vom Code gefunden: Betrag, Datum, Name passen …», «… von der KI geprüft», «von der KI gewählt», «von Hand») and «Lösen» undoes it; a pair you unlinked is never paired again, by the chain, the receipt sets or the payout orders. A late receipt pointed at a filed payment has its own «Lösen», and a filed payment one receipt points at is never pointed at by a second. A payment of a filed quarter is never linked: the receipt points at it, as before. New buttons «Zahlung suchen» on a receipt without a payment and «Beleg suchen» on a payment without a receipt (and in the task «Beleg fehlt») run the same chain for that one record and show the outcome in place: linked, the candidates to pick (the best five of how many), or what was searched. `npm run eval:pairing` measures the chain on a workspace's own links, blinded, and prints counts only.
- The AI setup on a fresh Mac (docs/0.4.0/ai-setup.md, design B). A fresh install of the Mac app opens straight into «Einrichtung», and its first message is the AI: the Claude Code card with the minimum plan («Mit Claude Pro oder höher, etwa 20 USD im Monat», with the link to Anthropic's plans), the answers «Claude Code installieren» and «Ohne KI weiter», and the folded «Erweitert: mit API-Schlüssel». The steps are drawn by code, so they work before any AI exists. «Claude Code installieren» opens Terminal with Anthropic's official installer written out (`curl -fsSL https://claude.ai/install.sh | bash`); it runs only once you press Enter there, and the same window can then start `claude` to sign in. No Homebrew and no npm. The app notices the result by itself, without a restart: while a card waits it looks every 2 seconds, in `~/.local/bin` and on the login shell's PATH, and after 15 minutes it says «nicht gefunden» with where it looked. «Anmelden» opens Terminal with `claude auth login`. The sign-in comes from `claude auth status`, of which the app keeps whether you are signed in, the method and the plan, never the email or the organization; it never opens a CLI's login, credential files or Keychain entries. Once signed in, one test call with the cheapest model shows a plan without Claude Code or a used-up limit as a fact; «Nochmals prüfen» makes it again. With an API key instead, the key is checked by its provider, kept in this Mac's keychain store and tested once; a refused key is not kept. Then the conversation asks for the folder (today's default as the proposal), «Ich habe schon eine Buchhaltung» or «Erst die Demo ansehen», and goes on into the setup conversation; without an AI it goes on with buttons and fields, and typing in your own words says it waits for an AI. «Ohne KI weiter» is remembered on this Mac (`noAi` in `~/.vanilla-accounting/ai.json`), so the first run does not ask again. «Neue Buchhaltung anlegen» opens the same conversation; the form stays as «Ohne KI einrichten». The same card stands in Firma › KI («KI-Programme auf diesem Mac», Claude Code and Codex, installing and signing in from there; the API keys moved into «Erweitert: mit API-Schlüssel»; with both programs ready, an option to ask the other one when one cannot answer, off by default), in the KI column when its program does not answer (Claude Code, or Codex when the app asks Codex; the input off, with the reason), and in the error line that replaces the old «braucht die App Claude Code» message: «Beleg lesen braucht eine KI. Auf diesem Mac ist keine eingerichtet.», with «KI einrichten» and «Von Hand erfassen». The books' and the manifest's format versions are unchanged.
- Codex in the first run (issue #184). The first message of «Einrichtung» shows both cards, Claude Code and Codex, each with its minimum plan («Mit ChatGPT Plus oder höher, etwa 20 USD im Monat», with the link to OpenAI's plans), and the answers «Claude Code installieren», «Codex installieren» and «Ohne KI weiter». After you pick one, the conversation follows that program: install, sign-in, one test call, ready («Codex ist bereit: angemeldet mit ChatGPT, ein Probelauf hat geantwortet.»). The other one stays a quiet «Doch lieber …» answer, and «Ändern» on your pick goes back. When one is already on the Mac, the conversation starts from it («Codex ist schon auf diesem Mac …») and skips the install: a signed-in program first, else an installed one, else Claude Code. «Codex installieren» opens the same friendly Terminal screen as Claude Code, in your language, with OpenAI's official installer (`curl -fsSL https://chatgpt.com/codex/install.sh | sh`, run with `CODEX_NON_INTERACTIVE=1`, so it does not ask «Start Codex now?» in English of its own) and then `codex login`. Codex now gets the test call too, with its cheapest model, and its card no longer warns about Apple's developer tools: a check in a fresh macOS VM showed that the app's own Codex call raises no such dialog. Of `codex login status` the app keeps only whether you are signed in and whether with ChatGPT or an API key.
- The ChatGPT subscription as an AI, through the Codex CLI (issue #121). A Mac with `codex` installed and signed in with «Sign in with ChatGPT» (ChatGPT Plus or higher) can run the app's single requests on it: receipt titles and VAT facts, reading a scanned receipt and matching a receipt to a payment, booking proposals, learning from a correction, saves, Tutti purchases, mail triage and the setup conversation. Codex takes images but no PDF: when one of its steps needs a receipt's PDF, the app draws the PDF's first pages (at most 4, at 150 dpi) as PNG images with macOS's own PDF engine at that moment, never at intake, and sends them with the PDF's own text and a line that says which pages of how many it sees. The PDF stays as it was received. Drawn pages are kept in the books' `data/pdf-pages/` (outside git, safe to delete), by the file's content, page and resolution, so a page is drawn once. Claude Code and the API keys still get the PDF itself. The KI column (the sidebar) runs on Codex too, with the Claude sidebar's write boundary: Codex gets no tool of its own that reaches the disk, a shell or the web (the closed run above), and its only tools are the app's, served for each message on this Mac (an MCP server at a random local address): Read, Glob and Grep in the books folder, the app's files and the company's repository, and Write and Edit only in the draft (`data/draft/…`, never the chart's draft). A change to the books is named in the answer's change block, as on Claude Code, and goes through the books' write door, which refuses a filed quarter. A run that starts anything else (a command, an edit of a file, a web search, a sub-agent, another tool) is ended at once and nothing of its answer is applied. Which CLI runs the column follows «Die App fragt» in Firma › KI (or the column's own row there); a conversation started on one continues there, and after a switch its next message starts afresh. Codex's sessions are kept in the app's own Codex folder. Firma › KI shows Codex beside Claude Code (bereit, installiert und nicht angemeldet, or nicht gefunden). With both on the Mac, «Die App fragt» chooses which one the steps ask first; it also decides what «Im Terminal» opens, which had its own choice before. When the one asked cannot answer (its usage limit, signed out), the app says so in the CLI's words and does not switch; the option «das andere fragen» below the choice lets it ask the other one by itself, off unless you turn it on. A subscription never asks an API key on its own. The app runs `codex exec` as an answer only: from an empty folder, with the step's own instructions in place of Codex's, in a Codex folder of its own (`~/.vanilla-accounting/codex-home`, yours alone) that holds only a link to your sign-in, so your `~/.codex/AGENTS.md`, config, rules and skills never reach a prompt (`--ignore-user-config` alone still sent `AGENTS.md`), without plugins, apps, web search, shell, image or browser tools, in Codex's read-only sandbox, keeping no session; a run that starts anything but an answer (a command, an edit, a search, a sub-agent) is ended and fails. It never reads `~/.codex` or a token: `codex login status`, asked in the app's folder, says whether it is signed in, and a token Codex refreshes is written through the link into your own file. Each call is counted in `data/llm-ledger.jsonl` as `codex-cli`. The choice is kept per Mac in `~/.vanilla-accounting/ai.json` (`cli`, `askOther`; the old `terminal` is read as `cli`).
- The microphone in the AI chat of the Mac app, recognized on the Mac (issue #150). The button showed only in Chrome and Safari: in the Mac app the browser's speech recognition fails, so there was none. Now the app brings its own. Click the microphone, speak German, French, Italian or English (de-CH, fr-CH, it-CH, en-US), and the words appear in the field as you speak; a second click, sending, closing the chat or switching to another window stops it. Your voice never leaves the Mac: from macOS 26 it is recognized by the system's on-device SpeechAnalyzer, which loads a language's model from Apple the first time («Lädt die Spracherkennung auf diesen Mac»). Before macOS 26 the app uses the older recognizer only where Dictation in System Settings › Keyboard has the language on the Mac, falling back from the Swiss to the German, French or Italian model; where neither is there, the button says so instead of going online. macOS asks once for the microphone (and before macOS 26 for speech recognition); refused, the line under the field names the switch in System Settings › Privacy & Security.
- «Im Terminal öffnen»: what the AI sidebar cannot do goes to Claude Code or Codex in the terminal (issue #146). The sidebar runs no programs, so a wish such as one PDF of all receipts of a quarter, a converted file or a script was answered with «geht nicht». Now the answer offers to continue in the terminal: a card shows the request written out, «Im Terminal öffnen» opens Terminal in the books folder with the CLI started on that request, and the command is there to copy. When both CLIs are installed, the one chosen in Firma › KI › Im Terminal opens and the other is offered as «oder mit …». A CLI that is not signed in opens anyway, and the card says first that it will ask to sign in. With neither installed, the card links the vendors' installation pages. The app never checks a plan and never reads a CLI's login. The terminal AI follows the app's rules in `vanilla-accounting/AGENTS.md` (and `CLAUDE.md`, which loads it): read freely, write results into `output/` (not committed to git), never edit the books files, and change bookings only by a proposal in `data/terminal/`. The app takes each proposal into the sidebar as a conversation whose changes wait, booking by booking, for «Übernehmen», and makes them as the person's, refused in a filed period like any other change. New workspaces get these files. `npm run update` and each opening of the terminal add the app's section, between markers, to a company's own `AGENTS.md` without touching its other lines. The books' and the manifest's format versions are unchanged.
- «Im Terminal weitermachen» in the chat's input: a small terminal button beside «+» and the microphone, for when the sidebar is not enough and the answer offered no card. It opens Claude Code or Codex in the books folder the same way as the card (the CLI chosen in Firma › KI, the request one argument, never read as shell code). It hands over the words typed in the field; with an empty field, the conversation's last question and the first line of the chat's answer, cut to a few hundred characters, without attachments or references. With neither CLI installed it shows the card's installation guide. The button shows only where the app opens Terminal: on the Mac, not in a read-only export.
- The setup conversation before the books exist understands what you type, from the first question (issue #145). Below the conversation is a field for your own words, for example «Wir sind eine kleine Schreinerei in Bern, GmbH, zwei Angestellte, MWST-pflichtig mit Saldosteuersatz 4.5 %, Konto bei der Raiffeisen». The AI reads it with the answers so far, the question waiting and every answer the app accepts. It fills as many answers as your words state, asks back once for what is still missing or unclear, and answers questions about the setup in plain words («Was ist der Saldosteuersatz?»). The app checks each answer the AI read exactly as it checks a click, before it saves it. An answer it refuses is not saved, and the reply says why: a UID with a wrong check digit, an answer the question does not offer, one not supported yet. The AI never fills in a UID or an IBAN: the app takes one only when it stands in your own words. A firm named without its UID is not saved, but its card starts with the name and the owner. Every answer taken from your words carries «aus deiner Nachricht», in the conversation and on the last card, until you change it; the buttons and fields stay as shortcuts, and «Ändern» works as before. Your words go only to the AI you set up for the new step «Einrichtung im Gespräch» in Firma › KI (by default the Claude subscription), and each call is counted in the folder's `data/llm-ledger.jsonl`, which becomes the books' own. Without an AI the form opens with its reason, as before. The setup draft keeps which answers came from your words (`fromText`); the draft is not part of the books, and the manifest's and the books' format versions are unchanged.

- VAT on the agreed consideration (vereinbartes Entgelt, MWSTG Art. 39 Abs. 1), the law's default, besides the cash basis (issue #133). Setup, `npm run init -- --basis agreed` and Firma › MWST ask the basis; `company.vat.reporting` is `received` or `agreed`, and a switch is refused while the open year has entries, like the method. On the agreed basis an invoice the app issues is booked on its date against the receivable (1100) with its VAT, a credit note and a cancellation on theirs, and the customer's payment only settles the receivable. A payment's line with a VAT code takes the date of its linked receipt and is booked against 1100 or 2000, which the payment settles on its own date. A foreign document converts at its own date's ESTV rate, and the payment's exchange difference goes to the Kursdifferenzen account. The eCH-0217 XML writes `formOfReporting` 1. Open invoices from before a switch count in the first period after it (MWSTV Art. 106). A customer payment that could pay an open invoice is not booked as revenue a second time: it waits for the person to confirm it on the invoice. A prepayment is taxed when it is received, a credit note or refund in the period it is booked (Art. 40 Abs. 1 lit. c, Art. 41). The VAT return is not written while an invoice's VAT is unbooked for want of a rate. Workspaces on the cash basis compute the same figures as before.
- «Neue Buchhaltung anlegen» in the Mac app is the setup conversation, as one flow (issue #139). The start screen asks only the folder («Buchhaltung 2026» in Documents, in the app's language) and the books' language. Then the setup conversation opens in a wide panel and asks who the firm is (name, UID, owner), what fits it best (the usual answers of consulting, a webshop, a trade or a freelancer, or every question one by one), each VAT question, the approved net tax rates when they apply, and the bank accounts. Each answer is saved at once into the folder (`vanilla-accounting/setup-draft.json`); «Ändern» takes one back. A last card shows everything the books will get, with «Ändern» on every answer, and «Buchhaltung anlegen» creates them through the same path as the form. The conversation then goes on inside the new books where it stopped: from when you book, the previous program, the opening, the receipts. A setup broken off before the books exist resumes at the same step when the folder is opened again, from «Buchhaltung öffnen», from the recently opened list («Einrichtung läuft») or at the next start. Books the conversation created open into the setup until «Los geht's»; after that, only «Einrichtung fortsetzen» in Firma opens it. The form stays as the way by hand behind «Ohne KI einrichten», and it opens by itself, with the reason, when no AI answers on this computer (neither the claude program nor an API key). Both paths write the same `belege.workspace.yaml`. The draft is removed before the books' first commit, and the manifest's and the books' format versions are unchanged.

- Input tax on other proof than a receipt (issue #134). Swiss law weighs evidence freely (MWSTG Art. 81 Abs. 3), and the ESTV accepts several documents that together prove the VAT was charged and paid, such as a contract with the VAT and the payment confirmation, where no invoice is at hand (MWST-Info 09, Ziff. 1.6). A payment without a receipt still waits for it, and the app and the AI still never deduct input tax without one. A person can now book such a payment by hand with the rate: when a line deducts input tax and no receipt is linked, the booking form asks for «Anderer Nachweis der Vorsteuer», where she names the documents. The payment keeps her words (`inputTaxProof`), Kontoauszüge shows them, the booking's «Warum» says «Vorsteuer mit anderem Nachweis», and the booking is no longer listed as input tax without a receipt. «Rückgängig» takes the proof back with the booking. The task «Beleg fehlt» now offers «Beleg zuordnen», «Mit anderem Nachweis» and «Ohne Vorsteuer buchen». The field is optional and an older app ignores it: the books' format version is unchanged. Invoices keep asking for the customer: the law lets only a receipt from an automated cash register up to CHF 400 leave out the recipient (MWSTG Art. 26 Abs. 3, MWSTV Art. 57), and the app issues invoices, not cash-register receipts.

- Reporting dimensions on bookings (issue #127). A booking can carry one value each of several dimensions at once, such as a project, a cost centre and a location, like Banana's cost centres or the classes and jobs of QuickBooks. You set the dimensions up in Firma › Dimensionen: a name, and the values each offers, each with an optional label. A value you no longer need is set aside: it is no longer offered, and the bookings that carry it keep it. A dimension that bookings carry cannot be removed; the page says how many bookings carry it. A firm without dimensions sees nothing new. In Buchungen, the booking's form has one field per dimension. It offers the dimension's values and the values other bookings already carry, and it takes a new value as you type it. The new-entry dialog has the same fields, and so has each line of a payment booked by hand, so a split booking can put each line on its own project. A copy, a reversal or a correction of a booking carries its values along. A booking of a filed quarter keeps its values as they are, because they belong to the sealed record: the form shows them read-only and says so, and the API refuses the change like any other change there. A rule can set the values with `then.tags` (`then: { account: "6570", vat: 81, tags: { projekt: alpha } }`): every line the rule books carries them, and the rule's card says them in its line. Firma › Regeln › «Neue Regel» has one field per dimension. The Erfolgsrechnung can be filtered by one or more values (several values of one dimension: any of them; values of several dimensions: all of them; «ohne Wert» for the bookings without one), and it can show one column per value of a dimension, with the bookings without a value in a last column; the columns add up to the total. The ledger of an account follows the filter. The filter is kept for the session and never put into the link, because a value may be a client's name. Eigene Berichte filter bookings by dimension values (`filter.tags`) and group them by a dimension (`group: tag:projekt`); the form offers both. No VAT figure reads the values. **Format:** the books go to schema 4. No record changes, and every seal stays as it was. An older app refuses the new books instead of dropping or ignoring the values. `npm run update` writes the new number, with a backup in `data/backups/`. The manifest keeps schema 2: its new `dimensions` block is optional, and an older app that cannot open the books never edits it.
- The year close asks of money accounts only what the law asks (issue #119). Every bank and card account needs its balance on 31.12. from a document: an imported statement that covers the day, or the bank's year-end confirmation. A firm that imports no statements enters that balance in Abschluss › Prüfungen › Saldi per 31.12. and attaches the confirmation (PDF or image), or runs `npm run year:close -- 2026 --balance 1020=<balance> --document Saldobestaetigung.pdf`. The cash box needs the amount counted on 31.12. (`--cash 1000=<counted>`); a difference to the books is shown and blocks the close until you book it or count again, the app never books it. The owner's and employees' accounts need no confirmation. «Die Kontoauszüge decken das ganze Jahr ab» now checks only accounts with at least one imported statement, as Kontoauszüge does since #116, and `npm run doctor` only notes a missing statements folder instead of failing. A country with stricter rules lists them in its module (`moneyAccounts.monthlyStatements` in `year-end.yaml`); Switzerland lists none. The confirmation lies in `transactional-data/<year>/filings/year-end/`, its balance in the books' statement balances as a statement PDF's does, and the cash count in the year's close record (`filings/year-close.json`): the books' format does not change.
- `npm run doctor` checks that every reference in the books names a record (issue #126). It reports a link to a payment or a receipt that is gone, an entry that names a missing receipt or books on an account the chart lacks, a VAT adjustment or a correction case that names a missing entry, an invoice payment, a statement row or a receipt's filed payment whose movement is gone, a hand movement confirmed by a statement row that no statement lists, an open question about something gone, a movement on a money account `belege.workspace.yaml` lacks, and opening balances of a year that differ from the closing of the closed year before. Doctor prints a count per kind with the first three; the app logs one line when it first loads books that have any. The check only reports and changes nothing.
- Money accounts are equals, and bank statements are optional on every account (issue #88). Each account of `bankAccounts` in `belege.workspace.yaml` now names its role: `bank`, `cash`, `owner`, `employee`, `card` or `clearing`. Any of them, a bank account included, takes movements made by hand, from a receipt or from an answer, and also statement imports when you have them. A statement you import later is matched with the movements already known: a row on the same account, in the same direction, for the same amount, within 7 days, and with a matching name when both sides name one, confirms that movement. The row does not become a second payment. The movement keeps its bookings and links, and Kontoauszüge marks it «abgeglichen». If you take the statement back, the movement stays and is no longer confirmed. A month without a statement depends on the account's own history. On an account that has had at least one imported statement it is «fehlt» and counts on the dock's badge, as before. On an account that never had one, such as the cash box, the owner's account, or the bank of a firm that never imports, it shows «ohne Auszug» with the balance from the movements, not reconciled with a statement, and does not count on the badge; you can still import a statement there. There is no setting for this: the first statement you import for an account turns it on. Firma › Geldkonten (formerly Bankkonten) shows each account's role. An account that no statement covers shows the sum of its movements, «aus den Bewegungen, nicht abgestimmt». The owner's and an employee's account can carry their own IBAN. A movement on an account the manifest does not name is refused; before, it was booked on the first bank account. `POST /api/transactions/manual` makes a movement on any money account, and adds a missing account (cash box, employee, card) together with its first movement. A bank payment between the company and the owner's account is now a transfer without a VAT code, like a cash withdrawal. **Format:** the manifest goes to schema 2 (each account gains its `role`) and the books go to schema 3 (no record changes, and every seal stays as it was). An older app refuses the new books instead of booking them wrongly. `npm run update` writes both files, with backups in `data/backups/`. A change on the Firma page writes the manifest at the new schema by itself. To try it first, run it on a copy of your books, as in the pull request.
- TypeSafe Jev as an optional provider in Firma › KI (issue #48, step 3 of 5). Jev answers typed questions only: yes or no, or one of a few choices, with how sure it is. It writes no text and no JSON and reads no PDF, so Firma › KI offers it only for a step that asks such a question, never for reading, booking or the AI sidebar. The first such step is «Mail mit Beleg erkennen»: a mail source's code can ask `io.mail.triage(uid)` whether a mail brings a receipt. It gets the probability, how sure the model is, and which model answered. The question carries the mail's sender and subject only, never its body or attachments. Below 80 % certainty, or when Jev does not answer, the default model answers instead and the ledger shows both calls. Without Jev, the step uses the default model, Haiku through the Claude subscription. The app's own IMAP source keeps its rule (every attached PDF) and does not ask. A Jev key is kept like the others (`VANILLA_JEV_API_KEY` without a keychain) and checked with one question about a single word, with no company data. Jev reads PNG and JPEG images of 280 to 1568 pixels a side; the app refuses any other image before sending it.
- Firma › KI: which AI the app asks for each step, on this computer (issue #48, step 2 of 5). Besides the Claude subscription through Claude Code, the app can now use an Anthropic or an OpenAI API key. You paste the key there; the app checks it with the provider's model list before it keeps it, keeps it in this Mac's keychain (the desktop app's sealed store, or the login keychain under `npm start`), and never writes it into the books, a log or the ledger, and never shows it again. Without a keychain it goes into `.env` as `VANILLA_ANTHROPIC_API_KEY` or `VANILLA_OPENAI_API_KEY`; the app never reads `ANTHROPIC_API_KEY` or `OPENAI_API_KEY`, so a key exported for another tool costs nothing unasked. Below the providers, each step (reading a scan, the VAT on a receipt, titles, finding a receipt's payment, booking a payment, learning from a correction, proposing saves, Tutti purchases, the AI sidebar) has a model list with only the models that can do what the step needs: the provider's shipped models and those its model list adds. The shipped ones are Claude Haiku, Sonnet and Opus 5.5 and OpenAI's GPT-6 Luna, GPT-6.1 Sol and GPT-6 Astra; a model the list adds counts as reading PDFs when its provider says its generation does (every Claude model, every OpenAI chat model from gpt-4o on), so a newer model is offered for reading scans without an update of the app. Reading a scan and finding a payment need a model that reads PDFs; the AI sidebar runs only with Claude Code. The choice is kept per computer in `~/.vanilla-accounting/ai.json`, never in the books, so two people on the same books can use different models. When a key does not answer, the step asks the Claude subscription instead and the ledger shows both calls; the subscription never asks a key that costs money on its own. With no key and no setting the app works as before. A save of the books refuses a file that holds an API key, and `npm run check-public` refuses one in the app's files. The ledger and `npm run llm-usage` name the provider of each call.
- A booking without a payment carries its receipt (issue #89). A depreciation, an accrual, a private share, a correction or a gift received is booked without a bank payment, under a ref of its own (M2026-004). Until now no receipt could be linked to such a booking, so the document stayed «offen» in Belege. On such a booking in Buchungen, «Beleg verknüpfen» lists the receipts no payment holds yet, a receipt of the booking's amount first. In Belege, «Zahlung verknüpfen» lists the open bookings without a payment of the receipt's amount under «Buchungen ohne Zahlung», and a search finds the others. The receipt then shows «Gebucht … · ohne Bankzahlung» with «lösen», and it no longer counts as «offen». Linking changes nothing on the booking: it keeps its accounts and its VAT code. A booking of a filed quarter takes no receipt (refused like any change there), and the seal of a filed quarter stays as it was. A receipt the owner paid privately is linked to nothing else. Deleting the booking takes its links along, and «Rückgängig» brings them back. The AI sidebar can do the same: `link` with the booking's ref, or a new booking with `receipts`. It no longer books such a booking without its document.
- The setup asks «Was verkauft ihr?» with any combination of products you make yourself, goods you buy and resell (Handel), and services (issue #46). The answer sets the default accounts: material for your own products on 4000, goods for resale on 4200; sales of own products on 3000, traded goods on 3200, services on 3400. The AI gets them as context for every booking; a supplier or customer can still differ by a rule of its own. The setup form and `npm run init` (`--sells made,services`) ask it, and the setup conversation asks it right after the VAT, as three toggles started from what the form saved, with a card of the default accounts and «Übernehmen». The manifest keeps the answer as before (`profile.sells: services | goods | both`) and which goods in a new `profile.goods: [made, traded]`, so a manifest written before reads unchanged: `goods` without `goods:` is traded goods, as a webshop, and a payout's sales still go to 3200 there. The format version is unchanged: an older app ignores the new line.
- Several receipts that pay one payment together are linked and booked per receipt (issue #27). When receipts of one vendor add up to a payment exactly, to the centime, in the payment's currency, and are dated near it, the inbox run links them all without the AI. It also links a receipt that completes those already linked. The payment is then booked one line per receipt, each with its own amount and the VAT code its own receipt shows: if the AI books such a payment on one line, the app splits that line by the receipts. A set that is off by a centime, or one of two sets that fit, is not linked. Kontoauszüge shows it on the payment's line instead («Diese 3 Belege passen zusammen», or «passen fast: … statt …») with «Verknüpfen». Receipts in another currency than the payment are never summed against it.
- «Privat oder bar bezahlt» for only a part of a receipt (issue #87). The link dialog's «Privat oder bar bezahlt» now opens a short form: «Ganzer Betrag», as before, or «Nur ein Teil» with an amount in the receipt's currency and what it was for («Kaffee mit Kunde»). The owner's payment is made for that part and linked to the receipt. The booking books only that part against the owner's account, with the VAT the receipt shows for it: the receipt's one Swiss rate, the rate line the person picks when the receipt shows several, and I0 when it shows no Swiss VAT. The private rest is never booked. A foreign receipt is booked at the ESTV rate of the receipt's day, and the toast names the amount in CHF. The receipt says «Nur Kaffee geschäftlich», with the part and the receipt's total, instead of an amount that is off. A payment made privately in a foreign currency is now booked on the owner's account; before, it landed on the first bank account of the manifest. The AI sidebar can do the same with its new change `privatePayment`, shown as a card with «Rückgängig», and it no longer books a receipt's purchase as a booking without the receipt: it asks who paid. A booking by hand that books the receipt already (the amount of the receipt or of the part, on its day or with its words) holds the private payment with the booking's number, and «Buchung ersetzen» replaces it in the same step. A receipt paid privately can no longer be linked to a second payment, also not to the bank payment that pays the owner back (issue #90): that payment belongs on the owner's account without VAT.
- A receipt whose VAT cannot be read is asked about, not booked on a guess (issue #57). If a payment's only receipt is a scan too poor to read, or a reading that names no Swiss VAT either way, the inbox run does not book it. It waits for the person at once: Aufgaben shows «Beleg nicht lesbar: <business>, <amount>» with «Neu fotografieren» (opens the receipt), «So buchen wie meistens (8.1 %)» (the rate most of this business's receipts showed before, offered only when one rate wins) and «Ohne Vorsteuer buchen». Each answer sets the receipt's VAT facts as the person's, and the payment is booked. Under the rate answer, «Immer so?» drafts the company's rule for that business, `apply: { unreadReceipt: <rate> }` in `knowledge/<role>/<id>.yaml`, which then waits in Änderungen. Once the rule is kept, that business's unreadable receipts are booked at its rate without asking. Nothing shipped assumes a rate.
- A payment that needs a receipt waits for it instead of being booked without (issue #29). The inbox run no longer books a payment out while no receipt is linked to it, and later books it again once the receipt comes: it leaves it unbooked, marked «wartet auf den Beleg» (on the payment, in the run's note, and under Automatisierung › Übersicht at «Gebucht»), and books it as soon as a receipt is linked, with the receipt's VAT. A payment that needs no receipt is booked at once: money coming in, the bank's own fee row, a transfer to one of the firm's own accounts, a payment booked onto the owner's account, a money account or a debt of the chart (an outlay paid back to the owner or an employee, a card's bill: issue #90), a payment marked «braucht keinen», a firm that claims no input tax, and a business whose file says `apply: { receipt: none }` on a rule of `evidence.guide.yaml#rules` (shipped for the ESTV, the compensation fund and the cantonal tax office, whose notices are filed apart; a company can add it for its bank or its rent). A company rule that books waits for the receipt like the AI, unless it books onto such an account; a rule that asks first («frag mich bei Barbezügen») asks at once. Two weeks before the return of the payment's period is due, a payment still without its receipt becomes a task under Aufgaben, «Beleg fehlt: …», with «Beleg zuordnen» and «Ohne Vorsteuer buchen», which books it at once with the zero code (an Eigenbeleg, #8, is not built yet, so it is not offered). «Neu klassifizieren» on a payment still books it at once: the person asked for it.
- «Ins Wissen übernehmen?» under a booking you corrected (issue #40). When you change the account or the VAT code of a booking that no rule made, the journal entry shows one quiet line with how many of the partner's payments this year are booked the same way, and two buttons. «Vorschlag der KI» asks a small model (Haiku) for a rule for this business or payer, or for one sentence for the partner's notes, or for nothing. The app checks the answer before you see it: a rule is offered only when at least two of the partner's payments, this one counted, are booked as it would book them, only when it books this payment exactly as you did, and only for one business or payer, so the app can always carry it out. Anything else is refused and nothing is shown. A rule goes to the usual card with its payments, including «Hält für 4 von 5 gebuchten Zahlungen; die andere ist heute anders gebucht», the national VAT warning of #75 and «Trotzdem behalten». A sentence is added to the partner's notes only with «In die Notizen». «Selbst schreiben» opens the rule form of Firma › Regeln, started from the payment. A changed amount or date offers nothing, and a booking a rule made keeps «Regel anpassen?».
- A booking keeps what the AI read about the supplier (issue #80). On each line it books, the AI says what the receipt buys (goods, a service, an exempt service) and where the supplier sits. The app used these facts to decide Bezugsteuer and then dropped them. Now the entry keeps them as `supply` and `supplierCountry`. A rule's card uses them to warn about Bezugsteuer on a service from abroad, also for a payment the app never booked with Bezugsteuer. Only new bookings get the fields: entries booked before, and every entry of a filed quarter, stay as they were, so the seals hold. The books' format version is unchanged, because a reader that finds no such field reads the entry as before.
- A rule's card warns when the rule books against a national VAT rule (issue #75). For each payment of the open year the rule books, the app works out the code the national rules alone would give: Bezugsteuer on a service from abroad, the receipt rules of `purchase_vat` and the facts of the app's business files. Where the rule's code differs, the card says so in words, as in «Diese Regel bucht 6 Zahlungen ohne Bezugsteuer, obwohl der Anbieter im Ausland sitzt und keine Schweizer MWST verrechnet» or «… mit Vorsteuer, obwohl kein Beleg Schweizer MWST zeigt». It lists those payments with the booking the national rule gives, and the button becomes «Trotzdem behalten». «Behalten» without that confirmation is refused, on the card, in the AI sidebar and by «Speichern». Whether a payment buys a service from abroad is what the app read when it booked it: a payment the app or a person booked with Bezugsteuer before counts as one. A rule that agrees with the national rules shows nothing new.
- The AI reads the VAT facts of a payment, the app computes the VAT code (issue #64). For each booking line the AI now says what the receipt shows: Swiss VAT charged or not, the rate, Bezugsteuer, or that the amount is the VAT itself. It writes no code. The app takes the letter from the account (M on material and goods, I on operating costs and investments, V on revenue), the minus from the direction of the money, and `-1`/`-2` from the kind of booking. An AI answer that still contains a code keeps only its rate, so I81 on a 4xxx line is booked M81. A rule can name a rate, as in `vat: 81` or `vat: 8.1`, and the account decides the code. A rule that names a code, as in `vat: I81`, still works and gets the letter of its account. Entries that people booked and closed quarters do not change.
- A payment provider's payout is booked per order when its order documents are linked (issue #26). The match step reads an order page's facts (number, what the customer paid, the delivery country, its tax line, and what went into or came off which payout) and links the order pages to the one payout they add up to, within three days of the date they name. The booking step then books that payout in code: each order's gross on the sales account, at the firm's sale code when delivered in Switzerland or Liechtenstein and at V0 when delivered abroad, a refund deducted from the payout with the code's minus, and what the provider kept as its fees, all by the payout rule's accounts (`fees`) or the firm's payout answer. The lines add up to the payout. Orders that do not add up to it, or an order whose tax line disagrees with its delivery country, book nothing: the payout waits under Aufgaben with the reason («Bestellungen der Auszahlung: …»), as without its documents. The `rebook-shopify` and `extract-shopify-orders` commands are gone (issue #52): their per-order booking is this step now, with no account of one company in the app. Payouts they split in older books stay as they are.
- **⚠ figures** The booking applies the app's own VAT knowledge in code, not only as text for the AI (issue #45). On a line the AI booked, the input-tax code now follows the receipt rules of `processes/booking.guide.yaml#purchase_vat`: a rate code with no receipt, or with receipts that show no Swiss VAT, becomes the zero code of its account (I0, M0); a zero code beside a receipt that shows one Swiss rate on the line's whole amount becomes that rate's code (I81, M81). Where a receipt taxes only part of a payment (a customs invoice, a mixed cart) or the AI split the payment into several lines with codes, the AI's codes stand. A public business file can state a fact the app carries out, as `apply:` on a rule of its `booking.guide.yaml#purchase_vat`: Shopify's payouts claim no input tax on their fees (the monthly bill does, `pv-shopify-fees`). Whether a receipt that could not be read counts as Swiss VAT is the company's choice, not a fact of the business: `apply: { unreadReceipt: 8.1 }` goes only in the company's own file of the business (`knowledge/suppliers/<id>.yaml`), and without it an unread receipt decides nothing. The shipped Coop Pronto file states facts only, and neither it, the AI's instructions nor AGENTS.md assume 8.1 % for an unreadable Coop Pronto receipt any more. Such a booking says which rule decided and is marked as the app's check («die Vorsteuer folgt dem Beleg …»). The company's word goes first: a payment one of its rules decides never reaches this, and a business whose file in `knowledge/` has purchase_vat rules of its own keeps the AI's codes. A firm on the net tax rate method or not registered books its purchases without a code as before. `npm run check-processes` checks every `apply:`. Filed quarters are untouched: only new bookings of open periods, so the next booking run can move a payment's input tax between I81 and I0 (Ziffer 400/405); the demo goldens are unchanged.
- Two new areas in the dock, Geschäftspartner (G P) and Wissen (G E) (docs/0.4.0/knowledge.md). Geschäftspartner lists the businesses the company works with: its own partner files, the public businesses its payments route to, and the customers of Rechnungen (one partner, two views: a customer in Rechnungen links to its partner page). A partner's page leads with its notes, prose the person writes and edits in place («Bearbeiten», «Speichern», «Rückgängig» in the toast; saved at once, with who changed them last from the history), then the rules the app carries out for it with how often they booked and «Ausschalten», then its bookings, receipts, payments and invoices of the open year, each a link, and, folded, how the app recognises it and what its files say. Wissen holds the pages of Arbeitsweise (one prose page per topic, «Neue Seite»), Lücken (who decided the open year's bookings, the payments that belong to no partner with «Regel anlegen», the open questions per partner) and Von der App (the public layer, read only). Notes live in `knowledge/<role>/<id>.md` beside the partner's file and `knowledge/company/<topic>.md`; `npm run check-processes` names notes of no partner and topics without a title or with a step that does not exist. The demo ships a few.
- A sentence of a partner's notes can become a rule the app carries out (docs/0.4.0/knowledge.md, issue #10 part 1). Pointing at a sentence offers «Als Regel vorschlagen»: a small form starts with the account, VAT code and private share the sentence names, «Vorschau» drafts the rule into the partner's file for the usual card, and «Die KI fragen» hands the sentence to the AI sidebar instead. Code, not a confidence score, decides whether it is offered: a rule for one partner or payer that no rule switched on already says, whose payments of the open year are booked today as it would book them. A sentence that leaves the choice open («meistens», «je nach Beleg») is offered only as an ask rule («Als Frage-Regel entwerfen»), one whose payments are booked otherwise stays a note, and the card says why; «Behalten» refuses what code does not offer. A kept rule quotes its sentence (`note: "…"`, `source: notes <date>`, which `npm run check-processes` checks), and the notes mark that sentence «führt die App aus», linked to the rule. When the person changes the sentence, nothing changes by itself: the rule asks «Notiz geändert: Regel anpassen?» with the sentence before and now, and «Regel anpassen» shows the rule as it is and as it would be, with «Trotzdem behalten» when this year's payments are booked otherwise. Switching the rule off or deleting it removes the mark, never the sentence. Firma › Regeln names such a rule «aus den Notizen». The booking AI does not read the notes yet (part 2).
- Automatisierung opens on an overview (Leitstand) instead of the table of runs. One sentence says what the app did with the open period's receipts («18 Belege gesammelt, 18 gebucht, 78 % davon ohne dich») and how many tasks wait; five stages, Gesammelt › Gelesen › Zugeordnet › Gebucht › Abgerechnet, each with its count and a link to where those receipts are; «Wartet auf dich» answers a task («Übernehmen») or a question with one click, through the same commands as Aufgaben; «Läuft gerade» and «Als Nächstes» show the running runs and the next scheduled starts. Every figure comes from the books: «ohne dich» counts the bookings the app made and no person changed, among those whose author the books record, and says how many it leaves out. A period other than the open one is chosen above the sentence (`/automation?period=2026-Q2`). In the list, each workflow has a status dot and its next scheduled start; workflows that cannot run yet move to «Einrichten» with how many steps are missing. The runs table is now «Läufe» (`/automation/runs`).
- The setup as a conversation (docs/0.4.0/onboarding.md). A new, empty workspace opens the AI sidebar wide on «Einrichtung», a Vanilla Accounting specialist who leads the person through the books topic by topic, as the prototype shows: the company as the setup form saved it (name, UID, legal form, VAT) with «Stimmt so» / «Etwas stimmt nicht»; how VAT is filed; from when the app books and which returns of the year were already filed; the old program, the kind of bookkeeping and who has access; what a switch needs and from whom, with the plain note that the import from the old program is a later step; the opening balances, the bank accounts and the sources of receipts as the app has them, each with its place by hand; the open year's business partners sorted into «Eindeutig», «Uneinheitlich» and «Selten», where a clear one becomes a rule draft on the usual card with «Behalten»; and a closing summary of what the app knows and where it comes from, with what is still open. The code draws every step from the person's answers and the app's settings, so the first turn shows at once, without an AI call; an answer is a click, or typed words that name it, and anything else goes to the AI, which may record the answer for the person. A VAT answer that differs from the settings waits on a card for «Übernehmen», saved through the Firma page's own checks, with «Rückgängig». A rail across the top shows each topic as the app sees it: done when a bank account has its IBAN, a source is switched on, opening balances are entered or the VAT setup is confirmed; a click opens its place by hand. The answers and the checklist stay in the workspace (`onboarding.json`), so the setup continues where it stood after a restart. Firma has «Einrichtung öffnen» / «Einrichtung fortsetzen» at the top of its list. The setup form stays as it was.

- A whole folder can be dropped on the AI sidebar: its PDFs and images, from every subfolder, become the message's attachments (at most 50); one line says what stayed behind (other file types, or more than 50).
- Web addresses in an AI answer are links: a bare https:// address or a [text](https://…) link opens in the browser. Nothing but a web address becomes a link.
- Dictation in the AI sidebar: the microphone beside «Senden» writes what you say into the message as you speak, in Chrome, Edge and Safari, in Swiss German, French or Italian, or in English. While it listens it says that the browser's online service recognizes the speech; a refused microphone or an unreachable service shows one line. Firefox has no speech recognition, so no microphone there; the Mac app gets its own on-device recognition next.
- Files go to the AI sidebar as attachments, not only to the receipts. Dragged over the window, a file shows two targets: «Als Beleg ablegen» over the page (as before) and «An den Chat anhängen» over the open sidebar; with the sidebar closed, a narrow «An die KI» edge on the right opens it on a new conversation with the file attached. A screenshot pasted with ⌘V and «+» › «Datei anhängen …» attach too. Images (PNG, JPG, HEIC, GIF, WebP) and PDFs up to 20 MB show as chips with × beside the references; another type or a bigger file is refused with why. Attachments are kept beside the conversation in `data/assistant/attachments/<conversation>/` (never in the books or the inbox) and the AI reads them by path; a sent message shows its files, each with «Als Beleg übernehmen», which hands a copy to the drop, so a receipt dropped on the chat by mistake still reaches the inbox.
- Belege shows how receipts come in. With no receipt yet, the list offers the three ways as cards («Hierher ziehen» with «Dateien wählen», «Mit dem Handy» with «Handy koppeln», «Automatisierung» with «Mit KI einrichten» or «von Hand») and why a bank statement helps («Kontoauszug einlesen →»); the detail column shows the empty table. Once receipts are there, three slim rows sit above the list: the drop row (click to choose files), the phone with the Mac app's access as it is (not paired, «an · nimmt Belege an», or amber «aus · nimmt nichts an» with «Einschalten»; under `npm start` the scan page), and «Automatisierung» (the automation area's lightning) with each source's state, how often it fetches and when it last did («info@… · täglich · zuletzt heute 07:00»), the last «Belege holen» (✓ when, ✗ how many of how many sources failed, or «läuft …») and the button that starts it. Opened, it lists the sources (one that needs its password or is not switched on in amber), how many of the period's payments have a receipt with «n ohne Beleg →» into Buchungen, «Letzter Lauf» to its run, «Weg hinzufügen» and «Alle Wege». A line under the rows says only what is happening: files being read, or tasks waiting under Aufgaben. No reminders. «Handy koppeln» takes two steps: the receipt app's download (QR code and link; the App Store link is a placeholder until the app is published) and the pairing code of Firma › Handy, which it switches on. «Weg hinzufügen» asks the address, looks up who hosts its mail (the domain's MX records), asks where the mail is read, and proposes reading the mailbox (Firma › Quellen's form, filled in with the address and the server) or a forwarding filter to an address of its own; what the app cannot do yet (Apple Mail, Outlook, signing in with Google or Microsoft, webmail, customer portals, a watched folder) shows faded in «Alle Wege». «Mit KI einrichten» opens the KI sidebar with the request as its first message.
- The AI sidebar sets up receipt collection itself instead of sending the person to the settings. Asked «Welche Belegwege habe ich eingerichtet?», it answers from what is really set up (each source switched on or not, its password stored or missing, how often it fetches: a mailbox daily, checked every hour; the phone). Asked «Richte meinen Belegeingang ein», it follows the new guide step `setup` of processes/receipt-collection.guide.yaml: the person is taken as non-technical, the AI finds out first how they read their mail and then proposes what fits (next entry), and connecting the mailbox's server is the way «für Fortgeschrittene». When the person takes it, the AI drafts the mailbox (the server found from the domain's MX records) and a card under its answer has the password field (written straight into the keychain store, never into the chat, the transcript or the model), «Verbindung prüfen» and «Einschalten», the same as Firma › Quellen; «Rückgängig» takes the source, its password and its switch away. The AI names the app's places in the UI's language («Firma › Quellen», not «Sources»).
- The AI's receipt-collection setup finds out first and proposes after, instead of offering the ways in a fixed order. Before its first word it reads what the app can see by itself, sent with the message: the computer, on a Mac the mail programs installed and the one macOS opens for an e-mail link (a web browser there points to webmail), the company's mail domains (its sending address, connected mailboxes, the address its suppliers write on several receipts) with who hosts them, and how receipts came in so far (phone, each source), as counts and domains only, never an address or a text, and without any permission prompt. It opens with what it found in a sentence or two and one question («Wo liest du deine Geschäftsmails …?»), asks only what that leaves open, then proposes the way that fits with its reason. Apple Mail is recommended only with a sign (set as the Mac's mail program, or the person says so); on a Mac without other signs it may be asked as a guess. What the app can do is listed without a ranking, and the Mail app's way only where it runs (macOS); the person connects it with «Mail-Programm auf diesem Mac» in Firma › Quellen.
- A button that starts a chat for one purpose («Mit KI einrichten», «Einrichten» above Belege, the AI button in «Weg hinzufügen») starts a new conversation without the page's chip: the receipt open in the detail no longer goes with the request or with the answers after it, until the person adds the page with «+». Such a conversation gets only its own context (for the setup: what collects now and what the app can see), and the AI stays on its purpose: no open tasks tacked on at the end. Opening the chat plainly (⌘J, the bot button, «Mit KI ändern…» on an object) works as before.
- With no source at all, the «Automatisierung» row above Belege says only «Noch keine Quelle» with «Einrichten», which opens the AI setup: no last run and no coverage line. The rows show their status on a second line, so nothing is cut off in a narrow list.
- The AI's receipt-collection setup never says a way is «coming» or does not work. It proposes the most preferred channel that fits the person and is available here (a provider's API, the mailbox's server, the Mail app on this Mac, a browser session last) and makes it work with them: it walks them through an app password by their provider's page names, or writes a source for them. A person who reads Gmail in the browser gets the mailbox's server, which reads the same mail.
- The AI writes a source with the person, on any channel. It drafts the source's folder (source.yaml, and its own code when no code of the app fits) and names it in its answer. The app checks it, refuses one that does not load or holds a password or key, takes it into Firma › Quellen waiting for «Einschalten», and shows a card under the answer: what the source may reach in plain words, the field for its password or key, «Prüfen», «Einschalten», and «Rückgängig». Apple Mail is connected this way: the source binds the app's Mail code to the account. Nothing for one provider is built into the app.
- «Prüfen» runs any source without delivering anything and lists the documents it would bring («kommt in den Eingang», «schon da», «kein Beleg»), or says why it could not look: a password or key is missing, a sign-in is needed, the page was not as expected, the source reached outside its reach, or Chrome is missing. It is on the source's card and in Firma › Quellen. When the Mail app has no account of the name given, «Prüfen» lists the accounts it has.
- A source can read pages in its own browser window (io.web: open, read, list, click, download) and a provider's interface with a token (io.api, GET only). The app checks every navigation and download against the source's reach before anything leaves the Mac. It never types, sends a form or deletes. A sign-in page becomes the task «Bei … anmelden», whose «Anmelden» opens the source's window, and the person signs in there as always. Every browser run keeps what it saw (steps, pages, screenshots) in ~/.vanilla-accounting/traces, outside the books.

- Reports the owner asks for, in words or by hand. In the AI sidebar, «alle Spesen im Q3 nach Konto» makes a report: the AI writes what it shows (a spec: period, sections over bookings, payments, receipts or invoices, filters, grouping, columns, a comment with placeholders), never a figure; the app runs it on the books and shows a card with the first rows, every total, «PDF», «CSV» and «Bericht speichern». Words of the answer that state a figure the app did not compute are withdrawn. Bilanz & ER › Eigene Berichte lists the saved reports (`knowledge/reports/<name>.report.yaml`, the spec without figures), runs each for its own period or the page's with one click, and edits, deletes (with «Rückgängig») and makes them in a form without the AI. Änderungen lists them until saved. The PDF carries the period, the filters in words, the totals, the comment's figures and the note on the VAT the amounts hold (their net is what Bilanz and ER show); the CSV is the plain table. Getting started: «Your own reports».
- Firma › Regeln has «Neue Regel»: a rule made by hand, without the AI, for a business, a payer (IBAN or name) or a text, optionally with direction and amount, booked on an account with one of the firm's VAT codes (no VAT field for a firm not registered). «Vorschau» shows the same card as the AI's draft, with the payments of the open year it matches and «Trotzdem behalten» for a broad rule; «Behalten» saves it like any rule, in Änderungen and with its undo. The task for an unbooked payment links to the form, started from that payment. Persona finding r8k-4.

- A mailbox is connected in the app: Firma › Quellen › «Postfach verbinden» takes the address, the IMAP server, the login and the app password. It writes the mailbox's source (every sender, the inbox, daily), which waits to be switched on like any source, and keeps the password in the keychain store, never in the books. A source's password is set or replaced on its row. «Verbindung prüfen» signs in and lists which of the last 60 days' mails the source would read, without fetching anything. The Mac app keeps the source and its password and checks the connection, but does not fetch mail by itself yet, and says so on the page; `npm start` and `npm run pull` do. Persona finding r5h-27.
- Belege lists what lies in quarantine (`data/quarantine/`): each file with why it is there (Newsletter, AGB, Versandbestätigung, Preisliste, set aside by hand), «Ansehen», and «Ist ein Beleg», which puts it back into the inbox and has it read. Persona finding r5h-28.
- Aufgaben lists the receipts that may be a copy of another, each with «Ist dasselbe» and «Verschieden». Persona finding r5h-34.
- A firm not registered for VAT is warned before the registration threshold, and gets a task once it is passed. The MWST page warns from 90 % of the threshold reached, or from 90 % projected at the year's pace (the turnover so far scaled to the whole year, once a quarter of the year has passed), with the figures: reached, percent, projected. From the threshold on, Aufgaben carries the task «MWST-Pflicht: der Umsatz {year} hat die Grenze erreicht» with what follows (liable from the next 1.1., gross until then, registration under Firma › MWST after the year close) and a link to the MWST page; it stays until the year close. Persona finding r5f-9.

- Two more kinds of Swiss firm keep their books here. A firm **not registered for VAT** (the freelancer preset, or «nein» at setup) books every purchase and sale gross: no VAT codes anywhere, no return, no VAT questions; the MWST page says so and shows the calendar year's turnover against the CHF 100'000 registration threshold. A firm on the **Saldosteuersatz** (net tax rate method) names its one or two approved rates at setup (`--net-rate 4.5:Schreinerei`, or in the desktop form), books its sales gross with F1 (F2 for the second rate), its purchases without input tax, Bezugsteuer in 383; its returns are half years (`2026-H1`), the MWST page shows form 0553's figures, the filing workflow writes the eCH-0217 return with each rate's activity code (Firma › MWST), closes and seals by half year, and a correction carries `corrects: "2026-H1"`. The setup is one block of belege.workspace.yaml (`company.vat`) that Firma › MWST shows and changes; registration, method, period and rate hold for whole years, so a change is refused while the open year has entries. Demo books of each kind: `demo-coiffeur` and `demo-schreinerei`. The effective method's figures are unchanged. Persona requests «Coiffeur Salon Bella» and «Schreinerei mit Saldosteuersatz».

- Questions between the accountant and the owner, without e-mail. «Frage stellen» on a payment, a receipt or a booking: who asks, to whom, the question, and whether the answer is «privat» or «geschäftlich». The other person finds it under Automatisierung › Aufgaben («Fragen an dich»), counted on the dock's badge and in the status corner, and answers with one click or a few words; the asker sees it «beantwortet» under «Deine offenen Fragen» until «Erledigt». «privat» books the payment on the owner's account without VAT code and is kept as a precedent (`knowledge/rules/booking.guide.yaml`, step `private`), so the next such question about the same counterparty is answered and booked at once; «Zurücknehmen» takes both back. The questions travel with the books folder: `transactional-data/<year>/questions.jsonl`, one line per event, appended only. The KI sidebar can put such a question too (change `question`). A precedent answers only when it names one counterparty for certain: the receipt's VAT number, else its vendor, else a bank text that is not generic («Karte 5412 Einkauf», «TWINT» never answer for anyone). Persona finding treuhaender-25, -37.
- A customer payment without an invoice is asked about once. For books registered for VAT, the booking step asks the owner about each incoming payment it booked as revenue without VAT and with no receipt or invoice: «mit MWST (8.1 %)», «ohne MWST (Ausland)» or «keine MWST darauf (kein Umsatz: Darlehen, Rückzahlung …)». The app proposes one: the normal rate for francs from a Swiss or Liechtenstein IBAN, the export code for a foreign currency or IBAN. The answer books the payment so (a filed quarter, a payment with a receipt or an invoice are never changed) and is kept as a rule for that payer, keyed by its IBAN or its name (Firma › Regeln), so its next payment is booked without a question; a payer the bank names only by «Gutschrift» is asked again. The warning stays until the question is answered. The bank import now keeps the other party's IBAN of a payment in an open period. Decision 2 of the overnight log of 15.10.2026.
- **⚠ action** (books set up before this version): add the line `vanilla-accounting/transactional-data/*/questions.jsonl merge=union` to the `.gitattributes` at your repository's root, so questions asked in two clones merge without a conflict. `npm run init` writes it for new books.
- The KI sidebar knows the whole app and does what its areas do. It carries a map of every area, every Firma section and the export dialog, by the names you see, and no longer says the app writes no invoices or has no payment term. From the sidebar it now writes an invoice draft (for a customer, or a new one), sets the payment term and the invoice texts per language (Firma › Rechnungslayout, at once; the term also for every customer who had the old one), adds or renames an account of the chart (before the bookings that need it, in the same answer), sets a receipt aside as a duplicate or no receipt (its links go, its payment is booked again), packs an export preset such as the Treuhänder-Paket for download, and books again what is open through the inbox run. Each shows as a card with «Zeigen» and, where it can be taken back, «Rückgängig». Wishes test w07, w12, w13, w15, w16, w18.
- Firma › Rechnungslayout has «Zahlungsfrist in Tagen» after setup too; a new term also goes to every customer who had the old one.
- A rule's card shows what the rule would do before you keep it: the payments of this year it matches, the newest five with the booking it would make and their booking today, a changed rule as it was and as it would be, and the other rules that meet the same payments. A rule that recognises payments only by single words («Werbung»), without a business, payer or amount, is kept only with «Trotzdem behalten». Wishes test wishes3-2, -4.
- A rule can book a private share: `then: { account, vat, private: 50 }` books half on the owner's account without VAT and the rest with its VAT code. After you split a payment so (by hand or in the KI sidebar), «Immer so?» drafts that rule for the business or the payer. Wishes test wishes3-6.
- Firma › Regeln counts how often an ask rule asked («1× gefragt»). Wishes test wishes3-12.
- A rule can book a card terminal's payout as what it is («bei SumUp immer so»): `then: { account: "3400", vat: V81, fees: { account: "6840", vat: I0 } }` books the gross sales at the rule's code, a refund at its minus and the provider's fees apart, in the figures Claude reads from the payout report linked to the payment; the accounts and codes are the rule's, whatever the reading proposed. Without a report the whole payout is booked as sales until the report is linked; with a report and no Claude the payment waits. The KI sidebar drafts such a wish as this rule, not in sentences it cannot keep. Finding corner-39.
- A booking says who decided it. Buchungen › Herkunft names the rule that booked it (with the way to Firma › Regeln), the app's own check that set a sale's code, the model that booked it, or a person and the day she changed it; «Warum» quotes the rule's line, the app's words or Claude's, each as its own, and after a change by hand it says what was changed («MWST V0 → V81»). Bookings carry this as `by` from now on; older ones read as before. «Rückgängig» after a change by hand puts back who decided and why. Overnight log item 25, corner-5.
- The KI sidebar's message field works like VS Code's chat input: one bordered field with the references as chips on top, the text, and a bar with «+» and a round send button (an arrow, muted while the text is empty). «+» opens a search over what a message can refer to: this page's item (on or off), a receipt, a booking or a payment of the books (by any word or the amount), a workflow or run (what «@» offers). Arrows, Enter and Escape work throughout. The header's «Gespräche» is now the button «Verlauf» with a clock; it opens the list «Vergangene Gespräche» and, pressed again, goes back to the conversation. Open, the sidebar's toggle at the top right shows an arrow «KI-Spalte schliessen» instead of a second robot.
- A mail channel for the Mail app on the Mac, with nothing to type and no password (macOS only), as the foundation for sources a company's AI sets up (ADR 0015); nothing in the app sets one up yet. A source on it reads the account's inbox daily, from where it stopped, and puts every attached PDF into the inbox (photos too when the source's `settings.photos` is on), each once. Mail not open, the permission refused, or the account renamed becomes the task «{Quelle}: Mail-Programm prüfen» instead of an error. It runs in the Mac app too. Under the hood a new mail channel `mac-mail` beside IMAP, with the reference source `sources/mail-client/` a company's own source starts from (docs/0.4.0/mail-channels.md).
- Documents that are no receipt, such as a carrier's sales brochure, no longer reach the books as receipts (issue #186). A document whose words decide nothing and that shows at most one sign of an invoice is asked of the AI once, when it is first read, on the provider you chose (the new step «Was ein unklares Dokument ist», fast tier). The AI reads the first 3000 characters of the text and answers receipt or no receipt, with a one-line reason; it can only set a file aside, never type it otherwise (a customs decision or an order confirmation stays a receipt); for a longer text the file's page says that the AI read only the first 3000 of its characters. «No receipt» hides the file under «Keine Belege» with that reason; nothing is deleted, and «Ist doch ein Beleg» takes it back. Without an AI, or when the call fails, the document stays a receipt as before. A kind you gave is never asked about.
- Belege › «Keine Belege» says, for each file, why it is no receipt and who decided: the app's rules, the AI with its reason, or you by hand. The search finds a file by the AI's reason too.
- «Immer so?» under your «kein Beleg» on a file a mailbox collected: the click shows a card with the mailbox, the sender, how many attachments came from it, and which of them the books hold as receipts. «Behalten» skips that sender in the mailbox's settings in the workspace; what came already stays. This works for a mailbox of the Mail app too.
- «Im Terminal» opens your terminal app (issue #210). The app finds Terminal, iTerm2, Ghostty and Warp on this Mac. With Terminal alone it opens Terminal as before; with several, the first click asks «In welchem Terminal öffnen?» with «merken» on, and Firma › KI keeps the choice beside the CLI's («Im Terminal öffnen mit», changeable there). The choice is kept on this Mac, not in the books.
- From a chat without a request of yours, «Im Terminal» starts Claude Code or Codex with no prompt, so you type your own. Neither CLI can prefill its input, so the app's suggestion is shown under the note, with «Vorschlag kopieren». A request you typed is handed over as before.

### Fixed

- The AI that books a payment now reads your company's decisions. The precedents and the rules in words of the booking steps in `knowledge/rules/booking.guide.yaml` (account, VAT, a private share), and those in the payment's business file, never reached it: a precedent such as «anything for the old van is private» did not stop it from booking a part for the van as an expense with input tax. They now come with every booking request, beside the receipt that names the item, and decide over the AI's defaults; a decision that calls a purchase private books it on the owner's account without VAT. The receipt's items as read are listed too, so a long receipt whose text is cut still names them. At most 80 of the company's decisions go into one request, the newest; the request says how many older ones were left out. A rule the app carries out itself is not shown.
- A supplier's monthly invoice titled «Statement» (an advertising platform's statement of its charges, with its period and balances) is no longer taken for a bank statement by the drop (issue #289). It came in as a statement that asked «Bewegungen prüfen» and had to be made a receipt by hand each month, while the charges it proves waited for a receipt. A PDF is a bank statement when it names itself so and shows the account's IBAN or the columns of its movements (money out and money in: «Belastung» and «Gutschrift», «Outgoing» and «Incoming»; `movements` in documents.yaml). The same rule types a statement among the receipts.
- A bank's fee rows no longer wait for a receipt (issues #286, #290). The fee of a Wise transfer («Wise Charges for: TRANSFER-…») is now read as a fee of its transfer, as a card's fee already was: it is booked at once, no receipt is asked for it, and the transfer's booking may not carry it a second time. The statement reader knows a fee row by the id Wise gives it (`FEE-<the payment's id>`), no longer by the card's wording. A bank's own fee for the account (a camt.053 entry whose ISO bank transaction code is for charges or fees, `…/CHRG` or `…/FEES`, with no other party) is a fee row too (`type: 'bank-fee'`): booked at once, never a candidate for a receipt, and in no business's payments. A movement of amount 0 (a quarter's closing statement of nothing) is no longer sent to booking or listed as unbooked in the year close. Statements already imported take the new marks on the next rebuild of the books; a filed quarter's movements stay as they were filed.
- Last month's invoice of a subscription is no longer paired with this month's payment (issue #291). When a payment of the invoice's own day already holds a receipt, a payment 25 days or more from that day is no candidate for the invoice. Such an invoice is often a second document of last month's payment. Before, the chain paired it with this month's charge when the bank file came before this month's invoice. The payment was then booked by the wrong invoice (reverse charge instead of 8.1 % input tax), and this month's invoice stayed unlinked. A receipt that fits a payment's day can also take over a link the chain made to a receipt dated a whole period away. The payment is then booked again with the new receipt. This happens only while the link is the chain's own and every booking of the payment is the app's own. A link or a booking you made stays as it is.
- **⚠ figures** The import VAT of a customs assessment (BAZG «Veranlagungsverfügung MWST») is deducted again (issue #285). Its header is a column of labels beside a column of values, so the text read from the PDF puts the bordereau number, the country, the account, the UID and the currency between «Gesamtbetrag MWST» and the amount; the app looked only 80 characters past the label and read no total, so no assessment proved its import VAT and the deduction (M81-2, Ziffer 400) was missing. The total is now the first money value after the label on its page; a date, a UID or a tariff number is never read as one.
