A courier's invoice that names the import VAT it advanced only as «MWST.-freie Kosten» is now read as a customs invoice when it cites the customs declaration's number (postage and other invoices list VAT-free costs too, so the words alone do not count), and its «8,10 % MWST» line is the courier's VAT on its fee, added to that fee, no longer taken for import VAT. An assessment the books hold unlinked brings its deduction on the next run when the app's booking of the payment has the import VAT line (as a correction when that quarter is filed), so the open quarter's Ziffer 400 can rise; `npm run test:books` shows it.
- A legible phone photo of a receipt is read for its VAT, never stamped «keine Schweizer MWST» off noise (issue #284). The inbox's VAT step judges whether a receipt's text can show that a «MWST» is missing. A PDF's own text can. A reading of a picture can only when it holds at least three words and an amount, or twenty words. Before, any text of 20 characters or more counted, so the 28 characters of noise that an old local OCR left of a fuel receipt became «no Swiss VAT» with no AI call, and the payment would have been booked without input tax. Such a receipt now waits as «Beleg nicht lesbar» (issue #57). In the open period, the AI reads it again once, from the photo, and the VAT facts, plus the day and total if the receipt lacks them, come from that reading. A day or total the receipt already has stays as it is, since a person may have set it. A «no VAT» that an earlier run read off such noise is forgotten the same way. A receipt of a filed quarter keeps its text and its facts. «Neu lesen (KI)» forgets the VAT facts read off the old text, so the next run reads them off the new one. If the AI cannot read a photo, it answers «Nicht lesbar» instead of guessing, and the photo stays a receipt: it is not sent to the question of what kind of document it is. `npm run reconcile` reports a rate-coded booking whose linked receipt has its VAT not read in a line of its own («rate code, a receipt is linked but its VAT is not read yet»). Before, it reported such a booking as «NO receipt linked at all» or «no linked receipt shows Swiss MWST».
- A card charge that Wise paid from two balances is booked on both (issue #287). Such a charge arrives as two movements under one reference, one per balance (EUR and USD, or EUR and CHF), each with its own fee movement. Until now the inbox's run booked the receipt on the first balance only: the second movement and its fee got no entry and counted as booked, so no list showed them and the second account no longer matched the bank. Now the AI books each movement on its own bank account with its share of the receipt's VAT, and each fee on the balance it was taken from. A movement whose share would claim more input tax than the receipt shows is refused and stays open, so it is listed. On your books, the next inbox run books a movement of the open period that was left without an entry this way. «Automatisch buchen» on such a charge books all of its movements and no longer removes the booking of the other one.
- A refund inverts the code of the purchase it refunds, decided in code after the AI's answer (issue #288). Money back onto an asset account (a returned camera on 1540) no longer books a plain input-tax code that raised Ziffer 405: the side of the bank gives the minus (-I81, -I0). A refund from the merchant of a booked purchase, onto that purchase's account and for its amount, takes that purchase's code with the minus (-I81, -M81), also when the refund's own document shows no Swiss VAT or the AI wrote -I0; its reason names the purchase. A refund of another amount (a goodwill, a part of an order) keeps its code, and you correct it by hand. A credit note that takes the code of the invoice it names keeps the family of the account it is booked on, so a credit note on a materials account lowers Ziffer 400, not 405. Bookings already in your books stay as they are.
- A supplier's payment whose receipt the run did not link waits for its receipt again instead of being booked on the owner's account as private (issue #201). The AI's answer books a payment onto the owner's account without a receipt only when it is paid to the owner (her outlays or her loan paid back: her name stands in the payment's counterparty or text). Any other payment it would put there waits, and becomes your task two weeks before the return is due, so the missing receipt and its input tax are not hidden. A private booking still comes from a rule or from you.
- A fee the bank debits as a movement of its own (a card charge's fee) counts as booked only by its own booking (issue #42). Before, it counted as booked once its card charge was booked, although the charge's booking never carries it (#24), so a fee the run failed to book left Aufgaben and Kontoauszüge and the account was off by the fee with nothing to show it. An open fee is now listed among the open payments and keeps a row of its own on its statement, where it can be booked; a booked one is folded into its charge's row as before. A fee the bank pairs with its charge by the bank's id instead of the ref is folded too.
- `npm run check-closed` reads its git baseline for books in `vanilla-accounting/` of a repository (issue #117). It found no closed record at HEAD there («0 baseline / 39 current») and reported every closed entry, payment and link as added. Books whose folder is the repository's root were checked right before and still are.
- A narrow window with the AI sidebar open keeps the detail readable (issue #34). The detail column is at least 420 px wide; the list narrows to its compact 280 px first, then the sidebar gives up width down to 320 px (520 px for the setup conversation). With still less room (below about 1070 px with the sidebar open, 1270 px in setup, with the narrow dock) the sidebar lies over the page's right edge with a shadow instead of squeezing it, the list compact; ⌘J or its arrow closes it and the page has the whole window. Before, at 1100 px the detail was about 300 px wide, and 210 px to 260 px in setup. Selecting a row moves nothing.
- A bank's camt.053 and its monthly PDFs dropped together (issue #273). A camt.053 of an IBAN no account carries yet goes to the one account of the bank it names, without a question. Its row says «der Bank zugeordnet: <Konto>, IBAN dort gespeichert», the IBAN is kept on that account, and «ändern» takes the statement back out and asks which account it belongs to. The question comes when the file names no bank of the books, or that bank's account or folder holds another IBAN. Being the only account of its currency still without an IBAN places no file: a second bank's camt.053 is asked, with that account as the proposal, instead of landing on another bank's account. A camt.053 of an IBAN an account carries goes straight onto it. A statement PDF of that IBAN in the same drop is the account's statement without a question, whether it came before or after the camt.053 and also when the person answered the camt.053's question without keeping the IBAN. Its «Bewegungen prüfen, dann Einlesen» closes in the open dialog with that answer.
- Kontoauszüge shows a bank export over several months by month (issue #273). A camt.053 is no monthly statement. Its period is the bank's, which can begin at the account's opening, years before the books. Konto and Monat show one row per month: the month's PDF when there is one, marked «auch im Bank-Export», else the export's part of that month («aus dem Bank-Export»), with that month's payments and balances. A month an export covers is never «fehlt». The detail links to the export, which «Datei» lists whole. An export the bank dates one day into the next month (a quarter's export «bis 01.07.») no longer shows a one-day row «01.07.2026 – 01.07.2026» holding all of its payments. No export reaches back before the books' first year.
- The VAT consistency audit (`npm run reconcile`, and the end of `npm run classify`) names no vendor (issue #264). A 0-code line beside a receipt with Swiss VAT is no missed deduction when it is booked on the account the bank's own fee movements are booked on: a bank fee is an exempt financial service, and the receipt beside it is the paid merchant's. This holds for any bank and replaces the match on «Wise» and fee words in the line's text. The exception for Shopify's webshop fees is gone: since #203 they are booked from the payments export without input tax, so it matched nothing. So is the exception for Coop Pronto: its fuel lines at 8.1 % with no receipt linked, or with one that shows no Swiss VAT, are now listed like any other business's, so you can link the receipt or name other proof. The audit only reports; no booking and no figure changes.
- A dropped receipt or bank statement holds the app up for a shorter time on a full year's books (issue #258, in part). Every read of the books listed the year folders again for each receipt and parsed every file anew, about 30 times per dropped file. A read now lists the year folders once, a read of files unchanged since the last one copies what that one parsed, and a run reads the books only where a step needs them. A PNG screenshot becomes a PDF through macOS's sips in a process of its own, as an iPhone photo does, instead of half a second inside the app. On invented books of a full year (1,600 entries and receipts, AI off), the longest stall while 20 files are dropped fell from 4.4 s to 2.0 s and while a camt.053 of 60 payments is read from 9.2 s to 3.1 s. Matching a receipt against 1,500 others is most of what remains.
- Matching a dropped receipt no longer compares it with every receipt and payment of the year (issue #258). The search for a copy looks only at receipts near its day, with its text or with one of its rare numbers, and reads each receipt's text once while the books keep it. The search for a payment looks only at payments within the day bounds, of the receipt's amount or in another currency. The matches are the same as before: `npm run eval:pairing` on a real year's books gives the same counts. On invented books of a full year (1,600 entries and receipts, AI off), the matching part of each intake run fell from 610 ms to 155 ms, and while 20 files are dropped the longest stall fell from 1.4 s to 1.0 s and the time the app is blocked from 22 s to 12 s.
- A PNG that sips cannot read still becomes a PDF, as before sips was used, and sips stops after 30 s (issue #258).
- No vendor left in the app's code (issue #260). Which account a payment provider's sales wait on is the `provider.clearing` its company file names, for every provider, never a fixed 1093: a booking on it keeps its order's date (on both bases, also in the AI sidebar), and the sidebar learns it from the provider's file. The Shopify monthly input-tax correction is no longer a classifier rule (no payment carries it); it stands in `processes/suppliers/shopify.yaml`. `npm run evidence` keeps a provider's order pages and payouts apart by the provider files (it no longer links order pages to payouts, which the provider way forbids), and `npm run board` lists the businesses whose files say how to fetch their receipts. No figure changes.
- A payment provider's order page no longer waits for a payment once the provider's sales come from its order export (issue #253). With `provider.since` and `reports.sales` set, a page of one order dated on or after the cut-over day (known by its read order facts or the provider's `orderPage`) says «Bestellseite von Shopify: braucht keine Zahlung. Wartet auf den Bestellexport …» instead of «Noch keine Zahlung», offers no «Mit Zahlung verknüpfen», is not in «Braucht dich» and counts in no badge. Neither code nor the AI pairs it with a bank payment. Once the export books its order, the page is linked to that sale, also a page known only by `orderPage`; while the export holds the order back, the page says so and leads to the export. An order the export reached past without booking (paid by bank transfer, say) is a receipt like any other again. The drop row says such a page is not needed with the provider way. A page known only by `orderPage` counts only when it is the provider's own: no invoice, payout or report, and named as that provider (its name in the first lines, its UID or its file name), or typed a sales record that no other provider's `orderPage` fits; so a supplier's invoice whose line begins with «#2001» is neither kept from its payment nor linked to the sale of order 2001. Two shops on the same platform each keep their own pages and sales; a page no single shop can be named for is no order page. Derived on every read: nothing is stored, the books keep their format.
- The fix of #247 now holds for order pages read by an earlier version too (issue #249). Such a page has no read order number, so a customer's bank transfer with it linked still counted as the provider's payout and held every order and fee up to its day. A provider's app file now says how its order page begins (`provider.orderPage`, a pattern whose first group is the order number; Shopify's: the order's name «#1001» on a line of its own, below the browser's header lines when printed from the browser), and code reads the order number from the page's text. Nothing in the books changes and nothing needs reading again.
- A bank account added under Firma › Bankkonten now counts for the runs at once (issue #246). The runs of Automatisierung kept the bank accounts and settings the app had when it first looked at its runs, until it restarted: a statement read into the new account could land on another account of its currency, because the inbox run read the statement folders with the old list.
- A provider's sales report no longer holds every order up to the day of a customer's bank transfer whose order page is linked to it (issue #247). A payment now counts as the provider's payout only by the provider's own recognition (its reference, payment processors and `match`), never because an order document is linked to it. A payment whose linked order document names an order the report counts as paid otherwise («anders bezahlt») holds nothing, and a payout booked from the clearing account holds nothing because of a correction of a filed period (`corrects`) on the same payment. A real payout booked as sales still holds every order up to its day, so no sale is booked twice.
- A provider's sales report no longer holds a whole quarter for one correction (issue #242). A person's correction of a filed period (`corrects`) now holds only the orders it names. Any other entry that names an order with another amount than its sale, or several orders, still holds every order up to its day, as does one that names no order, so no sale is booked twice beside it. An order edited after checkout and charged on several days gives the same books whichever export is dropped first: a sale the app booked in one sum in the open period is split onto the charge days once the payments export is in (never a person's sale, a filed one, or one a person linked a receipt to; all parts or none). Each charge's fee is booked on its own charge day; a fee an older build summed onto one day stays as booked. A filed order whose refunds are filed too is counted as filed instead of being listed as waiting for refunds beyond its refunded amount. A refund of a filed period that the books already carry anywhere (on revenue or fees naming its order, paid from the bank directly at a sale code's minus, as a correction after it, or netted into its sale) is no longer offered as «Als Korrektur buchen»: the report's receipt says it is booked already and links the entry, and the click refuses it.
- A provider's sales report no longer holds an order charged a cent more than its total (issue #239). Shopify's order export writes such a cent of a currency's rounding as an `Outstanding Balance` of -0.01: the sale is now the total plus that cent, its tax in the same share, so it matches the payments export's charge, whichever report is dropped first. Charges a cent beside what the order says was charged are booked at the charges, and the entry's reason says so; more than a cent still holds the order. An order of a filed period is counted as filed instead of being listed as waiting for its charges. A refund of a filed period whose sale an older entry books alone with its amount (the old way per order) is offered as a correction like one whose sale the report booked; one whose sale sits in an old entry that names other orders too (a payout booked as sales) is named on the report's receipt and in the drop row, «prüfen und von Hand als Korrektur buchen», without the button, since that entry may net the refund already.
- **⚠ action** A provider's sales report no longer books an order edited after checkout at its full total (issue #231). Shopify's order export writes what was not charged yet in `Outstanding Balance` (the column `outstanding` of a provider's file): the sale is now the total less it, its tax in the same share, a foreign VAT too. With the payments export in, an order's charges must add up to that; if they do not, the order waits («Belastungen stimmen nicht mit der Bestellung überein») and nothing of the difference is booked. On the cash basis each day an order was charged is booked on its own day, so a balance charged in a later period is booked then, as its own part; a sale booked in one before its charges were known stays as booked. A refund paid in a filed period that the books never carried, of a sale they carry, is no longer only counted: the drop row and the report's receipt name it («Rückerstattung vom 09.11. … gehört in die eingereichte Periode …: als Korrektur buchen?»), and «Als Korrektur buchen» books it on the first open day with `corrects` naming that period, so it is declared in that period's correction return (golden rule 1). A later sales report that repeats a sale of a filed period no longer fails on linking it. Action: a sale booked before at its full total stays as booked; correct it by hand where the order had an outstanding balance.
- «Verlauf» on a detail shows of a batch run only what concerns the object (issue #233): on a receipt itself, its links and the entries of its linked payments; on a payment its entries, links and linked receipts; on an entry itself and its payment's links. The rest of the run is counted («und 17 weitere Belege, 25 Buchungen in diesem Lauf · Lauf öffnen»), and a run that wrote the receipt or the entry says so («hat den Beleg gelesen», «hat die Buchung geschrieben»). The run view in Automatisierung still lists everything.
- **⚠ action** A refunded order's sale is no longer booked while its refund waits, and a test-mode order is no longer booked as a sale (issue #214). Shopify's order export names the refunded amount but not the day, so the sale was booked and the refund held, and a fully refunded order counted as a sale of the open quarter. Now a refund is dated by the provider's payments export: its refund rows (`type` «refund», with `amount`, in Shopify's file `Type` and `Amount`) give the day the money went back, each day its own entry, an export's refund split as its sale (#206). While a refund has no day, its sale waits with it («Rückerstattung ohne Tag»), and a fully refunded order without a refund row stays held. Only a row of the type «refund» is a refund (a «refund_failure» is none); refunds dated beyond the order's refunded amount, or a refund the order export says is 0, hold the order («Rückerstattungen übersteigen den erstatteten Betrag»), nothing of it booked. Once a payments export is in, an order of the provider's gateway not booked yet, of a day strictly inside the days the export's charges cover, that no row names was never collected (a test-mode order): it waits («keine Zahlung im Zahlungsbericht»). The first and last day of an export never count, since an export cut mid-day or holding one payout of several covers them only partly, and an export of refunds only covers no day. A sale booked already is never taken back by code: it stays, and the app asks you on that booking «Keine Zahlung im Zahlungsbericht: Verkauf zurücknehmen?»; if it was never paid, you delete or reverse it by hand. So only such an order books differently depending on which report came first. A `test` column in either report, where an export has one, flags rows that are no sale, fee or refund. Each drop of a payments export takes the provider's sales reports again, and a re-drop changes nothing. «Rückgängig» on a payments export takes the sales reports again without it: what it held is booked, and the app's own refunds of the open period that only it dated go back, their sales waiting for a refund day again (a refund you linked a receipt to stays). Action: drop the provider's payments export for the days of your sales reports; a sale booked before stays as booked, and its refund is booked once the payments export dates it.
- The receipts list's heading no longer widens its column at a narrow list width (issue #211): the counts, chips and search wrap, the list column never scrolls sideways, and a browser test checks it at the narrowest width in German, French and English.
- A statement in «Dateien hinzufügen» that waits for your answer (which account, or whether an unknown account is the firm's) is listed under «Kontoauszüge» with «wartet auf Ihre Antwort», no longer under «Nicht übernommen» as «nicht gespeichert» (issue #208).
- An answer given in «Dateien hinzufügen» while further dropped files were still being read in could be lost: the statement was read in, but its row asked again, and the second answer said «… ist schon eingelesen». The dialog's requests now run one after the other. A statement that is really read in already says «schon eingelesen» with «Auszug öffnen», and the same file dropped twice in one go asks only once (issue #208).
- A courier's customs invoice that prints the import VAT as «MwSt / TVA / IVA» at code Z (0 %) is split by its positions again (issue #227). Such an invoice prints customs duty as a line of 0.00 when none was due, and the app left lines of 0.00 out before it asked whether the invoice charges duty or import VAT, so it took the invoice for an ordinary one and the model booked it whole. Now a line of 0.00 still tells what the invoice is; on a customs invoice a line that names only the tax, at no rate of its own, is the import VAT the carrier advanced, and a line no position names at the Swiss rate (a printed invoice, an extra service) is the carrier's fee (modules/ch documents.yaml `customs.tax`). The AI chat books import VAT by the same rule: it can no longer book a tax-amount code (M81-2) against the bank, nor claim input tax on the import VAT account without the customs office's assessment linked to the payment; the refusal says why. New on Aufgaben: «Veranlagungsverfügung fehlt» lists each import VAT booked without its assessment, with the declaration number, the access code and the download link the courier's invoice prints (`customs.assessment.access`). The assessment's number now also matches a declaration number with one letter after «CH». The books' and the manifest's format versions are unchanged.
- The AI sidebar's message box no longer rotates while it waits for the answer. Two animations shared one name, and once the AI setup had been opened, its spinner's turn replaced the box's glowing border.
- A kind you gave a file before its text was read (for example «kein Beleg» on a scan) is now kept when the inbox reads the file. It was dropped before.
- A folder dropped on the page is read with its subfolders (issue #188). Before, a folder arrived in the drop window as one file nothing could read; only the AI sidebar opened folders. Now every file of the folder and of its subfolders goes into the drop window, in the browser and in the Mac app (a Finder drag), and the window sorts them as usual: statements (camt.053, statement PDFs), receipts and «Nicht übernommen». Hidden files (`.DS_Store`, `.git`) stay out. At most 2000 files go per drop; past that the window says how many stayed behind and names the limit. The AI sidebar keeps its limit of 50 files per message, and its line now names that limit too: «Aus Q3 gingen 50 Dateien an die KI, 12 weitere nicht: höchstens 50 pro Nachricht.» An empty file in a dropped folder stays out and is counted («Q3: 1 leere Datei nicht übernommen.»).
- Closing the drop window while its files upload keeps what «Im Hintergrund weiter» says (issue #195). The files still uploading go on and are taken, and the drop's session closes on the server once the last of them is in. Before, the session closed at once, so the remaining files were refused, and the next drop reused the dead session: every file failed until the page was reloaded. Now the next drop opens a session of its own, and two drops made before the server answers share one session instead of leaving the first drop's files in an orphan.
- A file that fails to upload after the drop window was closed is no longer lost silently (issue #279). A line at the bottom of the page names every such file with its reason («2 Dateien nicht hochgeladen: … (…)») and stays until the person clicks «Erneut ablegen», which drops the same files again into a new window, or «Verwerfen». Opened again, the drop window shows the same line. This also covers a drop whose session could not open after the window closed.
- One file that fails to upload no longer stops the files dropped after it (issue #194). Every other file goes on, and the drop window names the ones that did not go, with the reason: «1 Datei nicht hochgeladen: leer.pdf (Keine Datei erhalten.)». A new drop no longer shows the limit note of the drop before it.
- A Mac with only an API key: every AI step now runs on the key (issue #175). With an Anthropic or OpenAI key saved and no Claude Code signed in, every step still went to Claude Code unless a person set it to the key one by one in Firma › KI, while the start screen counted the key and started the setup conversation, whose words then went to the missing program. Now, when no CLI answers on this Mac, the key's provider serves every step except the KI column (which needs Claude Code or Codex); with Claude Code or Codex signed in, the key serves only the steps set to it, as before. `GET /api/desktop/ai` asks the same rule as the steps. Whether Claude Code answers means installed and signed in (`claude auth status`), not only found. A key that fails still asks a subscription, and a subscription never asks a key that costs money on its own.
- Codex uses the models your ChatGPT account offers (issue #180). The app named fixed models for its three tiers, and on a plan without `gpt-6.1-sol` every standard step (booking proposals, reading a receipt, matching, saves) failed with `model_not_found` when Codex was the only AI. The app now reads the account's models with `codex debug models` in its own Codex folder (no model call, no token read) and takes per tier the first model of its preference list the account offers: fast `gpt-6-luna`, `gpt-5.6-luna`; standard `gpt-6.1-sol`, `gpt-6-sol`, `gpt-5.6-sol`, `gpt-5.6-terra`; strong `gpt-6-astra`. A tier with none of its models takes the next lower tier's. The list is kept in `~/.vanilla-accounting/codex-models.json` and read again after a new sign-in, after a day, and with «Nochmals prüfen» in Firma › KI, which shows the model of each tier and says when one is borrowed. A step's own model choice offers only the account's models. When the account still refuses a model, the app reads the list again, keeps that model out and asks once more with the tier's next model; a second refusal says «Dein ChatGPT-Konto bietet das Modell … in Codex nicht an».
- The Mac app no longer asks to install the developer tools on a Mac without git (issue #172). On such a Mac `/usr/bin/git` is only a stub, and every run of it opens the dialog «The git command requires the command line developer tools», which offers the Command Line Tools or all of Xcode (about 18 GB). Änderungen read whether there are saves to synchronize through that program, every ten seconds, so the dialog came back as soon as it was closed. The app now reads it itself, without any git program. It runs git only to synchronize with a remote, and only when a real git is installed (Homebrew's, or the one of the Command Line Tools or Xcode); it finds out without running the stub. Without one, Änderungen say «nur auf diesem Computer: Synchronisieren braucht das Programm git», and saving works as before. `npm run doctor`, `update`, `check-closed` and `check-public` say what to install instead of opening the dialog.
- Icons on filled buttons are readable (issue #174). The AI icon on «Speichern vorbereiten» in Änderungen was drawn in the AI violet on the blue button, almost invisible. An icon on any filled button now takes the button's text colour. The tick of a checked step in Abschluss, the filled «Stornieren» of Rechnungen and the delete confirmation were white on a fill that turns light in the dark theme; they are now dark there.
- A currency account's PDF statement without an IBAN no longer stays in Belege «ohne Datum» (issue #164). A USD account has an account number, not an IBAN, and its statement prints the balance under a heading such as «USD on 31 March 2026», which no balance word matched, so only the period of the file name counted and the file stayed a receipt. A line that starts with a currency code followed by «on», «am», «per», «au» or «al» and a day now counts as a balance line, so such a statement is typed as a statement and dated on its period's last day. Statements are now typed every time the books load, not only once when they went to schema 5, so statements already in the books reach Kontoauszüge too, a filed quarter's included: only their kind and date change, their links and every seal stay. A closed year's receipts stay as they are. When a person makes a statement an ordinary receipt again, the app remembers that a person decided and never types it again. The AI sidebar now sets such a file to «Kontoauszug», which keeps its links, instead of «kein Beleg», which takes them away and is refused when a link points into a filed quarter. The books' format does not change.
- **⚠ figures** (agreed basis only) A customer's prepayment on an invoice the app issued is taxed when it is received, no longer on the invoice's date (issue #144, MWSTG Art. 40 Abs. 1 lit. c). When a payment dated before its invoice is confirmed or matched by its reference, it books its part with the invoice's VAT codes on its own date, and the invoice's VAT booking is redone for the rest, on the invoice's date. «Lösen» of such a payment gives the invoice its whole amount back. A prepayment a person booked with revenue is now kept when it is linked; before, the link was refused. The cash basis and the demos are unchanged.
- **⚠ figures** (agreed basis only) A bill or a customer invoice of a closed earlier year, paid now, is declared in the correction return (form 0535) of its own period when the firm already reported on the agreed basis then (issue #143, MWSTG Art. 40 Abs. 1). Before, every such document was treated as an open item of the switch to the agreed basis and declared when paid. The same holds for an invoice the app issued whose VAT booking was missing; its credit note counts when it is booked (Art. 41). `company.vat.reportingSince` in `belege.workspace.yaml` records the year the basis began; Firma › MWST writes it when you switch the basis and shows it («seit 2027»). Documents before that year, or before the books, remain open items of the switch (MWSTV Art. 106 Abs. 1). The manifest's schema is now 3, so an older app refuses it.
- Every button that shows only an icon now shows what it does when you hover it or reach it with the keyboard (issue #159). In the AI chat these are «+» (add a reference), the microphone, the terminal button and send; the same holds across the app, for example the receipt row's actions and the top bar's buttons. Before, these buttons carried a browser tooltip that the Mac app did not show. Now the app draws its own small label beside the button after a short moment, also on a button that is greyed out, and the explanations on other words and figures (for example the statement totals on a bank statement) use the same label.
- The `AGENTS.md` at the root of a company's repository no longer sends a terminal AI into the Mac app's bundle (issue #149). Setup wrote `cd <app> && npm start` there, with the app's path; in the Mac app that path lies inside the app, where no npm project exists, so Claude Code or Codex started in the repository tried a command that cannot run. Now the file names no path of the app: it says the books are kept in the Mac app or in a developer checkout whose `.env` points at the repository (`npm start` runs there), and it sends the AI to `vanilla-accounting/AGENTS.md` for the books' rules (#146). The app's part of the file lies between markers and is renewed like the section in `vanilla-accounting/AGENTS.md`; the company's notes below it stay the company's. Existing repositories are corrected when the Mac app opens the books, by `npm run update` and before «Im Terminal öffnen»: only when the file is the text setup wrote (0.3 or 0.4), with at most its notes changed, and the notes are kept. A file a person changed above the notes, or wrote themselves, stays as it is. The books' and the manifest's format versions are unchanged.
- The chat's microphone in the Mac app hears you with an audio interface of many inputs (issue #160). With a Focusrite Vocaster One, for example, the Mac gets ten input channels, and the app turned them into one channel in a way that left only silence: it listened and ended without a word. Now it takes, moment by moment, the loudest input, whichever one the microphone is on. Sound the Mac plays can reach the interface's loopback inputs and be heard too. A microphone that delivers nothing but silence now ends with «Vom Mikrofon kam kein Ton» and where to check the input in System Settings › Sound, instead of ending silently.
- The chat's microphone in the Mac app shows what it hears while you speak (issue #165). Before, the words appeared only after you stopped, the button pulsed the same whether it heard a voice or nothing, and that it was recording was easy to miss. Now, while it records, the button turns into a red stop button, the field gets a red border, and beside the button a red dot, the time since it began and «Aufnahme läuft» say so. A ring around the button grows and shrinks with your voice, so you see at once that the right microphone hears you; with reduced motion in System Settings › Accessibility the ring only darkens with the voice. The words appear in the field about a second after you say them, paler while the recognizer may still change them, and in full colour once it settles them. Dictation now goes where the cursor is: text you typed before and after the cursor stays in place, and after the dictation the cursor sits behind the dictated words. You can type while it records: what you type before or after the dictated words stays, and the next words go where the dictation is; change a dictated word yourself and the dictation stops there, keeping your change. Sending a message while dictating no longer puts the last words back into the emptied field. In Chrome, Edge and Safari the button keeps its pulse, and the words not yet final also show paler.
- A bank statement among the receipts is a statement, not a receipt without an amount (issue #151). A PDF statement that came in with the receipts, by mail, a drop or a collection, stayed in Belege as a receipt nobody could pay, and a filed quarter's lock kept a person from setting it aside. Now code tells a statement by its own text and file name, with no bank named in the app: it names itself («Kontoauszug», «statement») in its first lines and shows at least two of an IBAN, a period and a balance line («Saldovortrag», «Closing balance»), so an invoice that mentions a statement stays an invoice. It is typed as a statement and dated on the period's last day, from the text or else from the file name (`statement_…_EUR_2026-02-01_2026-02-28.pdf`). The inbox run does this as it reads a new file; the VAT step leaves it, nothing links it to a payment, and a day the AI reads off it does not move its date. Statements already in the books are typed when the books load, unless a person or a rule gave them a kind, and keep a date of their own. The lock on a receipt of a filed quarter is now as strict as the filed return needs. Its amount, currency, VAT facts and file stay as filed, and so do its links; its date, description, remarks and kind may change, by hand and through the AI sidebar. None of these is in a VAT return or under a seal (the seals hash bookings, payments and links), so no filed figure can change. Deleting such a receipt, putting it back, reading it again with the AI and «ist dasselbe» stay refused, because they change the evidence or its links. **Format:** the books go to schema 5; no record changes, and every seal stays as it was. An older app would show the statements as receipts again, which is harmless. `npm run update` writes the books at schema 5, with a backup in `data/backups/`. In Belege a statement is never under «Braucht dich» and counts in neither «offen» nor «ohne Datum». Kontoauszüge shows it at its account and month, when the app can tell the account from the IBAN in its text or from the bank's name and the currency: the month's row links to the file under Belege, and a month without a PDF of its own shows the statement's pages. A statement whose account the app cannot tell stays under Belege among the files that are no receipt, as «Kontoauszug», without a warning. A receipt of a filed quarter says in Belege what still changes (date, description, note, kind) and what stays; its row keeps the pencil and loses the link and the delete button.
- `npm run desktop:dev` shows the checkout's UI once books are open. The books' API serves the checkout's `dist` folder itself when there is one (`src/server/files.ts`), and the dev window asked it before Vite: with books open, every page came from that build, as old as the last `npm run build`, with the version string of today's server. The chat then lacked the microphone and the terminal button of this version. Now the dev window hands every request that is no API or books file to Vite first; the books' API sees only its own.
- Dropping several photos at once no longer leaves one «in Arbeit» for good in «Dateien hinzufügen» (found by the desktop smoke, issue #154). Each photo starts its own inbox run, and the first run turned every photo in the inbox into a PDF, also those of the runs waiting behind it. Their rows then looked for a file that was no longer there, so the dialog never reached 100 %. A run now leaves the photos of a waiting run to that run.
- Books whose owner pays in several currencies open again (issue #35). Since the money accounts of #116, `belege.workspace.yaml` was refused with «one account at most has the role owner» when the owner's account was listed once per currency (`manual` CHF, EUR and USD on 2451), the shape every workspace of schema 1 has that booked an outlay in a foreign currency. The owner is one person on one ledger account: the rule now asks only that every row with the role owner books on the same ledger account.
- The start screen says why books cannot be opened. «Zuletzt geöffnet» showed «Nicht gefunden» for any folder whose manifest the app refused, with its button dead. Now a folder that is gone is «Nicht gefunden»; a folder that is there but refused is «Kann nicht geöffnet werden» with the reader's words below it, and its button still works. Pointing at a row shows its whole path, which the row cuts short.
- The Mac app's demo from an older app no longer breaks the app. «Demo ansehen» keeps a copy in the app's data folder; a copy an app before 0.4.0 made still has `books.json` at its top, and the app showed «books.json konnte nicht geladen werden» with a line about `npm run update`. The copy is disposable: one in the old layout is thrown away and the demo copied afresh, as `npm run demo:reset` does. `npm start` does the same with its copy under `~/.vanilla-accounting/demo/`.
- Books in the layout before 0.4.0 are moved by the app itself. The Mac app opened such a folder and failed on its first read, with words meant for a terminal. Now the start screen refuses it in the person's language and offers «Bücher ins neue Format bringen», which runs what `npm run update` runs: the files move into `vanilla-accounting/` and are split by year, `books.json` stays as a backup in `vanilla-accounting/data/backups/`, no record changes and every seal holds; then the books open.
- The setup form «Neue Buchhaltung anlegen» scrolls at any window height. Its page grew with the form while the body never scrolls, so the fields below «Ausgangslage» could not be reached in a small window.
- An inbox run or a workflow run no longer stops when another process saves the books at the moment it saves (issue #132). Since #123 such a save is refused instead of overwriting, and the app's own changes ran once more, but the inbox steps and the workflow runs stopped with «Die Bücher wurden seit dem Laden von einem anderen Prozess geändert». Now their change runs once more on the books as they are now, so the payment is booked or the receipt linked on the second try, and what the other process saved stays. The second try sees that process's changes: a payment someone booked meanwhile is not booked again, and a person's booking is never replaced. Only when the books change underneath it twice in a row does the run stop, with that message as its reason. A receipt's file now leaves `beleg-import/` only with the save that marks it filed: before, a refused save left the file in the year's `receipts/` while the books still had it in `beleg-import/`. The books' format is unchanged.
- Saving the books no longer overwrites what another process saved in the meantime (issue #123). Until now a save wrote its files over whatever was on disk, so when a command such as `npm run classify` or `npm run restamp-fx`, a script or a second app saved between another's load and save, the earlier change was silently lost. Now a save first reads `ledger.json` and every file it is about to write again and compares them with what it loaded. If one differs, it writes nothing and stops with «Die Bücher wurden seit dem Laden von einem anderen Prozess geändert … nichts gespeichert»; run the command again and it reads the books afresh. From that check to its last write a save holds `data/books.lock`, so two saves never interleave; a lock left by a process that is gone is taken over. The app mostly does that by itself: a change in the app runs once more on the books as they are now, and only when the books change underneath it twice in a row does it say «Die Bücher wurden gerade von einem anderen Programm geändert … Bitte noch einmal versuchen» (409 `booksChanged`). A change that also writes outside the books (a receipt's file name, an invoice's payment, linking or unlinking a receipt, a statement import) is not run twice and says so at the first conflict. Changes another process made to other files of the books are kept. The books' format is unchanged.
- A payment the inbox run first saw without the AI is judged by the AI on the next run (issue #110). A payment out with no receipt and no rule waits for its receipt unless the AI books it onto a settlement account, such as an outlay paid back to the owner. A run without the AI marked such a payment «wartet auf den Beleg», and that mark kept every later run, and «Buchen» on the payment, from asking the AI. So an owner's reimbursement waited for a receipt that never comes, until «Beleg fehlt» two weeks before the return. The mark now says whether the AI judged the payment. A mark set without the AI is provisional: the next run with the AI asks once, books what only settles and keeps the rest waiting. A payment marked by an earlier version is asked about once the same way. A payment whose AI answer failed keeps waiting and is asked again on the next run. The books' format version is unchanged: an older app ignores the new field.
- A deleted manual movement no longer frees its number while something still names it (issue #124). A movement made by hand and a booking without payment get a ref of their own (M2026-004). The next one used to take the highest such ref in the books plus one, so deleting the newest freed its number for the next movement, and a question, a run log line, an invoice payment or a change in the AI sidebar that named the old movement then pointed at an unrelated new one. A number now counts as taken while any record of the books names it in any field (a transaction, a booking, a link, a statement, a replaced booking, a correction's text, a correction case), or a question, a run log line, an invoice's payment allocation or an AI sidebar conversation does. Existing refs do not change, and the books' format stays the same. A number that nothing names any more can still be given again.
- Saving a page of Wissen keeps its front matter as you wrote it (issue #63). A save rewrote the whole head, so `steps: [a, b]` became a block list and comments were lost. Now the head stays byte for byte; a new title changes only the title line.
- An invoice's «Herunterladen», a sent document's preview and download, and the PDF saved by «Senden» load the PDF again (issue #61). Their links named the file's place in the workspace (`/transactional-data/<year>/invoices/…`), which the app does not serve; they now use `/invoices/<number>.pdf` and `/invoices/sent/<id>.pdf`.
- A cash withdrawal no longer waits for a receipt (issue #99). A Bancomat, Postomat or ATM withdrawal only moves money onto the cash box: it carries no VAT and has no receipt of its own. Until now the app decided that from the AI's booking alone, so a withdrawal the inbox run first saw without the AI waited for a receipt that never comes, and showed up only as «Beleg fehlt» two weeks before the return was due. The app now knows the wording of a withdrawal from `processes/evidence.guide.yaml` (rule `ev-exempt-cash-withdrawal`, `apply: { receipt: none, text: "…" }`) and books it at once onto the cash box; without the AI it is yours to book by hand at once. A withdrawal already marked as waiting loses the mark on the next run. A company rule that asks about withdrawals («frag mich bei Barbezügen über 500 Franken») still asks. A company adds its own bank's wording with the same `apply:` on `knowledge/rules/evidence.guide.yaml`.
- The audit export no longer hangs on Änderungen and asks its host for nothing it cannot serve (issues #66, #67). Opening Änderungen in the export kept the page busy without end, so the browser stopped answering; it now says the page needs the app with its server. Belege, Firma (Regeln, Quellen, E-Mail), Eigene Berichte and the setup asked the host for /api/ addresses a folder of files does not have; every call to the server is now refused in the export before it is sent.
- Buchungen selects its first row again when the link names no entry (issue #62). Opening Buchungen from the dock showed the list with an empty detail; the check that keeps a just-deleted entry in place took «nothing deleted» and «nothing selected» for the same entry.
- An answer to «Kundenzahlung ohne Rechnung» books only that payment (issue #38). The answer, or a correction of the booking, used to write a rule for the payer and answer the payer's other open questions along with it. One answer on a payment provider's payout then booked every later payout of that provider as an export, domestic orders included. Now the answer writes no rule. Under the answer, «Immer so?» offers one: the app first reads the payer's other payments. When they are booked the same way, the usual rule card shows the rule, and nothing is kept until you click «Behalten». When they are booked differently, or the payer is a payment provider whose payouts are split per order, the card says the payments are mixed and drafts no rule. Rules that earlier answers kept stay, and taking such an answer back still takes its rule back.
- A rule's VAT rate needs a purchase document that shows Swiss VAT (issue #81). A rule that books I81 kept the rate when only a delivery note, a copy or the company's own sales record showing MWST was linked to the payment. The booking check of the AI's lines already ignored those documents. Now a rule's booking and its preview on the rule card do too, and fall to the zero code. Bookings already made are not changed, and the demo figures are unchanged.
- No entry is dated before the books begin any more (issue #39). A payout of 2 January whose order is dated 27 December of the year before was booked on the order date, so the app made a books folder for the previous year and that year counted the order twice. The books begin on 1 January of the first year with opening balances, or of the year of the earliest payment when there are none. A sale whose order lies before that day is now booked on the payment date, as one in a filed quarter already was. The write door refuses any new entry dated before the books begin, from a person, a module or a workflow run. An entry already there may stay, and may be moved into the books.
- A person's or the AI sidebar's booking change can no longer carry a VAT code the engine has no definition for (issue #60). The check used the catalogue's patterns, which accept Z0, V81-B or an F-code on an effective firm; no return counts such a code. It now asks the engine, as the booking run does, and refuses with «Den Steuercode … gibt es nicht». A code the firm's form does not take is still refused as before.
- The booking run's note names every payout its orders hold, each with its own reason (issue #76). It showed only the first reason, as «1 fehlgeschlagen: …», or counted the payouts as ones a rule asks about. Now it reads «2 Auszahlungen warten auf dich, ihre Bestellungen buchen sie nicht: 14.08.2026 CHF …, die Steuer auf Bestellung #1003 passt nicht zum Lieferland; …», in the reader's language. The reasons are: the orders do not add up to the payout, a tax line that does not fit the delivery country, another currency, an order in several payouts, and less than half of an order in this payout (a part payment, which now has its own words).
- An order delivered abroad is booked at V0 whatever foreign tax its order page shows (issue #74). A payout whose export orders carried the destination's VAT (an EU or UK rate the platform charged the buyer) waited for the person; it is now booked per order, each export at V0 on its whole gross, and the booking's reason names the foreign tax lines («#1004 DE 19 %»). Under Swiss VAT an export is exempt (MWSTG Art. 23) and a foreign VAT is no Swiss turnover tax. A domestic delivery without the Swiss tax line, or Swiss tax on a delivery abroad, still waits for the person.
- **⚠ action** A card fee the bank debits as a payment of its own was booked twice (#24). With Wise, the AI read «(fee: 0.14 EUR)» in the card charge's text and split it out as a Bankspesen line of the charge, and the fee's own payment (FEE-CARD-…) booked it again, so the expense came out short by the fee and Bankspesen counted it twice. Now, for any bank: the AI is told that the payment's fee is debited separately, and code checks its answer. A line of the fee's amount on the account the fee itself is booked on goes back to the expense, and the booking says why; any other line of that amount may be a real item, so then (and with several expense lines, or a VAT rate on the fee line) nothing is booked and the payment waits with «Claudes Antwort hat in der Zahlung eine Zeile über …, so viel wie die Gebühr …». Wise's habit of naming the fee in the charge's text is now a fact in processes/suppliers/wise.yaml, beside the other Wise rules that moved there from the generic guides. Bookings already made are not changed: check the card charges of the open quarter for a Bankspesen line and correct them by hand; a filed quarter is corrected only through the Korrekturabrechnung.
- The separate card fee of #24 is recognised on Wise statements read from camt.053 too (#69). Wise names those entries by its statement reference (WEUR…), so the fee was never linked to its charge and was still booked twice. The statement reader now keeps Wise's own id of each movement (CARD-…, FEE-CARD-…) beside the reference, as `bankTxId`, and the fee is found through it; the transfer references of Wise's monthly PDFs are found through it as well. It is filled in when the statements are read again (`npm run extract` or an upload) for payments of the open period; a filed quarter keeps its transactions as filed. Bookings already made are not changed: check the open quarter's Wise card charges for a Bankspesen line as #24 says.
- Claude no longer books a payment on an account your chart does not have. The booking instructions named one company's webshop revenue account (3450), which the standard chart lacks, and nothing checked the account, so a webshop sale could land on an account that appears in no statement. The instructions now name standard KMU accounts only and tell Claude to use your chart's own. A booking that still names an account outside your chart is not made: the payment stays unbooked with the reason «… auf Konto 3450, das der Kontenplan nicht hat», and you add the account under Firma › Kontenplan or book it by hand (issue #37).
- Claude no longer books a payment with a VAT code the app does not know. A model once wrote an invented code (Z0) and the booking stood with it, a code no VAT return counts. Such a booking is not made now: the payment stays unbooked with the reason «… mit dem Steuercode Z0, den die App nicht kennt», and you classify it again or book it by hand. A firm not registered for VAT, or one on the net tax rate method, keeps what it had: a code its form does not take is left off and the payment is booked without it (issue #56).
- A camt.053 that several bank accounts of its currency could take (none of them names an IBAN) was refused with «Kein Bankkonto der Firma nimmt diesen Auszug», although two accounts would. The drop dialog now asks which account it belongs to, one button per account, and can save the statement's IBAN on the chosen account so the next statement lands by itself; «Ein anderes Konto hinzufügen» and «Nein, privat» stay. A statement PDF of an unknown name asks the same way, and a statement added in the Bank area goes to the drop dialog when it needs that answer (#25).
- A statement PDF whose first lines say «CHF statement» (the monthly statement of a currency account), «Relevé de compte» or «Estratto conto» was taken as a receipt, because the drop looked for «Kontoauszug» or «account statement» and an IBAN. A PDF is now a statement when its title, in any of the four languages, comes before any receipt word; the words are in modules/ch/documents.yaml (`statement`), and an invoice that mentions a statement stays an invoice. A PDF taken for a statement that no account reads in offers «doch ein Beleg» (#25).
- **⚠ figures** A service from a supplier abroad that charged no Swiss VAT is booked with Bezugsteuer (B81-1) by code, no longer left to the AI, which booked it I0 (issue #28, processes/booking.guide.yaml#purchase_vat pv-bezugsteuer). The AI reads two facts off the receipt, whether a line buys a service, goods or a service exempt from VAT, and the supplier's country; code books B81-1 when the line is a service from outside Switzerland and Liechtenstein and every linked receipt was read and shows neither Swiss VAT nor a CHE number. On the effective method Ziffer 383 declares the tax and Ziffer 405 deducts it again, net 0; on the net tax rate method it is owed in 383 without a deduction; a firm not registered books no code. The other way round, a B81 the AI wrote is taken back to I81 when the receipt shows Swiss VAT, and to I0 or M0 for goods, an exempt service (payment processing, banking, insurance), a supplier in Switzerland or one with a CHE number. Without a receipt, or without the two facts, the AI's code stands. The booking names the check («Bezugsteuer: eine Dienstleistung aus dem Ausland ohne Schweizer MWST») and its reason. Only new bookings of open periods change, never a filed quarter or a person's booking; the demo goldens are unchanged. `npm run test:books` shows no difference either, until the next booking run books such a payment: from then on the open quarter's 383 and 405 grow by it.
- The Wissen area shows a brain in the dock, no longer a light bulb (Lucide «brain», ISC licence).
- A workflow graph wider than its frame (Automatisierung › a workflow › Graph, and a run's page) no longer looks cut off on the right. It always scrolled inside the frame, but macOS hides the scrollbar, so the cards and arrows past the edge simply stopped. The frame now shows its scrollbar at all times and fades the side that still hides steps, at any window width and with the AI sidebar open (issue #12).
- Reading the bank statements again overwrote one leg of a Wise card charge drawn from two currencies (one bank reference, a CHF and a EUR leg) with a copy of the other. In a filed quarter the write door refused the change («Periode geschlossen … transactions CARD-… changed»), so every intake run stopped at «read». A re-read now matches each transaction to its previous self by reference and currency: both legs stay as they were, and a leg no statement file holds any more is kept and reported like any other filed payment (#23).
- Buttons such as «Neue Regel» or «Löschen» looked like plain text: a plain button now has a frame and a background on any surface, the quiet one a light frame, the main one a fill, and none takes the italics of the text it sits in; one disabled look for all. The tip that explains the folding bars keeps its own look, now on hover too.
- A narrow detail (the AI sidebar open) stacks the labels of a key-value list above their values instead of squeezing both into one row.
- Resetting a demo (`npm run demo:reset`) also removes the copy's record of what was switched on: kept, a fresh copy was compared against the old copy's record and its new partner notes showed as «Geändert und noch nicht eingeschaltet» in Firma › Regeln.
- `npm start` on a bundled demo (`VANILLA_WORKSPACE=demo`) wrote into the demo in the app's repository: a mailbox connected there fetched real receipts into the public demo. It now works on a copy in `~/.vanilla-accounting/demo/<name>/`, as the Mac app always did; `npm run demo:reset` starts the copy afresh. Test runs keep copies of their own.
- The hourly collection check that finds no source due is no longer kept as a run: «Letzter Lauf» above Belege no longer shows «heute 20:00» with no source connected, and such checks kept earlier are dropped when the app starts.
- «Regel anpassen?» shows on a rule booking a person corrected: the journal detail showed it only for bookings that could not be edited, so it never appeared in the open period.

- A filing run of a period that is filed with the ESTV while the books drifted cannot be discarded: the hold lifts only by sealing, and «Verwerfen» says to restore the books first, then «Weiter» (golden rule 1).
- A collective payment partly decided by payer rules is booked to the end when the model is ready: it books the parts no rule decided, the rule parts stay the rules'. Without a model the rest stays for the person.
- The MWST page of a filing period notes when the rate rows, each rounded, add up a Rappen off the rounded total: the return rounds the total once. No figure changes.
- A booking changed by hand opens again: the journal detail passed the payment to the «Regel anpassen?» card as `ref`, a name React keeps for itself, and every fresh load of such a booking was a white page. The card takes `payment`, and a test refuses a component prop named `ref`.
- The year close follows the legal form. An Einzelfirma's Abschluss said step 5 writes the result to Gewinnvortrag (2970) and offered a step 6 «Gewinn verwenden» with a Generalversammlung, a dividend, 35 % Verrechnungssteuer and the accounts 2950, 2206 and 2451, none of which her chart has. How the result is closed and the profit used is now data per legal form in the module (`byLegalForm` of `modules/ch/year-end.yaml`): an Einzelfirma carries the result to her capital (2800 Eigenkapital), closes the private account (2850) into it, so it starts the year at zero, and has six steps without «Gewinn verwenden» (the API refuses a profit allocation for her); a GmbH or AG keeps 2970 and the step, whose rows are the accounts the chart has, a missing one named and offered with «Konto anlegen». Persona finding r10f-28.
- A payout report books its payouts in code again, split into gross sales and the provider's fees (regression of r8f-15: a monthly SumUp report linked to a month's five payouts came out as five net sales, so the turnover the registration threshold watches was short by the fees). The split of round 8 was the model's own reading per payment, and with one report on five payouts it read each as net. Now the model reads a payout report's figures once, at the match step (sales, refunds, fees, paid out, kept only when they add up), and the booking step books every payout the report is linked to from them without asking the model: each payout's sales are its amount, the last one carries the month's refunds and fees on top, so the month's sales add up to the report's. The accounts and codes come from a rule naming the provider («bei SumUp immer so»), else from the firm's setup answer «Auszahlungen eines Zahlungsanbieters: ja» (the revenue account of what it sells at its sale code, the fees on the account the answer added, without codes for a firm not registered); a report whose payouts do not add up to what it paid out is left to the model as before. Such a booking says «der Auszahlungsbericht» as its author. Persona finding r10f-29.
- Änderungen › «Rückgängig» on a rule booking a person corrected by hand before any save held it puts the rule's booking back (its account, the rule as its author) instead of deleting the booking; the row says «von Hand geändert», not «von Hand gebucht». Persona finding r10k-2.
- A correction of a filed sale teaches the payer rule: «Korrektur erfassen» to «kein Verkauf» (or another account) on a payment a payer rule booked now offers «Regel anpassen?» on the correction's page, as a correction in the open period does; the rule's change is the account the correction moves the money to, without a code. The offer also comes after the KI corrected a rule booking on the person's wish. Persona finding r10f-35.
- The KI no longer promises an «Übernehmen» that is not there: the prompt forbids naming it or any confirmation at all (the app says under the answer whether anything is to confirm), and where an answer made at once still names it, the app's line adds that there is nothing to take over and the KI's words about it do not hold. Persona findings r10f-11, r8f-47.
- The KI answers in the person's language from the first word, also while it reads the books («Ich schaue zuerst …»), never with an English sentence such as «Let me look at …». Persona finding r10f-36.
- «Zuordnen» of a receipt to a payment that is booked already, on a Mac without Claude Code, no longer takes the booking away: the app rebooks a payment from what is linked now, and without a model nobody could, so the payment stood «nicht gebucht» and the bank off its closing balance until a booking by hand. Now the booking stays and only the link is made; the toast says so and that the booking's VAT code is the person's to check (the receipt may show VAT the booking does not claim). The toast's «Rückgängig» («Lösen») takes the link back and leaves that booking, which saw no receipt. A booking that claimed a receipt's VAT is still booked again when the receipt is unlinked, with a model or as a task without one. With a model the payment is booked again as before. Persona finding r8f-41.
- «Rückgängig» of a deleted booking brings it back with its provenance: who booked it, the rule, the reason, the run and the receipts it saw. The toast's undo had sent only the booking's figures, so a rule's booking came back as a person's and Herkunft said «Claude». Änderungen › «Rückgängig» kept the provenance already. Persona finding r8f-46.
- MWST and the 0535 see a return filed in the app without a reload of the page. The filed returns were read once per year and kept for the whole session, so right after «Eingereicht» the page said «keine eingereichte Abrechnung» while the file lay in filings/. They are now read again with each load of the books, and the books are loaded again when any run comes to rest (the filing's seal included), not only the inbox's. Persona finding r8f-45.
- «Verwerfen» on a run's page asks first («Ja, verwerfen» or «Abbrechen») and says what it means: the run ends with no further step, what it wrote stays. A run discarded that way is marked «von einer Person verworfen» in the app's language, no longer «discarded by a person». Persona finding r8f-44; the seal's way back after a changed figure (the period freed, «Weiter» seals once the bookings are restored) came with r8h-26.
- The KI sidebar's words about what it did come from the app. When an answer's changes are made at once, the app closes the answer with its own line («Gemacht: 8 Änderungen, je mit «Rückgängig».»), and the model is told that whether a block is made at once or held for «Übernehmen» is the app's decision, said under the answer, so it no longer writes «es ändert sich nichts, bevor du «Übernehmen» drückst» about bookings it has just made. A wish about a payer's or supplier's payments in general («X ist doch eine Kundin») now holds for every payment of the open period: the model reads them, changes each booking the wish holds for, names them with date and amount, and says a payment is unbooked only after reading its bookings. Persona findings r8f-47, r8f-48.
- The hand form of a firm without input tax (Saldosteuersatz, or not registered) no longer prefills a receipt's VAT rate («8.1») that no code can come of, and a rate typed there is refused with the method's reason (no input tax; purchases without a code) instead of «Zu 8.1 passt auf diesen Konten kein MWST-Code». Persona finding r8f-50.
- «Zurücknehmen» is not offered on an answered sale question whose payment lies in a filed period: the answer stays as filed, and the button only ran into the period lock. Persona findings r7f-36, r8f-35.
- «Korrektur erfassen» on a filed sale says «Bank und Ertrag bleiben», no longer «Bank und Aufwand bleiben». Persona findings r6f-26, r8f-32.
- «Zahlung verknüpfen» in a firm without input tax (Saldosteuersatz, or not registered): a payment of a filed period reads «keine Zuordnung mehr, und ohne Vorsteuer nichts zu korrigieren», and ↵ on it no longer opens a correction that has nothing to correct. Persona findings r6f-34, r8f-34.
- «Stornieren» › «Gutschrift anlegen» after an earlier credit note proposes what is left to credit, not the whole invoice again, and the warning names the maximum. Persona finding r8c-14 (r9c-9).
- A reminder fee the customer did not pay stays named on the invoice: «Mahngebühr … offen» with its amount beside the open or paid line, until it comes or the reminder is withdrawn. Persona finding r8c-15 (r9c-10).
- «Lösen» of an invoice's payment undoes the whole link. On the invoice, each linked payment is listed with «Lösen»: the payment leaves the invoice (open again), the bookings the link made from the invoice go, and the booking that stood before the link is back (a person's booking stays, as the link had kept it); a payment left without a booking goes to the booking run. The same happens for «Lösen» on the invoice PDF under Belege, which before took only the receipt link and left the invoice «bezahlt». Änderungen lists a payment linked or released since the last save, each with its undo. Persona finding r10c-3.
- Two bank statements that share a day each list only the payments their own file holds and their own opening and closing balance: a statement now carries the refs of its payments (`refs`, filled when the folder is read, by the same rule that keeps a payment one transaction across overlapping exports), and of two statements ending the same day the one that started later is listed first and gives the account's balance. Before, both statements showed each other's payments, the same closing balance, a false «weichen ab» and a difference. Persona finding r10c-2.
- Statements whose balances do not chain (the statement before closed on one balance, the next opens on another) are named as a missing statement for the days between, on the later statement and in the list's counts, instead of «Salden stimmen» and a hint at a missing booking. Finding r10h-7 of the Malerei Huber round.
- «Rückgängig» after «Auszug entfernen» brings the payments back booked: the removal answers with the bookings and links it took, and the undo puts them back as they were (who booked them included) once the file is read again. Finding r10h-3 of the Malerei Huber round.
- A year-end step whose figure changed after it was booked no longer reads «erfasst» with the new figure. The Debitoren were booked, then an invoice was cancelled or paid: the step now says «gebucht …, heute …» with «Neu buchen», which replaces the booking by today's figure on the same accounts (undoable in the toast and under Änderungen), or removes it when nothing is open any more; the step stays visible for that. The checks and «Jahr abschliessen» block while the two figures differ, and `npm run year:close` names the step. Persona finding r10c-1.
- The MWST page's «Zu bezahlen» of an open quarter is the figure the return will carry (the tax on each rate's total, as the authority computes it and as the approval card and the XML say). When the Ziffern summed from the entries differ by a Rappen, the page says so under the figure instead of showing two amounts. Persona finding r9c-13 (r8c-23).
- A supplier's credit note without its own PDF whose payment text names an invoice already booked takes that invoice's code with the minus (-M81 for an M81 invoice, -I81 for I81) instead of -M0, and its reason names the invoice. A credit note that names nothing booked keeps the receipt rule. Owner's decision 16.10.2026 (r10h-33).
- `npm run vat-report` on a filing period prints the figures the MWST page and the XML carry (the tax of each rate row on its total, the payable rounded once), not the per-entry sums that could differ by a Rappen. Page and CLI share one function for it. Persona finding r10c-5.
- `npm run vat-report` on a quarter approved («Freigegeben») but not yet filed says «FREIGEGEBEN, noch nicht eingereicht» instead of «EINGEREICHT — set in stone». Persona finding r9c-17.
- No file paths in the owner's words: the filing task names the XML file instead of its temp folder, and Firma › Bankkonten explains the accounts without naming the manifest file. Persona finding r9c-18.
- Änderungen lists what else changed since the last save, each with «Rückgängig» or the reason there is none: an invoice draft (deleted again), an invoice issued or cancelled (it keeps its number, so no undo), a customer added (never deleted) or changed (back as saved), a statement read in (its payments belong to the books), a step of the year close marked or booked (marked back, which takes the step's booking with it), an account added (removed, unless bookings use it) or renamed (named back). A booking made on the year close's form is a hand booking there too. «Speichern» without the AI names the save by kind («Bücher: 2 Buchungen von Hand · 1 Rechnung ausgestellt · 1 Kontoauszug eingelesen») instead of «Bücher: von Hand geändert». Persona finding r9c-6 (r8c-25).
- Firma › Firma checks the company UID's check digit when you change it, like a customer's: a typo in the last digit is refused in words of the app's four languages, a valid UID in any spelling is stored in its canonical form, and the UID as stored passes unchanged. The demo companies keep their invented UIDs, which fail the check on purpose so that no demo ever names a real company (`tools/demo/generate.ts` refuses a valid one); their invoices print them as before. Persona finding r9c-5.
- A payment is linked to an open invoice from either side, also partly, before the invoice's date and without a reference. The invoice's page proposes every payment whose text names its number, a prepayment dated before the invoice and a payment already booked by hand included, and offers any other incoming payment of its currency under «Andere Zahlung zuordnen»; the payment's page has «Rechnung zuordnen» with the open invoices of its currency, the named one first. Linking a payment a person booked keeps her booking as the revenue and records the allocation, so the invoice's open amount drops; a payment the classifier booked is booked from the invoice as before. Abschluss › Debitoren refuses while a payment names an open invoice without being linked to it, and names the payments, so the year end never counts that revenue twice. Persona finding r9c-3.
- The hand form of a payment for a cancelled invoice no longer opens with the earlier sale's account and VAT code. The form took its rows before the invoices had arrived, so the note «kein Umsatz, keine MWST» appeared under a prefilled 3400 / V81 that ↵ would book; the form now waits for the invoices and opens with the revenue side empty. Persona finding r9c-4 (r8c-5).
- The year end books only on accounts of the chart. Abschluss › Abschlussbuchungen › Debitoren prefilled 1100 from the module even when the chart had no such account, booked on it, and «Schlussbilanz geht auf» stayed green while the Bilanz left the account out and no longer balanced. The Debitoren account is now the chart's (the first of the module's candidates it has); a chart without one leaves the Soll field empty, says which account is missing and offers «Konto 1100 anlegen». A year-end entry or a Kursdifferenz on an account the chart lacks is refused with the account named; a balance on such an account fails the check «Schlussbilanz geht auf», blocks the close and is named among the causes. Persona finding r9c-2.
- Abschluss › «zurück» on a booked step takes its booking back too. It cleared the mark and left the entry in Buchungen, where the row still read «erfasst · in Buchungen: M2026-028». The toast now names the deleted entry, and its Rückgängig books it back and marks the step as before. A step ticked by an entry booked in Buchungen keeps that entry (it shows «in Buchungen:» instead). Persona finding r9c-16.
- A correction of a filed entry in a foreign currency keeps the currency and the rate the entry was booked at. «Korrektur erfassen» on a Wise EUR purchase prefilled the EUR figure as francs, so the 0535 took back more input tax than the quarter had claimed; the form now shows the amount in the entry's currency with its CHF value and rate, and books the correction, the storno and the account move with them. The correction case names both figures. Persona finding r9c-1.
- Small things of the coiffeur round: a statement is listed with the days the bank names (camt `FrToDt`), not a day off its first booking, in the list and the drop dialog (r9k-10); «Beleg öffnen» in the drop dialog opens a receipt of a sealed quarter in that quarter (r9k-8); a booking a rule or a person made no longer says «von KI gebucht» on its run line (r9k-12); the hand-booking form follows its payment's prefill until you type, and ↵ with an account list open only picks, never books a half-typed line (r9k-7); the refusal of a statement reaching into a closed period says «abgeschlossen» for a firm that files no return and names the file without its folder (r9k-9); Firma › Bankkonten names no file, a rule from the demo's decisions reads «Entscheid vom …», and a firm without VAT codes sees no MWST row on a booking (r9k-13). Not done: the Änderungen rows still name the bank reference and the file's last write (r9k-11).
- Every correction teaches: when you correct a booking a rule made (another account, another code), the booking's page offers «Regel anpassen?», without the AI. One click drafts the rule's new `then` into its file and shows the rule's card: the rule as it was and as it would be, the payments of the year it would book differently, «Behalten» or «Verwerfen»; a kept rule says `source: correction <date>` and books the next payment the corrected way, and Änderungen takes it back like any rule. The rules and the classifier now read the firm's own chart (Firma › Kontenplan, accounts.json) when the books have one, so an account added there can be named in a rule; the prior-year export serves only books without a chart of their own. Persona finding r9k-5.
- A statement dropped on Kontoauszüge can be taken back like one dropped on Belege. Its toast now has «Rückgängig», the statement's page has «Auszug entfernen» (its toast reads the same file in again), and both use the drop dialog's convergent undo (`DELETE /api/statements/<bank>/<file>`): the file, its balances and the payments only it brought leave with their links and entries, what another export still holds stays, and a payment with a hand booking refuses the removal. After an import Kontoauszüge opens the new statement in its own period instead of jumping to the open quarter. Persona finding r9k-3.
- A bank account added by mistake can be taken back. The question «Gehört dieses Konto der Firma?» now names the statement's account holder («von «Coiffure Nachbar GmbH» bei Raiffeisenbank …»), so the owner sees it is another firm's account. Adding an account also adds its ledger account to the chart under the bank's label (an account the chart has is reused), so the year close no longer blocks on «which is not in accounts.json»; a bank account whose ledger account the chart still lacks is named in the person's language, with the place to add it. The toast after «Bankkonto hinzufügen» has «Rückgängig», and Firma › Bankkonten › Bearbeiten has «Entfernen» per account, with its own undo; both are refused, with what still uses the account (statements, bookings or payments, invoice payment accounts), while the books use it. A removed account takes its chart account along only when that account was made with it and nothing is booked on it. Persona finding r9k-1.
- One truth for «gebucht»: a payment is booked when its lines add up to what the bank paid. Undoing one line of a split in Änderungen left the payment half booked while Aufgaben said «Nichts wartet auf dich» and the bank list «Salden stimmen»; only the statement detail showed the gap. Now a payment whose lines fall short counts as not booked everywhere (the task comes back, the bank list marks it, the year close lists it), «Von Hand buchen» opens with the rest and books it on top of the lines that stand, no rule or model books over a person's partial lines, and a payment whose lines add up still refuses a second booking. Persona finding r9k-2.
- A SumUp or TWINT payout report finds its payout again. The rule from round 8 («money in is never the best fit for a supplier invoice») was applied to every receipt without a kind, so a payout report typed by hand lost its exact credit as «Passt am besten» and the dialog proposed a debit instead. A receipt whose words say payout («Auszahlung», «payout», «versement») or whose kind is a sales record now takes the credit; a supplier invoice keeps the rule. Persona finding r9k-4 (regression of r8k-5).
